Catalog number : M00066
Uniform title: ānandatantra
Main title: ānandatantram
Editor : Dyczkowski, Mark S. G.
Manuscript : IFP transcript T0333
Manuscript : British Library in London Ms: 2541
Description:
This e-text has been prepared from two manuscripts of unpublished text. Ms. K is IFP transcript no. 333 which was copied from Ms. 5562 of the Government Manuscript Library in Chennai. Ms. Kh is deposited in the British Library in London. It is Sanskrit MSs. 2541: IO ** ? 3 (or 8) 011. It is a transcript of a (possibly South Indian) MS that was commissioned by Colbrooke in the first half of the 19th century. Both Ms.s are fairly correct. Ms. K is more so than Ms Kh. There are a good number of manuscripts of this Kaula Tantra that affiliates itself to the nityāṣaṃśodaśikārṇava. They are virtually all found in South India. Interesting features of this Tantra are the extensive descriptions of yonipūjā and related rites.
Notes:
Data-entered by the staff of Muktabodha under the supervision of Mark Dyczkowski. Revision 0 October 16, 2007
Internet publisher : Muktabodha Indological Research Institute
Publication year : 2007
Publication country : United States

prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ


śrīgurubhyo namaḥ [kh: nāsti] 

śrīmahāgaṇapataye namaḥ [kh: nāsti]

ānaṃdakaṃdabhūtāya jagadānaṃdahetave |
namassatatabhogāya mahāliṅgāya śaṃbhave || 1\1 ||
merupṛṣṭhe sukhāsīnaṃ mṛgāṅkakṛtaśekharam |
kāmeśvaraṃ mahādevaṃ [kh: -deva] śrīdevī paripṛcchati || 1\2 ||

śrīdevyuvāca

devadevamahādeva jagadānandadāyaka |
karuṇāmṛtavārāśe [kh: -hye] niṣkalaṃka niraṃjana || 1\3 ||
asvataṃtrajagaccakrasatatādhāramūrtaye |
svataṃtrāya namastubhyaṃ yat kiṃcit pṛcchate mayā || 1\4 ||
mayyanugrahabuddhiścet kathayasva mama prabho |
tvamevasaṃśayacchettā sarvaśāstreṣu kīrtitaḥ || 1\5 ||
sarvatradarśane caiva sarveṣāṃ cāpi vartmani |
tvameva pūjyase śaṃbho nāparaḥ ko'pi puruṣaḥ || 1\6 ||
sarvatrāpi samāneṣu tavāṅge maheśvara |
adhikāt pūjanaṃ kena tava liṅgasya kīrtyate || 1\7 ||
na mūrdhani na vā pāde nāṃsayornāpi vakṣasi |
arcitastvaṃ prasanno'si mayā liṅgamaheśvaraḥ [kh: -ra] || 1\8 ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ deva ccettumarhasya śeṣataḥ |

kāmeśvara uvāca

śṛṇuṣva devi suśroṇī [kh: -ṇi] taddhetuṃ kathayāmi te || 1\9 ||
yaḥ para brahmarūpī san niṣkalaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
3k) sarvadevaśarīrāntarvarti jīvapadāspadaḥ || 1\10 ||
sarvatra vartamāno'pi sarvāspaṣṭa nijākṛtiḥ |
ānandarūpī yaḥ proktaḥ tatsthānaṃ liṅgamucyate || 1\11 ||
ya ānanda raso jñeyo raso reta iti smṛtam |
raso vai sarasahyeva badhvā [kh: labdhā] nandī [kh: nadī] bhavediti || 1\12 ||
vedāditaḥ pramāṇena yad reto [kh: re?to] rasatāṃ gatam |
tasyānandasvarūpasya rasasya sa mahīyasaḥ || 1\13 ||
ekāyanamidaṃ liṅgaṃ tasmādānandarūpadhṛk |
ānandakandamānandapīṭhamānandamandiram || 1\14 ||
jagatsṛṣṭi jagaddhātṛ prāṇada prāṇarūpakam |
saṃsāravara ityetā mahāliṅgasya bhūtayaḥ || 1\15 ||
liṅgaḥ paraśivo jñeyo bhagasthānaṃ [kh: -na] maheśvarī |
tatsaṃsṛṣṭā [kh: -sṛṣṭau] jagatsṛṣṭirnānyathā kalpakoṭibhiḥ || 1\16 ||
devānāṃ ca narāṇāṃ ca paśūnāṃ pakṣiṇāmapi |
kinnarāṇāṃ ca siddhānāṃ sādhyānāmapi [kh: sā * *?nāmapi] kheṭatām [kh: 
-tā] || 1\17 ||
anyeṣāmapi jantūnāṃ sarveṣāmapi [kh: sarve *?mapi] sarvaśaḥ |
ahaṃ liṃgakharūpī sattānandādvaitadāyakaḥ [k: sannā-; kh: sannananadā-] || 
1\18 ||
tadarthameva [kh: tadarttha-] sarveṣāṃ pravṛttiḥ parame�svarī [kh: parameḥ * *?] |
madarcanāprasaṃgena sarveṣāṃ [kh: -ṣā] harṣasaṃpadaḥ || 1\19 ||
padametanmahānanda pūrteḥ pūjyasya vastunaḥ |
yalliṅgākāratāṃ prāptamātmajyotivivarjitam [kh: -ta] || 1\20 ||
4k) tasmādānandanāmāyaṃ liṅgarūpī maheśvaraḥ |
mahatānandatantreṇa mayābhūt prārthitaḥ purā || 1\21 ||
tannimittamidaṃ tantraṃ loke vistaramāgatam |
sarvāṇyapi ca tantrāṇi tantrasyevāsya bhūtayaḥ || 1\22 ||
etat [kh: etaḥ] tantraṃ sugoptavyamānando yatra ghuṣyate |
sarvāṇyapi ca śāstrāṇi prakaṭānyaphalāni ca || 1\23 ||
idaṃ tu guptamānandataṃtraṃ saphalamucyate |
taṃtre'smin śrūyamāṇe'pi mahānānandasaṃplavaḥ || 1\24 ||
sukharūpe mahāliṅge līyate [kh: liyyate] tasya mānasam |
layanālliṅgamityāhuḥ sarvasyāpīha vastunaḥ || 1\25 ||
yadi liṅge mano līnaṃ kaivalyaṃ tasya niścitam |
tadetat taṃtramāhātmyaṃ [kh: -tmya] pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ || 1\26 ||
taṃtrametaddhārayatāṃ paṭhatāṃ śṛṇvatāmapi [kh: śruṇva-] |
abhyasyatāmanukalamarthyāṃścaiva [kh: -martthāṃścaiva] viviñcatām || 1\27 ||
tadānutiṣṭhatāṃ karma tatra proktaṃ tu yādṛśam |
teṣāṃ madīyabhaktānāmānaṃdādvaitamudbhavet [kh: -madiyyabhaktā-] || 1\28 
||
idaṃ taṃtraṃ purā proktaṃ keśavāya mahātmane |
yadūnāṃ pāvane vaṃśe samāvirbhūya [kh: samāvirtyaya?] tiṣṭhatā || 1\29 ||
ṣoḍaśastrīsahasreṣu kṛṣṇenānuṣṭhitaṃ tvidam [kh: ?]  |
sadrūpamatha cidrūpaṃ tathānaṃdasvarūpakam || 1\30 ||
5k) tatsarvamavagamyeta taṃtrasyāsya vilokanāt |
pravṛttiṃ ca nivṛttiṃ ca lokānāmapi sarvaśaḥ || 1\31 ||
jñāyate paramātattvaṃ taṃtrasyāsya vilokanāt |
na yasya kaulikī dīkṣā na yenābhyarcito guruḥ || 1\32 ||
na yasya mayi sadbhaktirna yena racitaṃ tataḥ |
na yasya tattvajijñāsā na tvaṃ yasya prasīdati [kh: -ni] || 1\33 ||
tasya durlabhamevedaṃ taṃtramānaṃdalakṣaṇam |
etat tatra sakṛcchrutvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate || 1\34 ||
vipattisaṃkaṭe jāte coravyāghrādije bhaye |
sarveṣvapi ca taṃtre'smin saṃsṛte sukhamudbhavet || 1\35 ||
anapatyaḥ sutān viṃdet putrīśca śubhalakṣaṇāḥ |
daridrastu mahadvittaṃ vyādhitastu nirādhitām || 1\36 ||
etat taṃtraprapāṭhena nirguṇo guṇavān bhavet |
atraiva paramaṃ tatva maṃtrataṃtre mahatvaram || 1\37 ||
mattapralapitaṃ sarvamanyacchāstraviḍabanam |
unmattalapitaṃ cānyadaraṇye ruditaṃ smṛtam || 1\38 ||
ūṣare bījavapanaṃ ajñānātmani kevale |
atastaṃtramidaṃ [kh: -mida] vīro gurubhaktiparāyaṇaḥ || 1\39 ||
nirṇītāgamatattvārttho [kh: nirnītā-] gṛhṇīyādātma [kh: gṛhṇi * * *?] vittaye 
[kh: ?] |
etadabhyarhitaṃ taṃtraṃ śāstreṇānyena yo jaḍaḥ || 1\40 ||
6k) tulayeta vimūḍātmā [kh: -tma] saha vedāpadāṃ [kh: verā-] padam [kh: pada] 
|
yaḥ prabuddhastaṃtramidaṃ dṛṣṭvopekṣāṃ [kh: -kṣā] kariṣyati || 1\41 ||
saptajanmani mūrkhaḥ syānniḥsaṃtānaśca [kh: mūrkhasya niḥsaṃtānaśca] 
jāyate |
yastvetanniṃdati jaḍastaṃtramānaṃdalakṣaṇam || 1\42 ||
sahasrajanmaparyaṃtaṃ jāyate sa napuṃsakaḥ [kh: -ka] |
ānaṃdadevamīśānaṃ taṃtrasyāsya mahīyasaḥ [kh: mahiyyasaḥ] || 1\43 ||
yastat yajati saṃprāptaṃ sa rauravagṛhī bhavet [kh: bhavat] |
atastaṃtramidaṃ prāptaṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ [kh: rakṣaṇi *?] prayatnataḥ || 1\44 ||
abhyarcanīyaṃ puṣpādyairdhāraṇīyaṃ [kh: -dyairddhāraṇiyyaṃ] nije gṛhe |
vīkṣyaṃ sarvaprayatnena samanuṣṭheyamādarāt || 1\45 ||
abhyasyaṃ bahuśaḥ svārtheṣvetat [kh: bahuśasvārtthe-] taṃtraṃ mahodayam |
ānaṃdataṃtramāhātmyametat te kathitaṃ priye || 1\46 ||
atra vijñānaliṃgasya tattvaṃ te kathayāmyaham |

iti dvātriṃśatkoṭivistīrṇe nityāṣoḍaśikārṇave taṃtre

bhūya eva mahādevī śruṇu vakṣyāmi suvrate |
ānaṃdadevatāsthānamasmin liṃge maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 2/1 ||
taddvārā [kh: tadvārā] śivabhaktasya kṣetrasyāpyākṛtiṃ [kh: -kṛti?] śruṇu |
yadā svaśaktipātena prabuddhaḥ sa vimarśavān || 2/2 ||
guroḥ śivasvarūpasya pratipattiṃ [kh: pratipatiṃ?] vrajennaraḥ |
tadā sa deśikaḥ śaivyā dṛṣṭyā tasya navātmanaḥ || 2/3 ||
bhaktasyātiprasannasya paśupāśaṃ vinirdahet [kh: vinirhnahet] |
anyadeva punarbhūyāt kaulikasya mahātmanaḥ || 2/4 ||
ghṛṇāśaṃkābhayaṃ lajjā jugupsā ceti paṃcamī |
kulaṃ jātiśca śīlaṃ cāpyaṣṭau pāśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 2/5 ||
ete pāśāḥ [kh: ṣāśāḥ] pradagdhāścet [kh: -śced] guruṇā pāpahāriṇā |
tasya bhaktasya sakalaṃ vapuḥ sukṣetramucyate || 2/6 ||
śāktī māṃtrī śāṃbhavī ca dīkṣāḥ tisraḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
śāktiḥ śaktyunmīlanena māṃtrī maṃtropadeśataḥ || 2/7 ||
śāṃbhavī tu śiromadhye raktaśuklapade smṛte |
etābhiḥ tisṛbhiścaiva dīkṣābhirdīkṣito naraḥ || 2/8 ||
vīkṣitaḥ samyagālokairguroḥ kāruṇyameduraiḥ |
bodhaliṃgasvarūpo'sau jāyate vāsanākṣayāt || 2/9 ||
krodho lobhastathā mohaḥ kāmo'sūyāmadastathā |
stridviṣṭaṃ lokavidviṣṭamālasyaṃ [kh: lokadidviṣṭa-] caiva cāpalam || 2/10 ||
bhrāṃtadṛṣṭistathāmānaḥ [k: bhāṃta-] parasvadhyānameva ca |
paradārāvamarśaṃ ca paradrohasya ciṃtanam || 2/11 ||
parasya niṃdā durvākyaṃ kalaho durviceṣṭitam |
atiniṣṭuraśīlatvamatidainyamatigrahaḥ || 2/12 ||
8k) asādhyarogagrastatvamatidāridryameva ca [kh: ?] |

dahyaṃte gurumāhātmyād viśvāsasya balena ca |

māyā cāṃtaṃ maheśasya śaktayaḥ sarvarodhikāḥ |
etābhireva tīvrābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ kañcukāṭamabhiḥ [kh: ?] || 2/15 ||
anvītamūrtirīśāno jīvatvaṃ pratipadyate |

||

10k) evaṃguṇaiḥ prakaṭitāṃ prāpuṣaṃ paramātmanaḥ |

bhruvoḥ |
sarvāṇyapi ca tīrthāni mātṛkāsthānagāṇi [kh: gāni] tu || 2/40 ||
pauṣkaraṃ naimiśāraṇyaṃ kedāraṃ harimedinī |

11k) kāpū ojādināmāni divyapīṭhāniśāṃkarī |
bhaktasya mātṛkā sthānānya malānya viśerate || 2/43 ||
evaṃ sarvāṃśabhūtasya mama bhaktasya dehinaḥ |
astipaṃcadaśāloka ānaṃdamaya vigrahaḥ || 2/44 ||
lokātīto nirmalaśca duḥkhera [kh: duḥkhaira] vyatiṣaṅgavān [kh: vyatiṣagavān] |
sadāśṛṃgāra saṃpannaḥ sadā bhoga parāyaṇaḥ || 2/45 ||

yo niṃdati śivasthānaṃ sahavedā padāṃ padam || 2/52 ||
12k) tasmānmadiya [kh: -diyya] bhaktastu na niṃden [kh: niṃde] nijakaṃ vapuḥ |
ātmānaṃ satataṃ rakṣedātma [kh: -ḍhātma] sarva ---- janam || 2/53 ||

ānaṃdanāyakaḥ prokto devīmānaṃda [kh: devī * naṃda] bhairavīm [kh: -vīm?] 
|| 2/60 ||

ityānaṃdataṃtre dvitīyaḥ [kh: dvitiyya] paṭalaḥ || 2/2 ||



tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ

atha vakṣyāmi deveśi sarvasyāgamavartmanaḥ |
ānaṃdanātha pūjyayāmeva paryavasāyitām || 3/1 ||
darśanāni bahūnyeva mayā sṛṣṭāni bhairavī |
śāstrāṇi cātisaṃkhyāni saṃkhyāvatse [kh: -vatye] vitāni ca || 3/2 ||
13k) darśanānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ śāstrāṇāmapi sarvaśaḥ |
dvāradvārikayā vedamārgasyaivopakārikā [kh: -syevopakāritā] || 3/3 ||
śabdānuśāsanaṃ śāstraṃ sādhvasādhuvivecanam |
vedanirvacanārthāya niruktākhyā gamiṣyati || 3/4 ||
chaṃdoviditataṃtraṃ tu vedachaṃdonuśāsanam |
vedasthānāṃ svarāṇāṃ tu śikṣāśāstravivecanam || 3/5 ||
vedoktakarmajālasya grathanaṃ [kh: gra?dhanaṃ] kalpasūtrataḥ |
jyotiṣāmayanaṃ darśa paurṇamāsādibodhakam || 3/6 ||
evaṃ ṣaḍaṃga sahito vedakarma pravartakaḥ |
anyānyapi ca bāhyāni vedāṃgāni bhavaṃti vai || 3/7 ||
mīmāṃsā śruti niṣṭhānāṃ vākyānāṃ [kh: vādyānāṃ] pravivecikām [kh: 
-vecakām] |
nyāyena hetu ciṃtā syāt purāṇaistatvanirṇayaḥ [kh: -nirnayaḥ] || 3/8 ||
dharmaśāstraṃ vaidikānāṃ sadācāra pravartakaḥ |
etairapi ca bāhyāṃgairveda mārgaḥ pariṣkṛtaḥ || 3/9 ||
caturvidho bhaved devī sa eva prakṛtervaśāt |
sāma-ṛgyajuṣātharva [kh: -dharva] bhedātairyajñakalpanam || 3/10 ||
vedopadiśamānānāṃ nānāvasthābhidāyuṣām |
agniṣṭomādi yajñānāṃ phalaṃ svargo maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 3/11 ||
svargaśca sukharūpaḥ [kh: -pa] syāt sukhaṃ cā naṃdalakṣaṇam |
svargastu dvividhaḥ prokto deśo vā sukhameva vā || 3/12 ||
14k) prathamaṃ tu [kh: du] bhaved devī sukhasādhanamucyate |
sukhasya sādhanaṃ svargaḥ [kh: sarga] deśastasya tu sādhanam || 3/13 ||
yajñakarmeti pūrvokta pakṣasyāyaṃ kramaḥ [kh: krama] smṛtaḥ |
sukhamātraṃ yadi svargo na deśaniyamo'tra vai || 3/14 ||
yatrakvāpi sukhābhijñaḥ svargoyajña kṛteḥ phalam |
pūrva pakṣe [kh: vakṣe] tu yajñānāṃ phalasaṃpattirucyate || 3/15 ||
jyotiṣṭo [kh: -ṣṭho] mādike yāge [kh: yoge] kṛte sati samāpite [kh: samīpite] |
apūrvaṃ [kh: -rva] jāyate [kh: jñāyate] tasmāt taccātmasamavāyavat || 3/16 ||
arthāpatti pramāṇena tenā pūrveṇa sidhyatā |
ātmaikavartinā bhogaparyaṃtamapi naśyatā || 3/17 ||
ākṛṣyamāṇaḥ sahasā dehāṃte svargamaśnute |
kutrameruta doṣāṃte [kh: dopāṃte] svargasthān pratikīrtyate || 3/18 ||
dvātṛṃśat koṭayo [kh: koṭi yo] devā devyaḥ ṣaṭṣaṣṭhikoṭayaḥ [kh: -koṭiyaḥ] |
vasaṃti yatra niyataṃ sudhāpāna padākulāḥ [kh: madā-] || 3/19 ||
ānaṃda mātra niṣṇātāḥ [kh: -tā] nityatṛptā nirādhayaḥ |
yajamānastatra gatvā tatratyairdevatāgaṇaiḥ [kh: tatratyai-] || 3/20 ||
kalpitaṃ nijamāvāsamālokayati śobhanam |
ratna staṃbha samākīrṇaṃ muktājālasamākulam || 3/21 ||
bhogopakaraṇairyuktaṃ vitānādarśa bhūṣitam |
kālāgaru [kh: kālāgas] samudbhūta navadhū pālikomalam || 3/22 ||
15k) kalpavṛkṣatva cāṃ sārabhūtairbhoga pradāyakaiḥ |
madhurairmadhubhiḥ pūrṇaiḥ ratnakuṃbhaiśca śobhitam || 3/23 ||
sugaṃdhivastu puṣpasrakśayyā kalpanaśilpibhiḥ |
vidagdhaiśceṭikāvargaistataścetaśca saṃkulam || 3/24 ||
atyunmāda karākāra sā lokanala vādapi |
svarge divyagrahaṃ gatvā paśyatyekāṃ [kh: vaśyatye-] sulocanām || 3/25 ||
salajjaiḥ sasmitairbhāvagarbhairmanmatha nirbharaiḥ |
sā kūtasyūta saṃcārairapāṃgairati śobhanām || 3/26 ||
tayā sahamudāyuktaḥ taralomāra vibhramaiḥ |
kramādaṃgāṃga saṃsarga sukhaṃ sadyogyamaśnute || 3/27 ||
deśasya sauṣṭhavād [kh: sauṣṭa-] deha sauṣṭhavāt [kh: sauṣṭa-] kālasauṣṭhavāt 
[kh: -sauṣṭavāt] |
anyādṛśamivābhāti laiṃgaṃsaukhyaṃ tadevahi || 3/28 ||
mahadaṃtaramālokya naragīrvāṇa saukhyayoḥ |
camatkaroti yajvāsau nijakaṃ karmasādhanam || 3/29 ||
liṃgamūlasthitaṃ devamānaṃdodaya lakṣaṇam |
abhyarcayati gīrvāṇī bhagapuṣpasamarpaṇaiḥ [kh: -ṇai] || 3/30 ||
tasmādānaṃda devasyārārāddhireva [kh: rā * ddhareva] phalaṃ param |
samastaveda saṃproktā [kh: -prokta] yajñakarmābhyanuṣṭhitaiḥ || 3/31 ||
ataḥ samastaśāstrāṇāṃ mūrddhanyasya mahīyasaḥ [kh: mahiyyasaḥ] |
vedasyāpi ca tātparyaṃ mahāliṃgārcanakramaḥ [kh: māhā-] || 3/32 ||
16k) anyeṣāṃ darśanānāṃ ca phalametadudāhṛtam |
āyurvedasya sārastu vājīkaraṇamucyate || 3/33 ||
bājīkaraṇakannāma retodhātuvivarddhanam |
varthamānasyarogasya nāśadvārāṃgapuṣṭitaḥ || 3/34 ||
rasamutthāpayed āyurvedamārgasya darśanāt |
dharmecārthe ca kāme ca mokṣe ca girinaṃdinī || 3/35 ||
sarvatrāpya [kh: -trāsya] samarthaḥ [kh: samartha] syādrogayukta śarīravān |
rogeṇa prathamaṃ naśyedrava evātmalakṣaṇaḥ || 3/36 ||
anyeṣāṃ tu tato'ṃgānāṃ krameṇahāsa ucyate |
ataḥ prathamato vairi rasasyeva mahīyasaḥ [kh: mahiyyasaḥ] || 3/37 ||
rasenaṣṭe kutaścātmā kutaścānaṃda bhairavaḥ |
kuto liṃgamaheśasya pūjanāt puṇyasaṃbhavaḥ || 3/38 ||
tasmādrasasya saṃrakṣāṃ kuryādāyuśruti kramaiḥ |
śuklasya poṣakairvastu jātairātmānamarcayet || 3/39 ||
ataścānaṃdadevasya pūjārthā vaidyasaṃhitā |
dhanurvedasya cāpyetat prayojanamudāhṛtam || 3/40 ||
abhyastacāpanigamo yoddhā bhavati bhūpatiḥ |
yuddhe cenmṛtimāpnoti yajvavatphalamaśnute || 3/41 ||
vijayeta yadi prauḍhānathavālīn [kh: -rīn] mahīpatiḥ |
tadā rājyaṃ samārādya [kh: -sādya] laiṃgamānaṃdamaśnute [kh: -
māṃnaṃda-] || 3/42 ||
17k) tasmāddhanuḥ [kh: -nu] śrutirapi svalaiṃgānaṃdasādhikā |
gāṃdharvastu mahānvedo bharatādyaiḥ pravistṛtaḥ || 3/43 ||
maduktaistāṃḍavollāsaistvaduktairlāsya vibhramaiḥ |
yastūbhayaprakāreṇa saṃtoṣayati sajjanāt || 3/44 ||
nṛttaṃ gītaṃ ca vādyaṃ ca trayaṃ saṃgītamucyate |
prāyaḥ saṃgītasārāṇi nādakāni pracakṣate || 3/45 ||
teṣāṃ navarasādyānāṃ [kh: -ḍhyānāṃ] rūpakāṇāṃ sahasraśaḥ |
bāhulyāt pratipādya syāt śṛṃgārorasa ujvalaḥ || 3/46 ||
vīrādbhutādiṣu rasaprasiddhiḥ [kh: rasaprasiddhir] bhramataḥ kṛtā [kh: kṛtām] |
tenoparaṃjitatvādvā teṣāṃ rasa iti smṛtiḥ || 3/47 ||
prādhānyena rasastasmāt śṛṃgāraḥ kāmajīvitaḥ |
sacānukūladā [kh: -daṃ] catyordarśanasparśanātmakaḥ [kh: patyor-] || 3/48 ||
sakalānaṃdanādasya sannikṛṣṭataraḥ sakhā |
sātmāpātena tannādya [kh: tannāṭya] śāstramānaṃda hetukam [kh: * * *?] || 
3/49 ||
athārthaśāstramapi ca svaliṃgānaṃdahetave |
taccadvidhā nītiśāstraṃ bārhaspatyaṃ ca bhārgavam || 3/50 ||
prathamādeva nītisyād dvitīyānītirāsurī |
ṣaḍbhirguṇaiḥ śaktibhiścatisṛbhiścāpi siddhibhiḥ || 3/51 ||
udayāśca tribhiḥ sekairupāyaistribhirujvalam |
nītiśāstramidaṃ sarvaṃ [kh: sarva] vittasaṃpatsamṛddhidam || 3/52 ||
18k) arthaśāstreṇa saṃsiddhaiḥ [kh: saṃsiddhair] arthaiḥ satyamupārjate |
tṛtīyapuruṣārtho'yaṃ svacānaṃda svarūpakaḥ || 3/53 ||
tasmānnītirddhatiścāpi [kh: -tirddṛtiścāpi] bhogānaṃdapravartikā |
ityaṣṭādaśavidyānāṃ tātparyaṃ samyagīritam || 3/54 ||
darśanānāṃ ca sarveṣāmānaṃda paratā priye |
sarvatra śāstremokṣo'sti tasyarūpaṃ pṛthak pṛthak || 3/55 ||
sukhātmatā tu sarvatra samānāmokṣavartmanaḥ |
nocet pravṛttiḥ kenasyāt tattatsiddhāṃta vartinām || 3/56 ||
sukhaduḥkhasamucchedātmako mokṣaḥ paraiḥ [kh: parai] smṛtaḥ |
anānaṃda svarūpatvāt śilāvasthānviḍaṃbhayan [kh: -bayan] || 3/57 ||
satumārgo gautamena bhrāṃtadṛṣṭyāprakalpitaḥ |
tad doṣānnijayoṣāpi dṛṣadbhāvaṃ kilāgamat || 3/58 ||
ato vedāṃtamārgokta ānaṃdātmamaheśvaraḥ |
sa eva mokṣonānyo'sti duḥkhākāravivarjitaḥ || 3/59 ||
tasmād vāgjālamakhilaṃ vedaśāstrāgamātmakam |
ānaṃdanātha liṃgasthamadhikṛtyapravartate || 3/60 ||

ityānaṃdataṃtre tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ || 3/3 ||




caturtha paṭalaḥ
śrīdevyuvāca

bhūya eva mahādeva saṃśayo mama vartate |
liṃgānaṃdamayo yo'yamīśvaraḥ pratipāditaḥ || 4/1 ||
19k) taṃ kecit kena niṃdaṃti viraktaṃ manyamānasāḥ |
kecittān pūjayaṃ tyeva bhogamātra parāṅmukhān || 4/2 ||
teṣāṃ tu sadgatirnāsti kimasti brūhiśaṃkara [kh: - bṛhi] |
anyānyapi sukhāsaktānāti [kh: -nati] saṃdadhate hite || 4/3 ||
kiṃ tādṛśāste svābhāvyāt kiṃ te kapaṭa vṛttayaḥ |
etanme saṃśayaṃ chiṃdhi [kh: chiṃdi] sarvasaṃśayanāśaka [kh: -nāyaka] || 4/4 
||


kāmeśvaraḥ


śruṇu devi mahābhāge yatpṛṣṭhaṃ tadvivicyate [kh: -dicyate] |
mūrkhapravṛttayaḥ kecit hitāhitaparāṅmukhāḥ || 4/5 ||
anyathā [kh: anyadhā] śāstratātparyaṃ kalpayaṃtaḥ [kh: -yaṃta] svabuddhibhiḥ 
|
vināśameva gacchaṃti niṃdatolaiṃgamīśvaram || 4/6 ||
yo niṃdati durācārastasyarūpaṃ tu kīdṛśam |
nāśmanānirmitaṃ devī nāyasāna ca dārubhiḥ || 4/7 ||
notṛṇairna ca vāmṛdbhiḥ saptadhātu mayaṃ hi tat |
dhātūnāṃ ca svarūpaṃ tatpariṇāmo hi retasaḥ || 4/8 ||
vinānaṃdamayaṃ śuklamupadānaṃ tu no kutaḥ |
ena gātreṇa niṃdaṃti liṃgānaṃdamaheśvaram || 4/9 ||
tadgātraṃ tanmayaṃ haṃta na niṃdaṃti durāśayāḥ |
nabhaḥsthalāt [kh: nabhastha-] patatino no tiṣṭhati rasātalāt || 4/10 ||
āyāti digbhyo no dehi paramānaṃdasaṃbhavaḥ |
tasmādānaṃdarūpasya mahāliṃgasya niṃdakāḥ || 4/11 ||
20k) pataṃti narakeghore yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam |
ānaṃdājjāyate hyādā vā nandenavivaddhate [kh: nadenavivarddhate] || 4/12 ||
ānaṃdena nijaṃ dehaṃ saṃdhārayati pūruṣaḥ |
ānaṃdanāthalābhena duḥkhepyaṃgaṃ na muṃcati || 4/13 ||
sarvāṃ pravṛttiṃ taddhetuṃ karotisukhalolupaḥ |
kauṭilyena tirodhatte pravṛttiṃ liṃgatatparām || 4/14 ||
itthaṃ vidannapinaroniṃdannirayamaśnute |
raktaśuklapadadvaṃdvaṃ [kh: -pata-] dvādaśāṃtasaroruhe || 4/15 ||
pūjyate munibhiḥ sarvaiḥ śrīguroḥ pādukātmanā |
tasya svarūpaṃ deveśi nojānaṃti durāśayāḥ || 4/16 ||
raktaṃ śoṇitamityāhuḥ śuklaṃreta iti smṛtam |
tayoḥ samarasī bhāvaḥ paramānaṃdalakṣaṇam || 4/17 ||
paramānaṃdasaṃbhūta paramānaṃdadāyakaḥ |
sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ [kh: -nā] janma tata eva pravartate || 4/18 ||
paśupakṣimṛgādyā�sca tata eva sumudgatāḥ |
taṃ vinā prāṇimātrasya [kh: -traśca] janmakvāpi na vidyate || 4/19 ||
raktaśuklapadārthastu bhūyaḥ samyagvivicyate |
raktaśukle pade sthāne svānaṃdasya maheśituḥ || 4/20 ||
ānaṃdanāthapīṭhiyaṃ [kh: -ṭhiyyaṃ] raktaśuklapadadvayī |
caraṇadvaṃdvarūpeṇa paryāṇaṃsinmanīṣiṇām [kh: paryaṇaṃ-] || 4/21 ||
21k) samastadevatānāṃ ca pūjyamagre padadvayam |
apūjayan rakta śukle pade devāṃśca pūjayan || 4/22 ||
pariśramaṃ paraṃ yāti bhasmahoteva [kh: -heteva] niṣphalam |
raktaśuklapadepūrvaṃ dviṣaṭkāṃtakṛtālaye || 4/23 ||
pūjanīyaprayatnena kimanyairdevapūjanaiḥ |
ataḥ [kh: atha] śrīgurunāthāṃghri dvaṃdvarūpeṇasaṃmite || 4/24 ||
raktaśuklapadeniṃdan rauravaṃ narakaṃ brajet |
asuṃdarairasaubhāgyai rabhāvādasyavastunaḥ || 4/25 ||
antardagdhāśayairduḥkhāṃ niṃdyate liṃgaśaṃkaraḥ |
atomaheśvarasyāsya liṃgānaṃdakṛtākṛteḥ || 4/26 ||
alābhaṃ satataṃ pūrvajanmanyapi kṛtaṃ hi tat |
duḥkhādduḥkhaṃ gatāstetu jaṅgamastaṃbhasannibhāḥ || 4/27 ||
sukhamātrānabhijñāśca bhaveyuḥ pāpinonarāḥ [kh: -gāḥ] |
ānaṃdanāthasamayādathadūraṃ bahiṣ.kṛtāḥ || 4/28 ||
mugdhāaḥ pāṣaṇḍinaścānye jaṭilāśca digaṃbarāḥ |
anyeluṃcitakeśāśca malināṅgāstathā pare || 4/29 ||
śmaśānavāsinaścānye parapiṇḍopajīvinaḥ |
ete vinaṣṭāmūrkhāśca vṛthāpaṇḍitamāninaḥ [kh: vṛdhā-] || 4/30 ||
ānaṃdanādanindanto [kh: -nātha-] narakāyaivakalpitāḥ |
teṣāṃdurātmanāṃ tattadācārānuguṇaṃ phalam || 4/31 ||
22k) kāraṇānuguṇaṃ kāryaṃ sarvatraivopavarṇyate |
na hi kodravabījebhyo protpadyaṃte yavāṃkurāḥ || 4/32 ||
śālibījādapi ca no koradūṣa samudbhavaḥ |
yathā kārastathāvṛttaṃ yathā cārastathā phalam || 4/33 ||
yathā śāstraṃ tathā mokṣo yathā bījaṃ tathāṃkuram |
pāpiṣṭhānāmihācāro yathā sarvajugupsitam || 4/34 ||
yathehānaṃda nāthasya pūjāmānaṃdamedurām |
karoti tadvatpretyāpi parānaṃdasukhaṃ bhavet || 4/35 ||
imaṃ vinā maheśānamānaṃdaika svarūpiṇam |
sukhasya saṃbhavonānya vartmanā padmalocane || 4/36 ||
bahūnāmāśrayo [kh: bahu-] yatra bahūnāmavalaṃbanam [kh: bahu-] |
sa eva mārgodumārgo [kh: mārgodurmārga] aparaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 4/37 ||
caturāśītilakṣāṇi yonibhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
teṣu sarvatra sāmyena liṃgānaṃdaḥ pravartate || 4/38 ||
na maugdhyaṃ [kh: mauḍyaṃ] no jadvilatā [kh: jaṭilatā] na vai rāgyaviḍaṃbanam 
|
na vai jātiṃ vināduṣṭāmanyajātiṣu vidyate || 4/39 ||
narajātiriyaṃ duṣṭā bahudoṣaparigrahā |
mithyābhimānadarpāndhāmithyādoṣāti śaṃkinī || 4/40 ||
bhrāṃtadṛṣṭirmahātṛṣṇā saṃkalpajvaravihvalā |
paśupakṣimṛgādyeṣu naitādṛkkrama saṃbhavaḥ || 4/41 ||
23k) na [kh: nāsti] bhaviṣyannānusaṃghatte nātītaṃ cintayatyapi |
na ravarjā sarvajātirvarta mānāvalaṃbinī [kh: -labinī] || 4/42 ||
na [kh: sa] puṇyapāpasaṃkṣobho na ca saṃsāravibhramaḥ |
na vācyavācakākāra śāstracarcā piśācikā || 4/43 ||
na duṣṭaibahubhirmārgairitarasya [kh: duṣṭair-] pratāraṇam |
na kauṭilyaṃ ca manaso na droho nābhimānatā || 4/44 ||
sasyaikasya kṛte sarva jagadvastu sahaivanaḥ |
nāhaṅkāro [kh: na ha ṅakāro] mṛgādyāsu [kh: -ḍhyāsu] priye jātiṣu dṛśyate || 
4/45 ||
kṣudhite bhukti cintāsyādriraṃsāyāṃ rati kriyā [kh: priyā] |
suṣupsāyāṃ paraṃ nidrā nānyaḥ ko'pi manojvaraḥ || 4/46 ||
anekacittavibhrānto manuṣyaḥ [kh: mānuṣyaḥ] śailanandinī |
nānāmārgakṛtārohaḥ kutrāpya dṛdalaṃbanaḥ [kh: dṛḍhalaṃ-] || 4/47 ||
saṃśayena vinirdagdha pratipadyetadurgame |
ataḥ sarvāsu jātiṣvapyakhaṃḍita parākramam || 4/48 ||
ānaṃdanāthamīśānaṃ na viṃdaṃtidurāśayāḥ || 4/49 ||

ityānaṃdatantre [k: -taṃte] caturthaḥ [kh: caturtha] paṭalaḥ || 4/4 ||




paṃcamaḥ paṭalaḥ

atha vakṣyāmi deveśi tasyānaṃda maheśituḥ |
maṃtramānaṃda devyāśca śruṇuvīraniṣevite || 5/1 ||
bahudhāmaṃtrarājo'yaṃ munibhiḥ saṃprakāśitaḥ |
samastasiddhijanakasāṃdrānaṃda mahodayaḥ || 5/2 ||
24k) navātmānaṃ samuddhṛtya paścādānaṃdamuddharet |
nāthāyeti padaṃ paścāt procyabrūyāṃ navātmikām || 5/3 ||
ānaṃda devīti padaṃ vallabhāya śikhi priyā |
praṇavādirmahāmaṃtraḥ prokto viṃśati [kh: viṃśatiṃ] varṇavān || 5/4 ||
iyamānaṃdavallīti vidyā proktā surāsuraiḥ |
navātmamithunākhyā ca mahānaṃdakalātmikā || 5/5 ||
eṣā vidyā samastānāṃ vidyānāmādimā smṛtā |
anayāsadṛśo [kh: -sadruśo] maṃtrastvanayāsadṛśaṃ tapaḥ || 5/6 ||
anayā sadṛśaṃ tatvaṃ na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati |
liṃgayoni sadāsaṃgā kāreṇasatataṃ mayā || 5/7 ||
viṣaiva [kh: viṣṇaiva] satataṃjapyā lokānugraha hetave |
māṃ vinānaivajānaṃti vidyāmetāṃ mahodayām || 5/8 ||
iyaṃ vidyā samastābhirvidyābhirupajīvyate |
upadiṣṭaṃ mayā pūrvameṣā sā [kh: nā] ca muradviṣe || 5/9 ||
tenānaṃdamayo jāto gopīsantatabhogavān |
pūtanāśakaṭādīnāṃ yamalārjunayorapi || 5/10 ||
dhenukasyāpyariṣṭasya keśinaścāpi mardane |
govarddhanasyoddharaṇe tathā kālīyamardane || 5/11 ||
yugapat [kh: yugavat] ṣoḍaśavadhū sahasrarati karmaṇi |
teneṃdrāyopadiṣṭeyaṃ vidyāsaubhāgyadāyinī || 5/12 ||
25k) etad vidyā prabhāvena śacīprāṇādhipo'bhavet |
evaṃ smareṣu sarvatra suradvārānareṣu ca || 5/13 ||
vistāraṃ paramāyātā vidyaiṣā [kh: vidveṣā] viśvaviśvamohine [kh: * 
*viśvamohinī] |
sarvasiddhikarī sarvapāpa jālanikṛṃtanī || 5/14 ||
sarvānaṃda svarūpā ca sarvapīṭhasvarūpiṇī |
sarvatatvamayī sarvavyādhiśaila mahāśaniḥ || 5/15 ||
saṃkaṭe rakṣaṇe puṃsāmeṣāvidyā mahodayā |
mārīcoravyāghrabhaye ghorakāṃtārasaṃkaṭe || 5/16 ||
agnitrāse mṛtyubhayedurbhikṣe [kh: -durbbhikṣe] rājyaviplave |
sakṛtkṛteyaṃ sadvidyā sarvaduḥkhaṃ vyapohati || 5/17 ||


śrīdevyuvāca 


kiṃ taṃ navātmakannāma kāvāsācanavātmikā |
kiṃ vyastaṃ kinnukūṭaṃ vā kathayasva mama prabho || 5/18 ||

kāmeśvaraḥ 

anādyaṃtaṃ paraṃ brahma sarvaviṣṭaṃbhakāraṇam |
śivasya śaktirapi ca sāmarasya kalātmakam || 5/19 ||
tasyarūpaṃ dvidhā proktaṃ arthaṃ śabdaṃ ca pārvatī |
prathamaṃ viśvarūpaṃ syād [kh: syā] dvitīyaṃ tu navātmakam || 5/20 ||
tadeva kacchate brahma vācaṃ cedaṃ mahāṃbike |
tasmān [kh: tasyān] navātmakasyāsya proddhāraṃ śruṇu pārvati [kh: -tī] || 5/21 
||
anaṃta bhṛgusaṃvartā bhāṃta lauvarayūmiti [kh: kāvarayā-] |
etannavātmakaṃ prāhuḥ kūṭaṃ bījaṃ mahattaram || 5/22 ||
26k) navātmikāyā bhedastu varṇadvayaviparyavān |
bhṛguśaṃkara saṃvartā bhāṃta * * * * * * || 5/23 ||
eṣāṃ navātmikā kūṭa bījākārā smṛtā budhaiḥ |
ānaṃdavalli vidyāyāḥ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ kathayāmite || 5/24 ||
ādyena hṛdayaṃ proktaṃ śiraḥ kāryaṃ navātmikā |
tatastu navabhirnetraṃ dvābhyāmastraṃ [kh: -masttraṃ] prakīrtitam || 5/25 ||
evaṃ ṣaḍaṃgaṃ kṛtvā tu varṇanyāsaṃ samācaret |
ādhāre svādhiṣṭhāne ca maṇipūrepyanāhate || 5/26 ||
ājñācakre mukhe nāsā raṃdhrayornetrayostathā |
karṇayośca bhruvormadhye [kh: bhṛvor-] phālanetre śirastaṭe || 5/27 ||
brahmaraṃdhre suṣumnāyāmiḍā piṃgalayorapi |
dvādaśāṃta mahāsthāne varṇanyāsaṃ samācaret || 5/28 ||
kūṭanyāsamathovakṣye brahmaraṃdhrelike [kh: -śike] tathā |
ājñācakre nāhate ca tathānābhi [kh: tadhā-] padepi [kh: -ti] ca || 5/29 ||
liṃgamūleliṃgamadhye liṃgāgramūlasīmanī |
navākṣarāṇāṃ kathito nyāsa eva maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 5/30 ||
navātmikākṣarāṇāṃ tu bhedoyaṃ girinaṃdinī |
yonimūle yonimadhye yonyagre cāpi bhairavī || 5/31 ||
anyat sarvaṃ samānaṃ [kh: samāna] syācchakterevatanaunyaset [kh: 
syātśakterevatasaunyaset] |
aya [kh: ayaṃ] nyāso yāgakāle pravṛtte parameśvari [kh: -ri] || 5/32 ||
27k) evaṃ nyāsena saṃśuddhaḥ kṛtebhūtaviśodhane |
ānaṃdanātha pūjāyāṃ yogyatvaṃ sādhako'śnute || 5/33 ||
eta nyāsena dīptāṃgaṃ jvalaṃtamivapāvakam |
navīkṣituṃ [kh: navīkkituṃ] śaknuvaṃtī bhayārtā vighnakoṭayaḥ || 5/34 ||
atha dhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sarvapāpanikṛṃtanam |
ena vijñānamātreṇa parānaṃda tanurbhavet || 5/35 ||
udyadādityasaṃkāśe śuklaśoṇitarūpiṇi [kh: -rupiṇī] |
uttaraṃge [kh: -rāṃge] mahāsiddhau phenajālasamāvṛte || 5/36 ||
navakoṭyā yojanānāṃ saṃmite viśvakāraṇe |
muhuḥśabdāyamāne ca nadīnadasamākule || 5/37 ||
madhyedvīpaṃ manohāri [kh: -rī] nānāvṛkṣasamākulam |
nānāpuṣpagaṇāmoda bhrāṃtapuṣpaṃdhayabrajam || 5/38 ||
muktāsikatilopāṃta nānākuḍya [kh: -kuḍyā] samākulam [kh: samā * *?]  |
viciṃtayen maheśāni tanmadhye hemamaṃdiram [kh: - maṃdi *?] || 5/39 ||
caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ caturāṃnāyasevitam |
caturvarga phalaprāpti kāraṇaṃśokavāraṇam || 5/40 ||
tanmadhyeśa maheśāni gṛharājaṃ viciṃtayet |
nānāmaṇi vicitrāṇāṃ staṃbhānāṃ navaśobhayā || 5/41 ||
bhāsamāna prabhāpūra dividīpitabhāskaram |
caṃdrikābhiḥ parivṛtaṃ bālātapavilohitam || 5/42 ||
28k) śṛṃgāradevatāhastā nirmukta kusumāṃjaliḥ [kh: -lī] |
sajjacāpenakāmena rakṣyamāṇamitastataḥ || 5/43 ||
ratidevi karāṃbhojalīlānartita cāmaram |
utkaṇṭhā devatādatta vālavyajanavījanam || 5/44 ||
maṃdānilaparispaṃda spaṃdamānavitānakam [kh: -ka] |
bhogopakaraṇairyuktaṃ vividhaiśca prapānakaiḥ [kh: * * * *? || 5/45 ||
karpūrāmodibhirmālyaiḥ kastūrībhiśca saṃyutam [kh: sa-] |
narmālāpakasābhijñaiśceṭi vargairmanoharam || 5/46 ||
kālāgarurasodbhūta [kh: -soddhūta] dhūpadhūsaritāṃbaram |
nidhidevatayāyuktaṃ paṭṭāṃśukapariṣkṛtam || 5/47 ||
gṛharājamiti dhyātvā tanmadhye vedikāṃ smaret |
nānāratnasamākīrṇaṃ catustaṃbhopaśobhitām || 5/48 ||
indrakopavitānāḍhyāṃ muktāprālaṃbasaṃyutām |
tatrastaṃbhadvayāsakta tiryagdārupalaṃbite || 5/49 ||
māṇikyaśṛṃkhale dhyeye tatra laṃbitamuttamam |
maṃcamuccāvacamaṇijyotiḥ [kh: -jotiḥ] piṃjaradiṅmukham || 5/50 ||
brahmaviṣṇuśiveśāna catuṣpādamanoharam |
sadāśivavapuḥ kṣipta sujātaphalakojvalam || 5/51 ||
tasyoparisamuttuṃgama saṃkulita talpakam |
atyaṃta mṛdulaṃ hṛdyaṃ kṣīrābdhi dhavalacchaviḥ || 5/52 ||
taduparyapicāstīrṇamupadhānadvayāṃkitam |
29) kausuṃbhavasanaṃ navyaṃ bālārka sadṛśaṃ smaret || 5/53 ||
tatrānaṃdamaheśānamānaṃde śiratoddhatam |
unnaddhakabarīpāśaṃ [kh: unnattha-] kastūrītilakojvalam || 5/54 ||
ālaṃbikarṇā bharaṇāmaṇḍitaṃ gaṇḍasīmanī |
muktāhāreṇaramyeṇa vibhūṣitaśirodharam || 5/55 ||
keyūrakaṭakodāraṃ kaṭisūtrojvaladyutim |
satataṃ yuktayaṃtratvādanaṃbara nijākṛtim || 5/56 ||
karpūradhūlidigdhāṅgamīṣacchrama jalānvitam |
nakhakṣataiśca vividhaiścuṃbanaiśca paraḥśataiḥ || 5/57 ||
kūjitairbahuvistāraiḥ kṣobhaṇaiścabhidānvitaḥ [kh: -taiḥ] |
karaṇairbahuvinyāsai rāśleṣairbahubhedibhiḥ || 5/58 ||
karṇasyaraṇitairhṛdyaiḥ sītkāraiścasusītalaiḥ |
bāhyābhyaṃtabhedoya [kh: bāhyābhyaṃtarabhedoddha] saṃbhoga kramavistaraiḥ 
|| 5/59 ||
lālayaṃtaṃ mudāyuktamānaṃdaparameśvarīm |
tāṃ devīmapitaddhyāna [kh: -tadhyāna] krameṇamatimān smaret || 5/60 ||
tayoḥ sadaiva saṃbhogaṃ dhyāyaṃ dhyāyaṃ manuṃjapet |
svayamavyākulobhūtvā niścalendriyamānasaḥ || 5/61 ||
dvādaśāṃta pade dhyāyan raktaśukla pade guroḥ |
lakṣamātraṃ japenmantraṃ tataḥ siddho bhavennaraḥ || 5/62 ||
daśāṃśaṃ havanaṃ kuryād yonikuṃḍe bhage'thavā [kh: bhagedhavā] |
taddaśāṃśaṃ ca kastūrī karpūrābhyāṃ suvāsitaiḥ || 5/63 ||
30k) vasaṃtanairnavaistoyaiḥ tarpayelliṃgamīśvaram |
taddaśāṃśaṃ mahādevi brāhmaṇān bhojayet priye || 5/64 ||
athavā mithunākārā [kh: -rān] yoginīryoginorcayet |
hṛdyaiḥ pāyasa pūpādyaiḥ [kh: -dyai] rasaiścaiva rasāyanaiḥ || 5/65 ||
lehyaiścoṣyaiśca [kh: lehyaicoṣyaiśca] bhakṣyaiśca dhātupoṣakaraiḥ paraiḥ [kh: 
?] |
madyairmāṃsaiśca matsyaiśca nānāvastūpabṛṃhitaiḥ || 5/66 ||
arucighnaiścopadaṃśairati hṛdyaiścalaḍḍukaiḥ |
arcayed yoginī vīrān daśāṃśaṃ maṃtrasādhakaḥ || 5/67 ||
evaṃ saṃsiddhamaṃtrastu sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate |
ānaṃdabhogān bhugjānaḥ [kh: bhuṃjānaḥ] kṣoṇyāṃ khelati kāyavat || 5/68 ||
itthamānaṃdadevasya mayāvidyā prakāśitā |
atiguhyātva [kh: atiguhyatva] yā rakṣyā na deyā yasyakasyacit || 5/69 ||
śiṣyāyagurubhaktāya viśvāsa niratāya ca |
akutarka pravīṇāya tattvaniṣṭhāyadhīmate || 5/70 ||
nirahaṃkāragarvāya nirdaṃbhāya mahātmane |
eṣā vidyā pradātavyā satyaṃ satyaṃ vadāmyaham || 5/71 ||
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyamuddhṛtya bhujamucyate |
etad vidyāsamaṃ nāsti na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati || 5/72 ||
anāyāsena mokṣāpti ratraproktā maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
imāmabhyasyataḥ puṃso mokṣamārgaḥ karasthitaḥ || 5/73 ||

ityānaṃdataṃtre paṃcamaḥ paṭalaḥ || 5/5 ||




ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ

31k) atha vakṣyami deveśi nāṃdīyāgapurasaram [kh: -purassaram] |
yāgarājakramaḥ sarvapuruṣārthaṃ mahāphalam || 6/1 ||
nāṃdīyāga svarūpaṃ tu prathamaṃ kathayāmite |
brāhmemuhūrte [kh: -hūrta] utbhāya [kh: uddhāya] śrīguruṃ śirasi smaret || 6/2 
||
brahmaraṃdhra sahasrāra kamalodaravartinam |
varābhayayutaṃ śāṃtaṃ śaktyāśliṣṭaṃ manoharam || 6/3 ||
tatpādayuganiṣyaṃdi madhurāmṛtadhārayā |
āplāvita śarīraḥ sannaṃtardhyāyen maheśvarīm || 6/4 ||
ācamūlādā ca vilād [kh: vilāt] vahāṇaḥ [kh: braṃhaṇaḥ] prajvalattanum |
vibhinna ṣaṭsarojanma prakāśa nijavigrahām || 6/5 ||
dhruvacaṃdra [kh: dhṛva-] sudhāsyaṃdaistarpayaṃtīṃ jagattrayam [kh: jagatrayam] 
|
punarviṃśatimādhāraṃ [kh: punarviṃśaṃtī-] dhyāyet [kh: dhyāye] 
tripurasuṃdarīm || 6/6 ||
manasādaśavāraṃ ca japannasya mahāmanum |
nirgamya ca bahirgrāmānmuktamūtramalavyathaḥ [kh: -dhaḥ] || 6/7 ||
jalāśayaṃ samāsādya snāyācchuddhenavāriṇā |
sugaṃdhatailaliptasya piṣṭikodvarti tasya ca || 6/8 ||
maṃtriṇo vihitaṃ snānaṃ na tu rūkṣanimajjanam |
kadāpi kauliko mohānna rūkṣasnānamācaret || 6/9 ||
grahaṇe'pi ca saṃkrāṃtā vamāyāmapi ca [kh: vā] priye |
mūrdhnitvā mūlamaṃtra jāpenāṃ bhoṃjalitrayam || 6/10 ||
32k) tanmaṃtritaṃ jalaṃ pītvā tisraḥsaṃtarpya cāṃbhasā |
triḥ prokṣyātmānamacchācchā [kh: -cchau] paridhāya padau [kh: paḍau] śubhau 
|| 6/11 ||
hrāṃ [kh: ḥ rāṃ] hrīm [kh: ḥ rīṃ] ityādi maṃtreṇa vāryarghyaṃ 
tisramukṣipet [kh: tisramutkṣipet] |
sakuśe sahapuṣpe ca sagaṃdhe tāmrabhājane [kh: tāṃmra-] || 6/12 ||
aśvattha patrasadṛśaṃ yonyākāraṃ ca bhājanam |
tāmrarūpya [kh: tāṃmra-] suvarṇaistu racitaṃ tatpraśasyate || 6/13 ||
atha traipura gāyatryā tripurāyāstri rutkṣipet |
yathā śakti [kh: śaktyā] ca saṃtarpya praviśedyāgamaṃdiram || 6/14 ||
itastataḥ parivṛtaṃ mālābhiḥsumanoharam |
ābaddhapāṭalollo ca maṇidarpaṇa cāmaram || 6/15 ||
kālāgarusamudbhūta [kh: -muddhūta] dhūpāmodasuvāsitam [kh: -suvā * *?]  |
saṃpūritaṃ bahuvidhaiḥ [kh: -vidhair] naivedyairatiśobhanaiḥ [kh: -naḥ?] || 6/16 ||
sugaṃdhibhiśca kusumaiścaṃdanaiśca sa caṃdrakaiḥ |
trividhaiśca manohāri saurabhyaiḥ sarakairapi || 6/17 ||
khabhūjalacaraiścāpi dvitīyairapi [kh: dvitīye-] śobhitam |
piṣṭapaṃcāṃgulodāraṃ pūrṇakuṃbhasamāyutam [kh: pūrna-] || 6/18 ||
paricārakayoṣābhiḥ śuddhaveṣābhiraṃtike |
sarvakarmaṇipaṭvībhiranukūlapravṛttibhiḥ || 6/19 ||
saṃcāriṇībhirmāṇikya dīpikābhiḥ kṛtojvalam |
praviśyayāgasadanaṃ pīṭhamaṃtreṇa kaṃbale || 6/20 ||
33k) saṃpraviśyaguruṃ dhyātvā yajetpaṃcopacārakaiḥ |
tathāgaṇapatiṃ dhyātvā tanmaṃtreṇābhi pūjayet || 6/21 ||
tadvaṃdvapūjanā dhūta vighnavyatikarebudhaḥ |
siṃhāsana maheśānīmarcayetripurāṃbikām [kh: maheśāni-] || 6/22 ||
dvīpanāthaṃ samabhyarya sudhāṃbhodhimanaṃtaram |
ratnadvipaṃ tataḥ paścānnānāvṛkṣamahāvanam || 6/23 ||
kāmakalpaka vāṭīṃ ca tathā [kh: tadā] saṃtānavāṭikām |
haricandanavāṭīṃ ca mandārataruvāṭikām || 6/24 || 6/[kh: nāsti]
pārijātasya vāṭīṃ ca kadaṃbataruvāṭikām |
puṣparāgamayaṃ sālaṃ prākāraṃ padmarāgajam |
gomedhikasya prākāraṃ vajraprākārameva ca || 6/25 ||
vaiḍūryakasya [kh: vaiḍhūrya] prākāramiṃdranīlamayaṃ tathā |
muktāmayaṃ ca prākāraṃ tadāmṛtakaraṃmayam || 6/26 ||
prākāraṃ vaidrumaṃ caiva tatomāṇikyamaṇṭapam |
sahasrastaṃbhanilayaṃ sudhā vāṭimanaṃtaram [kh: vāṭī-] || 6/27 ||
ānaṃda vāṭikāṃ caiva vimarśasya ca vāṭikām |
bālātapasamudgāraṃ caṃdrikād [kh: caṃdrikod] gārameva ca || 6/28 ||
mahāśṛṃgāra parighāṃ mahāpadmāṭavīṃ [kh: -padnāṭavīṃ] tataḥ |
ciṃtāmaṇigṛhaṃ caiva gṛharājaṃ samarcayet || 6/29 ||
pūrvāmnāya mayaṃ tasya pūrvadvāraṃ samarcayet |
ratnapradīpavalayaṃ madhye siṃhāsanaṃ tathā || 6/30 ||
34k) brahmaviṣṇu śiveśāna sadeśamanu rūpakaiḥ |
pādopariṣṭaphalakairarcitaṃ maṃcamarcayet || 6/31 ||
kausuṃbhāstaraṇaṃ navyaṃ tat tanmaṃtraiḥ samarcayet |
mūlenamadhyamabhyarcya mūlakhaṃḍaistrikoṇakam || 6/32 ||
mūlenābhimanuṣyadbhirbhūta [kh: -nuṣvadbhirbhūta] śuddhiṃ samācaret |
triḥprāṇān samudāyamya bhūtāvaraṇavidyayā || 6/33 ||
pātālasthā nadhobhaumānvighnaṃścaiva [kh: nathobhaumānvighnāṃścaiva] na 
bhogatān |
pārṣṇighātakarā [kh: -kārā] sphoṭasamudaṃcitavaktrakaiḥ [kh: -vakvrakaiḥ] || 
6/34 ||
utsārayecca [kh: -ye ca] matimān devya'haṃ bhāvasaṃyutaḥ |
karaśuddhakhyamanunākaraśuddhiṃ vidārayan || 6/35 ||
ātmarakṣākhya manunā kuryādātmanirikṣaṇam [kh: -nirakṣaṇam] |
devyātmāsanamaṃtreṇa svasyāsanamudīritam || 6/36 ||
śrīcakrāsanamaṃtreṇa śrīcakrāsanamucyate |
maṃtrāṇāmāsane sarvamaṃtrāsanamanuṃ viduḥ || 6/37 ||
sādhyasiddhāsanecakra devī nāmāsanaṃ śrutam |
iti pīṭhānisaṃkalpya [kh: -saṃkalpyaṃ] bālāvṛtyā ṣaḍaṃgakam || 6/38 ||
sarvajñādi ṣaḍaṃgaṃ ca dvayaṃ kuryādataṃdritaḥ |
svaraistuvaśinī yuktā śirasinyasyate priye || 6/39 ||
kāmeśvarī lalāṭe tu kavargeṇasamāyutā |
modinī tu bhruvormadhye [kh: bhṛvor-] ca vargeṇasamanvitā || 6/40 ||
35k) vimalā tu galasthāne ṭa vargeṇavibhūṣitā |
aruṇāhṛdayet [kh: -yen] yasyāt tavargeṇavibhūṣitā || 6/41 ||
jayanī tu pavargeṇa nābhisthāne prapūjayet |
sarveśvarī yādivarṇa catuṣṭaya dhṛtāmaṇau || 6/42 ||
mūleśādi catuṣṭasya yukta [kh: yuktau] kālinyupāsyate |
evaṃ vāgdevatā nyāsaṃ nyased varṇān mahāmano || 6/43 ||
mūrdhni mūlena [kh: mūle ca ] hṛdaye netra tritaya eva ca |
śrotrayoryugalecāpi mukhe ca bhujayostathā || 6/44 ||
pṛṣṭhejānvośca nābhau ca nyaset paṃca daśākṣarān |
nyasya vā nyasya vā ṣoḍhā cakranyāsā ubhau priye || 6/45 ||
śuddhāṃbhasā vāmabhāge caturśraṃ prapūjayet |
abhyarcyagaṃdhapuṣpādaiḥ sādhāraṃ śaṃkhamarcayet || 6/46 ||
āpūryaśucito yena śodhayedali biṃdunā |
akadādi [kh: akathādi] trirekhāṃtā halakṣābhyaṃtarasthitam || 6/47 ||
haṃsamātraṃ [kh: -maṃtraṃ] vilikhyātra ṣaḍaṃgena samarcayet |
tadaṃbhasā puro bhāge caturaśraṃ ṣaḍaśrakam || 6/48 ||
vṛttaṃ trikoṇaṃ binduṃ ca vilikhyādhāramarcayet |
ādhāramanunādhātra pūjanīyāḥ śuceḥ kalāḥ || 6/49 ||
pātrasyamanunā pātramādhāre saṃprayojayet |
abhyarcya bhāskarakalāstataścādimapūraṇam || 6/50 ||
36k) vidhāyā mṛta mānośca [kh: bhānośca] kalāstatra samarcayet |
upādimaṃ madhyamaṃ [kh: mādhyamaṃ] ca tatra nikṣipya buddhimān || 6/51 ||
akathādi mayaṃ vahneḥ puraṃ tatra samālikhet |
koṇeṣu bālāmālikhya bahiṣṣaṭkoṇamālikhet || 6/52 ||
tatṣaṭkoṇaṃ samabhyarcya ṣaḍaṃgena vidhānataḥ |
arghyaṃ spṛṣṭvā sṛṣṭimukhaiścaturnavati raśmibhiḥ || 6/53 ||
akhaṃḍādyaiḥ paṃcamaṃtrairmūlenāpyabhimaṃtrayet |
etairmaṃtrairmaṃtritaṃ syādādimaṃ paramāmṛtam || 6/54 ||
samastadoṣanirmuktaṃ samastamalavarjitam |
paramānaṃdajanako bhavet sidhyaikasādhanaḥ || 6/55 ||
aśeṣaduritadhvaṃsa kārimānaṃdarūpiṇīm |
trikhaṇḍamudrayā bāhya saptamudrāśca darśayet || 6/56 ||
sthāpanaṃ sannidhānaṃ ca sannirodhāva kuṇṭhane |
vaṃdanaṃ dhenu yonī ca saptamudrāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 6/57 ||
catuṣṣaṣṭyupacāreṇa toṣayettāṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ |
catuṣṣaṭyupacārāṇāṃ maṃtrā devī pṛthak pṛthak || 6/58 ||
navamudrāḥ pradarśyātha mūlena triścitaṃ yajet |
aṃgamaṃtrairaṃgaṣaṭkaṃ tithi nityāśca ṣoḍaśa || 6/59 ||
nāthāvaliṃ ca tanmaṃtrairmunivedāṣṭa saṃkhyayā |
śirasi svaguroḥ pādau bhaktipūrṇaḥ samarcayet || 6/60 ||
37k) mahīpuratraye bāhya mahīgehe'ṇimādikāḥ |
abhyaṃtaramahīgehe brāhmādyāḥ [kh: brāhmyā-] parikīrtitāḥ || 6/61 ||
tadaṃtara mahīgehe mudrāḥ saṃkṣobhaṇādayaḥ |
samarpaṇenamanunā yoginībhyaḥ samarcayet || 6/62 ||
tripurākhyaṃ [kh: -khyāṃ] tu cakreśīṃ tanmaṃtreṇa yajedatha |
saṃkṣobhamudrāṃ saṃdarśya [kh: -śyaṃ] ṣoḍaśāraṃ prapūjayet || 6/63 ||
kāmākarṣiṇikāmukhyāḥ kalāstatra kalānidheḥ |
arcyāstu guptayoginya [kh: -nyas] tripureśīti cakriṇī || 6/64 ||
mudrā ca drāviṇī proktā tatoṣṭāraṃ yajed budhaḥ |
anaṃgakusumādyāstu śaktayastatra saṃsthitāḥ || 6/65 ||
yā guptatarayoginyaścakriṇī pura suṃdarī |
mudrā cā karṣiṇī jñeyā tatastu bhuvanārake || 6/66 ||
sarvasaṃkṣobhiṇī mukhyāścaturdaśa samarcayet |
saṃpradāyāstu yoginyastripurā śrīśca cakriṇī || 6/67 ||
mudrā vaśī kṛtā jñeyā daśāramathapūjayet |
sarvasidhyādikāḥ [kh: -kā] śaktiḥ kulottirṇā tu yoginī || 6/68 ||
cakriṇīpuravāsā ca mudronmādakarī śubhā |
aṃtardaśāredevyastu sarvajñādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 6/69 ||
etāni garbhayoginyaścakreśī [kh: -kreḥśī] puramālinī |
mahāṃkuśā ca mudrāsyādathāṣṭāra mahāvṛtau || 6/70 ||
38k) vaśinyādyāḥ prapūjyāsyā rahasyāścāpi yoginīḥ |
cakriṇī tripurā siddhā mudrā bhavati khecarī || 6/71 ||
athavarṇādyā yudhāni pūjyānāyudha vidyayā |
tryaśe [kh: tryaśre] kāmeśvarī vajreśvarī ca bhagamālinī || 6/72 ||
lalitā ca tato madhye pūjaniyāmanasvinā [kh: -niyyā-] |
yoginyati rahasyā ca tripurāṃbā ca cakriṇī || 6/73 ||
sarvabījamayā [kh: -mayī] mudrā tataścānaṃdacakrake [kh: -cakrike] |
biṃdurūpe mahollāse lalitāṃ parameśvarīm || 6/74 ||
parāpararahasyākhyāṃ yonimudrāsamanvitām |
tasyā ekāsane [kh: ṭakāsane] deva matisaṃsaktavigraham || 6/75 ||
udyadādityasaṃkāśaṃ sadā ṣoḍaśavarṣiṇam |
kaṃdarpa koṭilāvaṇyaṃ puṣpabāṇadhanurddharam || 6/76 ||
pāśāṃkuśadharaṃ sarvamaṇibhūṣaṇabhūṣitam |
hakārādyena maṃtreṇa kāmeśvaramupācaret || 6/77 ||
kakārādyena maṃtreṇa kāmeśīṃ paripūjayet |
itthaṃ navāvṛtiṃ [kh: -kṛtiṃ] devīṃ [kh: devī] pūjayitvā vidhānataḥ || 6/78 ||
dhūpadīpanivedyāṃte mudrāḥ saṃdarśayet kramāt |
saubhāgyahṛdayaṃ japtvā dhyāyet kāmakalāmatha || 6/79 ||
vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa homakarmasamācaret |
atha saṃtarpya mūlābhyāṃ devaṃ devīṃ ca pūjayet || 6/80 ||
ānaṃdā dvayamāpannastayoraikyaṃ viciṃtayet |
ekībhūte tayordehe [kh: -ho] yajedānaṃda bhairavam || 6/81 ||
39k) pūrvokta manunā devī ya [kh: sa] ṣaḍaṃgena maṃtravit |
bindubhistarpayennāthamānaṃda [kh: baṃdhu-] parameśvaram || 6/82 ||
daśāvatārān [kh: daśavārān] śatavārān sahasraṃ vā maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
yāgarājakramasyeti nāṃdi pūjā smṛtā [kh: smṛtair] budhaiḥ || 6/83 ||
krameṇayāgarājaṃ ca vakṣyāmyā karṇaya priye |
yaṃ śrutvā sahā * [kh: sahaṃsā] siddhi pūjayed [kh: -yad] yadi mānavaḥ || 6/84 ||

ityānaṃdataṃtre ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ || 6/6 ||




saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ

iti nāṃdī mukhaṃ kṛtvā yāgarājaṃ samācaret |
yāgarājavidhānaṃ ca marutāmapi durlabham [kh: -bha] || 7/1 ||
tasya saṃśravaṇaṃ puṇya janakaṃ pāpakṛṃtanam |
paramānaṃdasaubhāgya nimittaṃ sarvasiddhidam || 7/2 ||
sakṛtkṛto'pi yāgo'yaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam |
prativarṣaṃ tu vihitaṃ kalpitaṃ tasya siddhaye || 7/3 ||
pratimāsaṃ kṛte devī śivatvamapidāsyati [kh: -pi * *?ti] |
parvasukriyamāṇastu kadācidapi vāṃcchitam || 7/4 ||
mahāyāgārthamīśāni [kh: mahāyogārtha-] tarugulmalatādayaḥ |
brahmarūpeṇa suśroṇimayā [kh: suśroṇī-] sṛṣṭā jagattraye [kh: jagatraye] || 7/5 ||
tāsāṃ vividhaśaktīnāṃ [kh: -nā] moṣadhīnāṃ maheśvari [kh: -rīṃ] |
vyastānāṃ ca samastānāṃ rasena kriyate madhu || 7/6 ||
40k) śāstrokte [kh: -ktaṃ] madanaṃ [kh: madhanaṃ] kṛtvā tena dravyeṇapūjayet |
sarvāḥ [kh: sarvā] striyomayā sṛṣṭāḥ liṃgeśvarasamarcane || 7/7 ||
saparyāḥ [kh: saparyā]  sādhaneṣveṣu mukhyā etāḥ sulocanāḥ |
prācyāṃgānāmudīcyānāmaṃgānāmapi madhyamaḥ || 7/8 ||
sārabhūtamidaṃ prāhuḥ kulaśaktervapuḥ śuci [kh: suci] |
gaṃdhapuṣpaṃ ca tāṃbūlamāleporati bheṣajam || 7/9 ||
ādimaṃ copādimaṃ ca madhyamaṃ ca caturthakam |
paṃcamāścāpare [kh: -rī] paṃcadaśaite yāgasādhanam || 7/10 ||
eteṣu mukhyāḥ paṃcasyurādimādyāḥ kuleśvarī |
tatrāpi mukhyau dvāvevatvādimaḥ paṃcamaḥ priye || 7/11 ||
ādimovastu vimalaṃ paṃcamaḥ kulaśaktayaḥ |
ādimaṃ cāpyaṃtimaṃ ca sārabhūtaṃ janārdanaḥ || 7/12 ||
pratyāhārapade veśājagrāhapatagadhvajaḥ |
aṣṭamenāvatāreṇa baladeva svarūpiṇā || 7/13 ||
jagatīmaṃḍala mahābhāra bhāravibhoginā |
anaṃtamūrtināyena rohiṇī garbhajanmanā || 7/14 ||
ādimaṃ sārabhūtaṃ tu vavrevārijalocanaḥ |
tasya devasya mahato balabhadrasya dhīmataḥ || 7/15 ||
ādimasya tu sārasya viraho [kh: -he] na kadācana |
revatī vadanāṃ bhojavāsitaṃ madhuraṃ madhu || 7/16 ||
41k) pibatāṃbalabhadreṇa dhāryate jagatī talam |
kṛṣṇājñayā baleṃdrasya varuṇenopaḍhaukitā [kh: -lokitā] || 7/17 ||
madirābalasāmagrī [kh: -gri] jananī vīryavardhanī [kh: -varddhinī] |
gomaṃtaparvatopāṃtanīpa koṭarato bhavat [kh: bhavet] || 7/18 ||
taṃ dṛṣṭvārto rohiṇeya [kh: rauhiṇeya] svānaṃda parameśvarīm |
pibannānaṃdamakhilaṃ [kh: -lā] jagattrāṇāyajagmivān [kh: jagatrāṇāya-] || 
7/19 ||
athānyena śarīreṇa tamālasyāmalatviṣā [kh: -śyāma-] |
naṃdānaṃdasamudrasya samuttaṃbhasitatviṣā || 7/20 ||
antimasya tu sārasya saṃvitpūjanavastunaḥ |
upasaṃgrahaṇaṃ cakre bhagavānabjalocanaḥ || 7/21 ||
sahidevo yaduśreṣṭho vasudeva suto'pisan |
kaṃsapratāraṇāyātha naṃdasyātmajatāṃ gataḥ || 7/22 ||
tatra braje mahābhāgaḥ saṅyātītāḥ [kh: saṃkhyā-] sahasraśaḥ |
gopīḥ [kh: gopī] nisargamadhurāḥ prakṛtyā suraceṣṭitāḥ || 7/23 ||
kulapūjanavastūnijagṛhe jagadīśvaraḥ |
na tā gopyo guṇodārāḥ [kh: -rā] yena bhuktāmurāriṇā || 7/24 ||
na tad [kh: tat] bṛṃdāvanasthānaṃ yatrānena na khelitam |
svānaṃdamūrti sidhyarthametāsumuraśāsanaḥ || 7/25 ||
yāgarājakramaṃcakre mohayannakhilān janān |
muralīvādakṛtyena vallavīrabhinaṃdayan || 7/26 ||
42k) veṣaṃ madanagopasya navapallava pāṭalam |
puṇḍrekṣu cāpasahita puṣpāśuga pariṣkṛtam || 7/27 ||
kalayanvilasatpāśa sāṃkuśo vimatāṃkuśaḥ |
cikrīḍabahudhā kṛṣṇaścitrābhiḥ kulaśaktibhiḥ [kh: kula *?kti-] || 7/28 ||
evaṃ bālye brajasthāne paścādrājye ca yauvate |
narakeṇāvaruddhānāṃ sahasrāṇyapi ṣoḍaśa || 7/29 ||
anyā nāmanavadyānāṃbu [kh: sā-] bhuje puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ [kh: ṣuṣkare-] |
punaścarukmiṇī [kh: -rukmaṇī] satyājāṃbavatyāḍhyamujvalam [kh: -
dhyamujvalam] || 7/30 ||
mahāṃtaḥ purikā vargaṃ kulācāryaiḥ [kh: kulārcāryai] samāharat |
ataḥ pradhānamānaṃda devapūjanakarmaṇi [kh: -ṇī] || 7/31 ||
ādimādapi sārāḥsyussapriye [kh: sārāsyussaśrī ye] kulaśaktayaḥ |
pūjayā [kh: pūjāyā] api sarvasyā viśrāṃtistatra pārvatī || 7/32 ||
tasmādetadupādeyaṃ kulaśaktyākhyasādhanam |
āvirbhavaṃti yatraiva svānaṃdagradilāstviṣaḥ [kh: -grahilāstviṣaḥ] || 7/33 ||
nāṃdīmukhasya [kh: -mukhyasya] saṃsthāyāṃ balaṃ datvā ca maṃtrikaḥ |
namaskṛtya maheśānīṃ mahātripurasuṃdarīm || 7/34 ||
bahubhirmadhuraiḥ [kh: -rai] stotraistoṣayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ [kh: vi * kṣaṇa] |
pradakṣiṇa namaskārān kṛtvā bhaktiparānataḥ [kh: -bharānataḥ] || 7/35 ||
puṣpāṃjaliṃ samādāya sahagaṃdhakuśākṣatam |
maṃtreṇānena tatpāde vikṣipan daṃḍavannamet || 7/36 ||
43k) sarvaviśrāṃti janakaṃ samastānaṃdasuṃdaram |
ānaṃdayāgamadhunā karomyanugṛhāṇamām || 7/37 ||
ityanujñāpya deveśaṃ punarutthāyasādhakaḥ [kh: -ruddhāya-] |
svaguruṃ gurupatnīṃ ca dviṣaṭkiṃte [kh: dviṣaṭkāṃte] triśo'rcayet || 7/38 ||
athadūtīṃ pramuditāṃ śubhalakṣaṇalakṣitām |
kulākulāṣṭakādīni nimaṃtryāhūyaśobhitām || 7/39 ||
ānaṃdadeva maṃtreṇa maṃtritairgaṃdha tailakaiḥ |
abhyarcya sarvamapyaṃgamavamṛjyavicakṣaṇaḥ || 7/40 ||
paridhāpya paṭaṃ śuddhamaruṇaṃ vāthavāsitaṃ [kh: vādha-] |
athavā citramathavā patrorṇaṃ sumanoharam || 7/41 ||
bhūṣaṇairapi saṃbhūṣya [kh: -bhūtha] tāṭaṃkāṃgadakaṃkaṇaiḥ |
kāṃcīlatā tulākoṭi hāraikāvali vālibhiḥ || 7/42 ||
sīmaṃtasīma siṃdūra kālakajjalayāvakaiḥ |
anyaiścā bharaṇaistāṃtu bhūṣayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ || 7/43 ||
āsane mṛdunisphīta tūle sādhūpaveśayet |
na viraktā navātyaṃta raktā na kalaha priyā || 7/44 ||
na caṃḍinoddhatālā pānakrūrā nānṛtoditā |
nānyaraktā nātipuṣṭā nātikharvā navākṛśā || 7/45 ||
nātidīrghā nātikālī nātyuccānāti pāṭalā |
narūkṣāṃgī [kh: -gi] na jihmākṣī [kh: jiṃhā-] na ca karkaśa kīkasā || 7/46 ||
44k) na sirālatanunaiva [kh: -tanurnaiva] sthūlamadhyānadanturā |
na kuṭṭanī [kh: -ni] samācārā nopahāsaparāyaṇā || 7/47 ||
na kaulika kṛtadveṣā na cārvāka mata priyā |
na durgaṃdhā na duḥśīlā na ca duṣparivādinī || 7/48 ||
na ca tṛṣṇābhi bhūtā na kṣutpipāsādi pīḍitā |
na dīrgharogagrastāṃgī yogyā bhavati dūtikā || 7/49 ||
dūtikāyā dvidhā vakṣye saṃskāraṃ bāhyamātaram [kh: -māṃtaram] |
śārīramathajīvasya saṃskāradvitayaṃ dhruve [kh: dhṛve] || 7/50 ||
śārīredvau [kh: śa-] śivedvau ca saṃskārāste [kh: -rāsteś] caturvidhāḥ |
abhyaṃgodvartane caiva snānamālepanaṃ tataḥ || 7/51 ||
keśasaṃ dhūpanaṃ keśa racanāgaṃdhayojanam |
mradīyavastradharaṇaṃ yavakālaṃ kṛtiḥ paṭe [kh: * *?] || 7/52 ||
eṣa śārīrasaṃskāro bāhyaḥ parvatanaṃdini [kh: -nī] |
ābhyaṃtaro'pi [kh: ācchyaṃtaro'pi] vaktavyaḥ śṛṇudīrghavilocane [kh: śruṇu-] 
|| 7/53 ||
ānaṃdanāthasthānasya tryāśrākāreṇa [kh: tryaśrā] tiṣṭhataḥ |
ālomaśaṃtvaṃ [kh: -śatvaṃ] svacchatvaṃ ślakṣṇatvaṃ ca sugaṃdhitā || 7/54 ||
sudhūpitatvaṃ sārdratvamaṃtarmitajaladravam |
sukomalatvaṃ śuddhatvaṃ susparśatvaṃ ca pārvati [kh: -tī] || 7/55 ||
īṣadvikāsikamalakroḍakomalakāṃtitā |
sūkṣmakṣaumātapatratvaṃ [kh: sūkṣmakāmāta-] galaccaṃdrakalā sthitiḥ || 
7/56 ||
45k) nātyaṃta śītalatvaṃ ca nātyuṣṇatvaṃ  ca suṃdari [kh: -rī] |
svabhāva sukumāratvaṃ svabhāvena sugaṃdhitā || 7/57 ||
saṃskāra grāhitā sadyaḥ klinnasyāpi dṛḍhasthitiḥ [kh: -taḥ] |
ativaiśālya rāhityamatisaṃkoca hīnatā || 7/58 ||
sparśanādeva sahasā manmathadravapūritā |
aṣṭabhogopadheyatvaṃ bhogavegasahiṣṇutā || 7/59 ||
aṃtaḥ karkaśacarmatva rāhityaṃ snigdhavarṇatā |
atyaṃtacaṇḍakaṇḍūyā rahitatvaṃ ca pārvati [kh: -tī] || 7/60 ||
yonisthānagatairnaikai [kh: -ke] raktavāhādibhirgadaiḥ |
akliṣṭatā puṣṭatā ca rathanemisamānatā [kh: radhane-] || 7/61 ||
aśvattha dalatulyatvamanuththatamaṇi [kh: -nutthatamaṇi] sthitiḥ |
phaṇiśīrṣasamānatvaṃ prasannadravatā tathā || 7/62 ||
sadyaḥ prasūta susnigdha kalaviṃkāsyatulyatā |
sarvalakṣaṇa yuktatvaṃ kulakṣaṇavihīnatā || 7/63 ||
etā dṛśāguṇāḥ proktaḥ [kh: proktā] dūtikāyāḥ [kh: -yā] smarālaye |
saṃskārasya mahimneva [kh: mahimnaiva] bhāsaṃte te guṇāḥ priye || 7/64 ||
tasmātsaṃskāra vai mukhyaṃ dūtikāyā na śobhate |
saṃskāropi samastāni vastūninyāvasetpriye [kh: vastūnyāsādayet-] || 7/65 ||
saṃskṛtaṃ tu bhagasthānaṃ dūtikāyā nagātmaje |
sarvasaubhāgyajanakaṃ sarvasiddhimahāpadaṃ || 7/66 ||
46k) sarvānaṃdakaraṃ caiva sarvamuktividhāyakaṃ |
ānaṃda bhairavasyārcāsādhanaṃ viśvamohanam || 7/67 ||
mahaujasāṃ guṇāḍhyānāṃ [kh: guṇāḍyānāṃ] vīrāṇāṃ raṃjanapradam |
suśobhadakramagrāsī samastāpannikṛṃtanam [kh: namastā-] || 7/68 ||
ānaṃdaśakterbhavanamanaṃgasya vihārabhūḥ |
veśmacaṃdrakalānāṃ ca sadmasaubhāgyasaṃpadām || 7/69 ||
asaṃskṛtabhagasthānamati pāpapravartakam |
samastarogajanakaṃ daurbhāgyasyapravartakam [kh: daurbbhāgyasya-] || 7/70 ||
narakakleśa saṃtāpakārakaṃ durdaśārakam |
lokopakrośajanakaṃ lokadhikkāradāyakam || 7/71 ||
āpātakaṃ ca durgaṃdhi mūtragaṃdhi malānvitam |
phelinaṃ [kh: phenilaṃ] naṣṭamucchiṣṭamapakṛṣṭaṃ vicarcitam || 7/72 ||
lomaśaṃ lokaniṃdyaṃ ca garhaṇīyaṃ [kh: garhiṇiyyaṃ] ca kālikaiḥ |
aṃtaḥ karkkaśacarmāḍhyamaṃtargojihvayānvitam || 7/73 ||
aṃtaḥ karkaśakīlatvaṅnibiḍaṃ [kh: -kīlatvaṅvi?biḍaṃ] karkaśaiḥ kiṇaiḥ |
pāmakaṇḍūpracuritaṃ pāpiṣṭaṃ kathitaṃ dravam || 7/74 ||
asaṃskāra daśādeva [kh: vaśā-] jāyate dūtikābhagam |
tasmāt [kh: asmāt] saṃskāraracanā karttavyā yoginījanaiḥ || 7/75 || 7/
itopi kinnubhāgyaṃ syāt svabhāvānmaladūṣitam [kh: svabhāvā * * *?ṣitam |
svakīya vapure vai tat pūjāsādhanamiṣyate [kh: -na*? ṣyate] || 7/76 ||
47k) tasmāt susaṃskṛtedūtī madanālayamaṇṭape |
sannidhatte paraḥsākṣādānaṃdādvaita rūpadhṛt || 7/77 ||
iti saṃskārabhāgo'yaṃ bhagasya pravivecitaḥ |
dūtī mukhasya saṃskāraḥ kathyate śṛṇusaṃdari [kh: śruṇusuṃdarī] || 7/78 ||
daṃtānāṃviṣamāṇāṃ ca gharṣaṇaṃ śastradhārayā |
uttejanaṃ ca śastreṇa nirmalīkaraṇaṃ punaḥ || 7/79 ||
dagdhipūgarajodānādati [kh: dagdha-] ślakṣṇātvakalpanam [kh: ślakṣṇatva-] |
tailagaṇḍuṣaṇāt kāṃtirdaṃtānāmupajāyate || 7/80 ||
nālikelaphalasyāṃbu [kh: nālikera-] gaṃdūṣeṇātiramyatā |
trikaṭu triphalāmāṃsi guḍatvakpatrakesaraiḥ || 7/81 ||
tālisaiḥ sahapāṭīrairnnālikerāṃbu peṣitaiḥ |
kṛtvākṣa sadṛśān piṇḍān taṃ pūrvaṃ dūtikāgilet || 7/82 ||
tena kaṃṭha sthitānāṃ tu śleṣmaṇāṃ gandhakāriṇām [kh: gadha-] |
malenaivāpa sṛṣṭānāṃ sarveṣāṃ śamanaṃ bhavet || 7/83 ||
kastūryapi ca karpūraṃ khadirasya duvaiḥ [kh: duvauḥ] saha |
mardaniyaṃ [kh: -niyyaṃ] prayatnena guṭikākārayedbudhaḥ || 7/84 ||
tena sārddhaṃ [kh: sārthaṃ] tricaturān vāraṃ carvitavīṭikā |
pūtikāmati [kh: dūtikā-] saurabhya śālivaktraṃ prakalpayet || 7/85 ||
iti vaktrasya saṃskāraḥ kathitaḥ kulanāyike |
itthaṃ śarīrasaṃskāro bāhyābhyaṃtara īritaḥ || 7/86 ||
48k) atha tasyā dūtikāyāyo jīvātmatayā sthitaḥ |
tasyāpi devi saṃskāraṃ bruvebāhyamathāṃtaram || 7/87 ||
anādya vidyāmalanībhavenevopabṛṃhitam |
dūtikā jīvanāthopi saṃskāraṃ paramarhati || 7/88 ||
malānāmuddhatāṃ vṛttiṃ paśyaparvatanaṃdinī |
yadasaṃge'pi jīvākhye śive khelati nirbhayā || 7/89 ||
malāpanodanārtthāya sarvāḥ śāstrapravṛttayaḥ |
svajīvākhya maheśasya bādya [kh: nādya] vidyāvakuṃṭhitaḥ || 7/90 ||
notpādayati devasya svarūpaṃ jīvarūpiṇaḥ |
malāpanodikāḥ kiṃtu samastāḥ pūjanakramāḥ || 7/91 ||
yadi dūtījīva śive malālirnāpanudyate |
tat saṃsargavaśācchuddha svātmāpi malinī bhavet || 7/92 ||
saṃsargajā doṣaguṇābhavaṃtyanyeva [kh: -syaiva] niścayaḥ |
samayaśravaṇaṃ tasyāḥ prathamaṃ parikīrtitam || 7/93 ||
tatvatrayaṃ viśuddhiśca vidyānāmapi śikṣaṇam |
alpavarṇena [kh: -vargeṇa] maṃtreṇa pāśanirdahakāriṇā [kh: -rīṇī] || 7/94 ||
ayatna śikṣaṇīyena [kh: -ṇiyyena] śivamārgasya bodhanam |
na rāgānna ca lobhādvā na ca mohānmahīyasaḥ [kh: -mahiyyasaḥ] || 7/95 ||
strīṇaṃ [kh: -ṇāṃ] samarcanaṃ kāryaṃ śāstramātrānupālanāt |
prakṛtyāmugdhamanasastriyastatvaṃ na jānate || 7/96 ||
49k) vadāmi śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] he dūti mattastatva kathāmimām |
striyaḥ [kh: -ya] svabhāva ca palā [kh: palāḥ] prakṛtyāgarvitākṣayāḥ [kh: -
śayāḥ] || 7/97 ||
nadī pūrasadṛkṣeṇa kiyadvāsaravarttinā |
stanottaṃbhana dṛṣṭena yauvanena madoddhatāḥ || 7/98 ||
kāryāṇyakāryavat paśyatyakāryāṇi ca kāryavat |
aviśvāsaḥ samasteṣu pratyayona gurorgiri || 7/99 ||
sāhasaṃ sarvadā nitya manuṣaktasya mānase |
doṣāṇāṃ cāyutānāṃ ca sannipātā [kh: -pā *?] imāḥ [kh: * *?] smṛtāḥ |
śāstra dṛṣṭyārcanaṃ  svasya svarāgaika [kh: -gai *?]  nimittakam [kh: *?mitta-] |
manyamānādurācārāḥ pataṃtinarake striyaḥ || 7/101 ||
yathā pūjā sādhanaṃ syāt gaṃdhapuṣpasrajaḥ śubhāḥ |
tāṃbūlamanulepaṃ ca vāsaśca mukhavāsikā || 7/102 ||
ādimaścadvitīyaṃ ca madhyamaṃ ca caturthakam [kh: -rtthakam] |
sarvametan maheśasya pūjāsādhanitaṃ yathā || 7/103 ||
tathāsmadīya [kh: tathān-] bhāgyena pūrvajanmakṛtaiḥ śubhaiḥ |
asmadvapuridaṃ tucchaṃ pūjāsādhanituṃ budhaiḥ || 7/104 ||
ataḥ kṛtārtha [kh: -rttha] bhūyā sā mitiyāmanute vadhūḥ |
sālabheduttamāllo/kān [kh: -mānlokān] sādhakena mahātmanā || 7/105 ||
brahmaloke satyaloke devānāmapi vā pade |
viṣṇuloke rudraloke krīḍaṃti bahuvibhramāḥ || 7/106 ||
ante siṃhāsanādhisyā mukhyā bhavati kiṃkarī |
50k) mattaḥ śṛṇvakhilaṃ [kh: śruṇva -] dharmamīdṛgbhāgyaṃ yadīcchasi [kh: 
yadiccha-] || 7/107 ||
dūtikābhava pūjārthaṃ [kh: -rtthaṃ] sadgatiṃ te nalapsyasi |
ityādibhiryūkti yuktairvaca naiḥ śāstrasammitaiḥ || 7/108 ||
dūtikā jīvanāthasya saṃskāraṃ [kh: saṃsāraṃ] bāhyamācaret |
itthaṃ saṃskāraśuddhāṃgīmāsane mṛdunisthitām  [kh: mṛduniścitām] || 7/109 
||
dūtīkāṃ bhāvayed devīṃ [kh: devī] sākṣādānaṃdabhairavīm |
ānaṃdabhairavīṃ cāpi maṃtreṇābāhya sādhakaḥ [kh: -kāḥ] || 7/110 ||
paṃcopacāraiḥ saṃpūjyatarpayedali biṃdubhiḥ |
tryaśre sthitāḥ punaḥ pūjyā rati prītimanobhavāḥ || 7/111 ||
paṃcakoṇasya mūle tu drāviṇyādyāśca devatāḥ |
drāviṇī śoṣiṇī caivaṃ [kh: caiva] baṃdhinī mohinī tathā || 7/112 ||
unmādinī ca paṃcaitā manobhūbāṇa devatāḥ |
paṃcārasyāṃ cale pūjyāḥ kāmarājomaheśvarī [kh: -maleśvarī] || 7/113 ||
kaṃdarpomanmathaśceti tathāmakaraketanaḥ [kh: tadā-] |
manobhavaḥ paṃcamaśca jagatkṣobhakarā ime || 7/114 ||
athāṣṭapatre bālārka varṇabāṇadhanurddharāḥ |
anaṃgaśaktayoṣṭau tu pūjanīyāmanīṣibhiḥ [kh: pūjanīya -] || 7/115 ||
anaṃgakusumānaṃga mekhalā ca dvitīyakā |
anaṃgamadanānaṃga madanāturayā saha [kh: sahā] || 7/116 ||
anaṃgarekhā cānaṃga vegānaṃgākuśāpi ca |
anaṃgamālinī cāṣṭau pūjanīyā vidhānataḥ || 7/117 ||
51k) evaṃ saṃpūjya tadbhāsvaddhṛdayāṃburuha [kh: tatbhāsva-] sthitam |
ānaṃdacakramānaṃda kolāhalavivarddhanam || 7/118 ||
taccakradevatā dehādutthitairaruṇāruṇaiḥ [kh: -duddhitaira-] |
taṭitprabhā samullāsapiṃjaraiḥ kāmaraśmibhiḥ || 7/119 ||
samastaṃ kṣobhitaṃ tasyā nāḍīcakraṃ viciṃtayet |
anaṃgaśakti saṃkṣobhāṃ nāḍībhyogalitaṃ navam || 7/120 ||
eka pravāhatā pannaṃ rasaśroṇitamiśritam |
śṛṃgāravāhinīpūra pūrṇarāgāṃ kurāruṇam || 7/121 ||
samastānaṃda janakaṃ cittaviśrāṃti dāyakam |
manmathadrava saṃdoha juṣṭamarthaprabhedanaiḥ [kh: -marttha-] || 7/122 ||
nītvā caṃdrakalāsthāne yonicakraṃ samarccayet |


śrīdevyuvāca


keyaṃ caṃdrakalānāma kutra tiṣṭhati śaṃkara || 7/123 ||
kiṃ rūpā kiṃ prakārā sā kiṃ varṇā kathayasva me |


śrīkāmeśvaraḥ 


śṛṇukāmakalānāma [kh: śruṇu-] nāḍībhogāṃbuvāhinī || 7/124 ||
sarvatra tasyā nāḍīṣu vyāptiḥ śāṃkari vidyate |
nāḍīcakraṃ samastaṃ ca tayā vyāptaṃ varānane || 7/125 ||
śarīre nāḍikāḥ kāścitprāṇavāhinya īritāḥ |
kāścidraktapravāhiṇyaḥ [kh: -ṇyāḥ] kāścidutsāhavāhikāḥ [k: nāsti] || 7/126 
||
kāścidrasapravāhinyaḥ kāściddainya pravāhikāḥ | [k: nāsti]
kāścidānaṃdavāhinya [k: nāsti] ekaivamalavāhinī || 7/127 ||
ekāmūtravahā nāḍī kāścidiṃdriyakāhikāḥ [kh: -vāhikāḥ] |
aṣṭaubhogāṃbuvāhinyaḥ [kh: -nyas] tāsu caṃdrakalā parā || 7/128 ||
52k) ādyā caṃdrakalā proktā dvitīyā [kh: dvitiyyā] caṃdracaṃdrikāḥ |
caṃdrarekhā caṃdravatī tathā caṃdramahodayā || 7/129 ||
caṃdriṇī caṃdragarbhā ca caṃdronmattā tathāṣṭamī |
etābhogāṃbuvāhinya svāṃśaiścaṃdrakalāśritāḥ || 7/130 ||
bhogāṃbuvāhinībhiśca tābhirnāḍībhirāvṛtā |
pradhānabhūtā vilasadvidyuddāma samaprabhā || 7/131 ||
ati nirbharakaṃdarpa dravameduritāṃtarā |
sarvanāḍīmūlabhūtaṃ nābhicakraṃ samāśritā || 7/132 ||
proktā caṃdrakalānāḍī sarvasaubhāgya vardhanī [kh: varttinī] |
tanmukhot ghāṭanaṃ loke durlabhaṃ parameśvari [kh: -rī] || 7/133 ||
pūrvajanmakṛtairnaikaistapobhiratidāruṇaiḥ [kh: pūrvajanmakṛter-] |
dvitrāṇāmeva jāyeta sahasreṣvathakauliṣu || 7/134 ||
tannāḍī galitānaṃga galadbiṃdukaṇāṇunā |
yasya liṃgesvarāseka sakṛdvāpīhajanmani [kh: sarvakṛdvā-] || 7/135 ||
tasya prāptaṃ maheśatvaṃ prāptānaṃdamayī sthitiḥ |
anirvācyasya nirvāṇa padavīnadaviyasī [kh: -viyyasī] || 7/136 ||
cirasaṃmardanādeva dhīrāṇāṃ vāyubaṃdhanāt |
samayasya parijñānādupacārasya kalpanāt || 7/137 ||
bhogāṃgairvakṣyamāṇaiśca sāvadhānaniṣevaṇāt |
kadācit kvacidunmeṣaṃ mukhodghāṭanalakṣaṇam || 7/138 ||
53k) dhattecaṃdrakalānāḍī dharaṇīdharanaṃdinī |
tadarthānyevaśāstrāṇi tadarthāśca pravṛttayaḥ || 7/139 ||
mokṣavādāstadarthāḥ [kh: -rthā] syuḥ kiṃ punastatkṛte'khilam |
tasmāccaṃdrakalānāḍyāṃ bhogāmṛtamahodadhim || 7/140 ||
hṛccakraraśmi sṛṇibhirvikṛṣyānīyapālayet [kh: -niyyapālayet] |
kuṃkumadravasaṃkāśaṃ vilīnāśeṣa ceṣṭitam || 7/141 ||
ānaṃdādvaita saubhāgya kūlaṃ kaṣamahodayam |
rakṣitaṃ bhāvayed devī tasyāścaṃdrakalodaye || 7/142 ||
evaṃ bhāvanadārḍhyena dūtikāyāḥ kalevare |
sadyaḥ pulakitākāro bhāvaḥ ko'pi vijṛṃbhate || 7/143 ||
nṛtyaṃtīdiṃdriyāṇisyurnṛtyatīva [kh: veṃdriyā-] manobhavaḥ [kh: -va] |
nṛtyatīvasamutkaṃṭhā nṛtyatī va śirāmukham [kh: śivā-] || 7/144 ||
evaṃ pratyayamālokya prahṛṣṭātmā ca sādhakaḥ |
dūtikā smara gehasthāṃ bhagamālāṃ samarcayet || 7/145 ||


śrīdevyuvāca


bhagamālā tu kā [kh: sā] proktā [kh: proktāṃ] tasyācakraṃ tu kīdṛśam |
tasyāścārcana bhedastu vaktavyaḥ parameśvara || 7/146 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ 


śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] devi mahābhāge sthāne pṛcchasi pārvatī |
bhagamālīti niḥśeṣa bhagacakrābhimāninī || 7/147 ||
devatā jagatāmāḍyā [kh: -mīḍyā] taruṇāditya pāṭalā |
viśvasaṃohanākārā viśvajanmamahāsthali [kh: -sthiti] || 7/148 ||
54k) dvitīyā [kh: dvitiyyā] tithirūpeṇa viśvavyāptiṃ prajagmuṣī |
kālarūpākālakarī kālagrasanalālasā || 7/149 ||
kālasaṃkarṣiṇī kālī saṃsārāvarttarūpiṇī |
samasteṣu śarīreṣu puṃsāṃstrīṇāmathāpivā || 7/150 ||
kṛtasānnidhya vibhavā paramānaṃdahetave |
saṃhita [kh: -tā] śatasāhasre [kh: -sri] yadarthe kīrtyate mayā || 7/151 ||
yasya maṃtraprabhāvaśca mayā vaktuṃ na śakyate |
sarvamaṃtropasaṃhāra khaṃḍemaṃtra samuddhṛtim || 7/152 || 7/
vakṣyamāṇe śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] śive dhyāna * syā [kh: dhyānamasyā] 
nibhodhavai |
nityānaṃga madonmattā yauvanenātigarvitā || 7/153 ||
uttuṃgapīna vakṣojaluṭhanmauktikabhūṣaṇā |
pāśāṃkuśau karaṃ dhanva tadā vā madhubhājanam || 7/154 ||
mātuluṃga phalaṃ devī cinmudrājapamālike |
dhārayaṃtī [kh: dārayaṃtīṃ] manojñābhirbhujābhiḥ śoṇakāṃtibhiḥ || 7/155 
||
siṃdūra biṃdurekhābhiralaṃkṛta śiroruhā |
prasannavadanāṃbhojā madaraktavilocanā || 7/156 ||
sakhīvilāsahāsādi parānirbharalālasā |
svaraktakāṃtiśaṭībhirevāvṛtanitaṃbabhūḥ [kh: [kh: -śāṭībhi-] || 7/157 ||
hārakeyūrakaṭakakāṃcīnūpurabhūṣitā |
digaṃbarābhiranyābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ parivāritā || 7/158 ||
trailokyamohinībhiśca taṭitpāṭalakāṃtibhiḥ |
55k) evaṃ dhyeyā maheśāni devatā bhagamālinī || 7/159 ||
tasyāścakraṃ pravakṣyāmi dūtikāyonimaṃḍale |
tripurārghyāṃbunā prokṣya dūtyāḥ [kh: dūtyā] smaraniketanam || 7/160 ||
tatra saṃkalpayeddhīmān bhagamālinikāsanam |
trikoṇaṃ paṃcakoṇaṃ ca sāṣṭārakacaturddalam || 7/161 ||
bhagamālā mahācakraṃ dhyātavyaṃ dūtikātanau |
madhye jayenmahādevīṃ mādyaṃtīṃ bhagamālinīm || 7/162 ||
tryaśrebhagabhugaṃbāṃ ca bhaginīṃ ca bhagodarīm |
bhagaguhyaṃ bhagayoniṃ tathā bhaganipātinīm || 7/163 ||
aṣṭāre tu yajeddevī sarvabhagavaśaṃkarīm [kh: -rim] |
bhagarūpāṃ tadā devīṃ [kh: devī] nityaklinnāmbikāmapi || 7/164 ||
bhagasvarūpāṃ varadāṃ retāṃ caiva suretayā |
bhagaklinnāmaṣṭamīṃ ca sādhakovidhivarttanā || 7/165 ||
catuṣṭaye tu yaṣṭavyā devī klinnadravābhidā |
amoghabhagavidyā ca caturthi [kh: -rtthi] tu bhageśvarī || 7/166 ||
evaṃ cakragatā devyo bhāvanīyā [kh: -niyyā] bhagāṃtare |
gaṃdhapuṣpa [kh: gaṃdhaṃ puṣpaṃ] tathā dhūpaṃ dīpannaivedyameva ca || 
7/167 ||
paṃcopacārān kurvita na vā ṣoḍaśabheditān |
tripurārghya sthitaireva biṃdubhistat prapūjayet || 7/168 ||
athavā svakṛte [kh: sakṛte] nārghya biṃdunātarpayecchivām |
pūrvokta [kh: -ktaṃ] yattu hṛccakraṃ dūtikāyāḥ prakīrttitam || 7/169 ||
56k) tatratyānāṃ ca rāśmīnāṃ tarpaṇaṃ tripurāmṛtaiḥ |
evaṃ yonigataṃ cakraṃ pūjayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ || 7/170 ||
pañcamudrā namaskāraṃ pravidhāya vidhānavat |
cakrapūjena śuddhena tripurārghyeṇa bhāsvatā [kh: bhā * *?] || 7/171 ||
yāgagehaṃ ca vastūni dūtikāṃcāpi śodhayet |
taccodhitena dravyeṇa pūritaṃ sa dvitīyakam || 7/172 ||
sadakṣiṇā [kh: -ṇa] sa tāṃbūlaṃ dūtikāyaisamarpayet |
dūtīhastagate [kh: -haste-] tasmin pātre pūritasīdhuni [kh: -nī] || 7/173 ||
jvalaṃtīṃ [kh: -tiṃ] bhavayeddevī [kh: bhā-] mataḥ kāmakalānnavām |
mukhaṃ biṃduṃ vidhāyātha biṃduyugmena tat kucau || 7/174 ||
saparārdhena [kh: -rthena] tadyoniṃ bhāvayet parameśvarī |
jvalatkāmakalākārāsyaṃdamānāmṛtadravaiḥ || 7/175 ||
miśritaṃ tatkaragataṃ pātrasthaṃ dravyamunnayet |
dhyātakāmakalāpūtayeta dravyaṃ pibaṃtitāḥ || 7/176 ||
dūtyaḥ kāmakalā tatve pāṭavaṃ bahuvidyate |
pradravaṃti ca mūrcchaṃti layaṃ yāṃti pade pade || 7/177 ||
tasmāt kāmakalāyuktaṃ dravyaṃdūtyai nivedayet |
dūtikājīvanāthasya yā sā saṃskṛtirāṃtarī || 7/178 || 
saiveyaṃ sarakābhikhyā tapānāt [kh: tatpanāt] saṃskṛtiṃ brajet |
ayamābhyaṃtaraḥ proktaḥ saṃskārojīvaśodhakaḥ || 7/179 ||
sūcanāmātrakamidaṃ vistareṇāthakathyate |

ityānaṃdataṃtre saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ samāptaḥ [kh: nāsti] ||




aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ

śrīdevyuvāca


bhagavanbhavapāśaghna [kh: bhagavān-] saṃskāromādirastvayā |
jīvanāthasya devasya sūcitonopapāditaḥ || 8/1 ||
kenamārgeṇa saṃskārastayā devasya śaṃkara |
kiṃkaroti ca dehasya praviśannaṃtaraṃ guṇam || 8/2 ||
etadbubhutsitaṃ sarvaṃ kathayasva mama prabho |


śrīkāmeśvaraḥ 


idaṃ pavitramamṛtaṃ bhavabheṣajamūciret [kh: -re] || 8/3 ||
pāśaghnaṃ sarvarogaghnaṃ durdrāridryanikṛṃtanam [kh: durdāridrya-] |
pratyakṣasaukhyajanakaṃ bahupratyayadāyakam || 8/4 ||
samastacittadoṣāṇāṃ samūlonmūlanakṣamam |
nānāvidhānāṃ vasudhājanmanāṃ puṇyavīrudhām || 8/5 ||
sāraiḥ saṃpādyamānena tena puṇyaṃ ca varddhate |
ṣaḍbhiśca [kh: ṣaḍhbhiśca] nānāvidhatāṃ prāptairasakadaṃbakaiḥ || 8/6 ||
aṃtargata mahāroga nivāraṇavicakṣaṇam |
kaṣāyaṃ khalurogārtaḥ pibatyauṣadhavīrudhām || 8/7 ||
pakvatā tena dehārtteḥ krameṇaiva vilīyate |
namoyasya tvanirvācyā śaktiḥ śailatanūbhavet || 8/8 ||
maṇīnāṃ caiva maṃtrāṇāṃ yathāśakti svabhāvajā |
tathauṣadhānāṃ ca śiveśaktiḥ pratyayakāriṇī || 8/9 ||
58k) prātasvikaṃ [kh: prātisvikaṃ-] pāṭavaṃ tu pratyauṣadhamudīryate |
tanuparyanuyojyatvā [kh: -tvaṃ] mātṛkasyāpi vādinaḥ || 8/10 ||
na hi svabhāva vastūnāṃ dhatte paryanuyojyatām |
atastathauṣadhānāṃ ca śaktirasyeva pārvati [kh: -tī] || 8/11 ||
ya yā rogāḥ praṇaśyaṃti [kh: - syaṃti] sādhanvānena sevanāt |
pratyauṣadhaṃ bhinnabhinnāḥ [kh: -bhinnā] śaktayastatra pārvati [kh: -tī] || 8/12 ||
dviyoge ca triyoge ca caturyogādikā kramāt |
anyonyāḥ śaktayonūnamāvisyaṃtyeva vīrudhām || 8/13 ||
svasyā śaktirlatānāṃ tu mūlānāmapi pārvati [kh: -tī] |
saṃkhyā na śakti prābalyāt tasyāmaṃśena [kh: tasyāmaṃ * *?] līyate [kh: * 
*?te] || 8/14 ||
auṣadhye dvividhā jñeyāḥ svataṃtrāḥ [kh: -trā?] sanyataṃtrajāḥ [kh: * 
*?taṃtrajāḥ] |
svataṃtrāstvekayā śaktyā nikṛtaṃti mahāgadān || 8/15 ||
sargataṃtra [kh: -taṃtrā] auṣadhayaḥ śaktimelanasaṃpadām |
rogāṇyavaśyaṃ nighnaṃti svabhāvo vāsya vastunaḥ [kh: -na] || 8/16 ||
mayādakṣādhvaradhavaṃse [kh: -dakṣāddhvaradhavaṃ *] kriyamāṇe purā priye 
|
krodhāṃdhāṃ niṭilakrodhalocanāt [kh: niṭala-] krodhapāvakaḥ [kh: kopa-] || 8/17 
||
jvālāmālā kulojajñe vīrabhadrā kṛtiṃ vahan |
sa vīrabhadraḥ suśroṇi sākṣāt saṃhāra bhairavaḥ || 8/18 ||
kālāgniḥ kālanāthaśca mṛtyuścāpi maheśvarī |
dakṣakopātprajvalataḥ [kh: -tas] tasyāṃgādromakūpataḥ || 8/19 ||
59k) babhūve sakalavyādhimalloyakṣmā mahāmayaḥ |
tamanuproddhitāḥ sarve vyādhayovividhakramāḥ || 8/20 ||
atyaṃtatarṣataralā atyaṃtaśca bhubhukṣitāḥ |
atyaṃtalolādurvārāḥ [kh: -dāḥ] prajvalaṃtaḥ sadāmuṣā [kh: -juṣā] || 8/21 ||
anekavidhatā prāptāḥ [kh: prāptā] mahārogaparaṃparāḥ |
tamāśritya mahāsatvaṃ vīrabhadraṃ mahodayam || 8/22 ||
nipīḍayaṃti durvṛktānuddhatān [kh: durvṛttā -] samayadruhaḥ |
pāpaśīlān durācārān kanyāvidveṣakāriṇaḥ || 8/23 ||
dārasaṃtyāginodharma tyāginoguruniṃdakān |
āgama [kh: -maḥ] drohiṇo pāpān vṛthākalahakāriṇaḥ [kh: vṛdhā-] || 8/24 ||
sadāpareṣu roṣāṃdhāṃ nidrālasya kṛtādarān |
bhakṣayaṃti mahārogāḥ pibaṃti rudhiraṃ bahu || 8/25 ||
asatyavādinaḥ [kh: -na] stabdhā nabhimānena connatān |
vṛthāceṣṭān [kh: vṛdhā-] vṛthācārān [kh: vṛdhā-] vṛthevamadapīḍitān [kh: 
vṛdhe-] || 8/26 ||
mithyāhaṃkāraniratā na jñāna paramānasān |
marmabheda karālāpāṃ dhārmikācāra niṃdakān || 8/27 ||
strīdviṣṭān guruvidviṣṭān [kh: -vidveṣṭān] lokavidviṣṭamārgakān [kh: -kāḥ] |
hiṃsaṃti vyādhayastīvrāstatsaṃgādaparānapi [kh: -saṃgodaparānapi] || 8/28 ||
piḍayaṃti janān sādhūnitimatvāmarudgaṇaiḥ |
āśaṃkamānairvilaya mahaṃsaṃprārthitaḥ [kh: -prārddhitaḥ] purā || 8/29 ||
60k) karasthita kapālānna pūritaṃ paramāmṛtam |
parabrahmaḥ sāsvādamayamānaṃdadāyakam [kh: rasā-] || 8/30 ||
kiṃcit prāpyāmṛtaṃ bhaumau biṃdumekaṃ maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
sa biṃdu śatasāhasra pravahaḥ pavanāhatam || 8/31 ||
itastataḥ sudhādroṇīrvikirannūrjjitadyutiḥ |
viveśacauṣadhiḥ [kh: -coṣadhiḥ] sarvāḥ somarūpomahārasaḥ || 8/32 ||
tasmādoṣadhinātho'yaṃ biṃdubhaṭṭārakaḥ śive |
tataḥ prabhṛti vīrutsujāgartti paramāmṛtam || 8/33 ||
tanmūlikṛtyarogārthe vaidyaṃ taṃtraṃ mayākṛtam |
vividhaṃ vaidyataṃtraṃ tu jvarādīnāṃ vinuttaye || 8/34 ||
śārīravaidyake [kh: -kaṃ] jñeyaṃ jīvavaidyakamanyakam |
śārīravaidyake proktāḥ prakvāthaghuṭikādayaḥ [kh: prakvādha-] || 8/35 ||
jīvavaidyakataṃtroktā vividhāṃ madhuśaktayaḥ |
yathā dehojvaraṃ śītaṃ pittaṃ śleṣmamalonilaḥ || 8/36 ||
tatsaṃghātādvibhedena rogānānāvidhāḥ [kh: -dhā] smṛtāḥ |
tathā jīve gadāḥ proktāḥ krodhodveṣaścamatsaraḥ || 8/37 ||
ālasyāni ca nidrāśca dainyaṃ kāryeṣvanudyamaḥ |
nirvedaśca paritrāsaḥ śaṃkākutsitaciṃtanam || 8/38 ||
cāpalāṃ [kh: -lā] duḥkhabhūyastvaṃ vṛthātarko [kh: vṛdhā-] vṛthābhramaḥ 
[kh: vṛdhā-] |
vikaṃpatvaṃ vimūḍhatvaṃ vidyāloka navarjitam [kh: -va * *?] || 8/39 ||
61k) avidyātimirāṃdhatvaṃ saṃkocaḥ sarvakarmaṇi [kh: -ṇī] |
sarvasyakutsā śīlatvaṃ sarvatrāpi jugupsanam || 8/40 ||
aprāmāṇikamārgeṣu dhāvanaṃ dharmamūḍhatā |
etā dṛśānaikabhedāḥ koṭīnāmapikoṭayaḥ || 8/41 ||
tattaddurvāsanādārnān [kh: -nādāḍhyān] mṛdumadhyograśaktayaḥ |
avidyā parirabdhasya jīvasyaite mahāgadāḥ || 8/42 ||
nāśayaṃti durutsāhā ete svātmānamavyayam |
pātayaṃti ca dṛṣṭeṣu duḥkhasaṃkramavarttmasu || 8/43 ||
eteṣāṃ tvapi rogāṇāṃ samastānāṃ nikṛṃtane |
pāṭavopi vidhāvastu śaktayo yatra bhāṣitāḥ || 8/44 ||
tajjīva vaidyakaṃ jñeyaṃ svātmasaṃrakṣaṇakṣamam |
tasmādanenadravyeṇa nānauṣadhirasātmanā || 8/45 ||
dūtikājīvanāthasya śāṃkaraṃ kriyate priye |


śrīdevyuvāca


yathā jīvaśarīrasya [kh: -rastha] jīvasaṃskāra āṃtaraḥ || 8/46 ||
tatkīrttitānimān doṣān sahasaiva nikṛṃtati |
tathā dūti śarīrasthā jīvadeva samāśrayā || 8/47 ||
kevārogāni haṃtavyā śastenānena vastunā |


kāmeśvaraḥ 


svabhāvato manasttrīṇāṃ vrīḍājaḍimabhājanam || 8/48 ||
atipeśalamatyartha bhaṃguraṃ bīrucāṃbike |
caṃcalaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ caiva kuṭilaṃ ca svabhāvataḥ || 8/49 ||
62k) aṃtargūḍhābhimānaṃ ca stabdhaṃ satyapi manmathe |
atilolamanastrīṇāṃ sulabhaglāni ca svataḥ || 8/50 ||
ityādikāśca bahavaḥ [kh: -va] strīṣudoṣāmanogatāḥ |
naśyaṃti vastuśaktīnāṃ manuṣe vaṇadhārayā || 8/51 ||
samastaśṛṃgāra kalārodhinī dṛḍhamatrapā |
argalākāmasarvasva dvārasyāśuvilīyate [kh: -viliyyate] || 8/52 ||
sahasāpitrapātasya vastuśakteḥ prabhāvataḥ |
abhogasamaye bhūya [kh: bhūyaḥ] prāptuṃ kutrāpi līyate [kh: liyyate] || 8/53 ||
aṣṭapāśa pradhāneṣu [kh: -tu] vrīḍātaṃtre niyaṃtrite |
ānaṃdanātha pūjārthaṃ parivārāṃ ganā iva || 8/54 ||
śṛṃgāra ceṣṭā vividhā jṛṃbhate [kh: jṛṃbhaṃte] dūtikā tanau |
sakṛto dṛṣṭisaṃcārasmitamullāsamāṃsalam || 8/55 ||
prasādaśca mukhāṃbhoje kiṃcidāraktatādṛśoḥ |
līlāvaśena dehasya ghūrṇanaṃ cāṭubhāṣaṇam || 8/56 ||
avyaktamadhurālāpāskhaladvarṇaḥ pade pade |
ākūtasya samastasya svecchayaiva prakāśanam || 8/57 ||
rahasya rakṣaṇadvārā rahasyasya prakāśanam |
parīhāsakathālāpaḥ kiṃcidāviṣkṛtātrapā || 8/58 ||
kiṃcidā virbhavemāne kiṃcitkopasamudbhavaḥ |
kilikiṃcita saṃcāraḥ krīḍāprāyaviceṣṭitaḥ || 8/59 ||
63k) aṃtasthānāmakṛtyānāṃ samuttaṃbhanapāṭavam |
pratyaṃgapulakodbhedaḥ pratyaṃgasvedabindavaḥ || 8/60 ||
pratyaṃgamabhijātasya dhāmnaḥ kasyāpi bhāsanam |
etā dṛśāguṇādūtyāmudbhavaṃti kulāmṛtaiḥ || 8/61 ||
yathā tasya samullāsaṃ dūtikāyā maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
athāsvātmani vinyāsamānaṃdasyāpi kalpayet || 8/62 ||
yathā na cittacāpalyaṃ yathānaṃdamayī sthitiḥ |
yathā cinmātra rūpatvaṃ tathā vastu prakalpayet [kh: * * *?yet] || 8/63 ||
idamarghyaṃ mahatpuṇyaṃ kuṇḍalīmūlaśāyinaḥ |
jīvarūpasya mahatā cidvahneḥ [kh: -vahnoḥ] prajvalattanoḥ || 8/64 ||
* ścyotā [kh:  * * tā] jvalacidvahniḥ sarpiṣeva hutāśanaḥ |
tasmātpuṇyamidaṃ sevyaṃ madhurūpaṃ rasāyanam || 8/65 ||
itthaṃ citte parāmṛśya parāṃbāstanya pānavat |
parāmṛtasyāhutivat śīlayet sarakakramam || 8/66 ||
āraṃbhastaraṇaścaiva yauvanaṃ prauḍha eva ca |
ānaṃdataṃtre caivāsmin catvāraḥ paramīritāḥ [kh: -taḥ] || 8/67 ||
prauḍhakeva smṛto [kh: smṛte] devī liṃganāthasya pūjanā |
tasmāccatvāra ullāsā iha proktā maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 8/68 ||
yogibhiḥ [kh: -bhi] svasyasāmarthyaṃ [kh: svasva-] gaveṣyaṃte dṛḍhavrataiḥ [kh: -
vṛtaiḥ] |
tasmādānaṃda pūjāyā mālaṃvyaṃte tu na trayaḥ || 8/69 ||
64k) yathā yathā samullāsaṃ dhatte vastuniṣevaṇam |
tathā tathā parānaṃda nāthavidyāṃ parāmṛśet || 8/70 ||
maṃtrapūtena sāreṇa śodhitāśaya doṣayā |
dūtyā saṃdhukṣita svātmā naṃdamūrttiṃ guruṃ smaret || 8/71 ||
vastūllāsa śriyāyuktaṃ cittamatyaṃtapeśalam |
dvādaśāṃtagate raktaśuklapāde vinikṣipet  || 8/72 ||
tatkālagalitairnnāthacaraṇāṃburuhāmṛtaiḥ |
śivatvasāmrājya padābhiṣiktaṃ svaṃviciṃtayet || 8/73 ||
atonmīlitanetrastu dūtikāmujvaladyutim |
paśyan pratyaṃgamaṃkūradanaṃ gottuṃgamānasaḥ || 8/74 ||
vibhāvayet parānaṃda devīmānaṃda rūpiṇīm |
yathā yauvanamullāsaṃ saṃprāpnotyaṃba dūtikā || 8/75 ||
tathā sarveṣu gātreṣu tathātīrthāni sarvadā |
ālokaneratiḥ sākṣāt smite jāgarti jāhnavī [kh: jāhvavī] || 8/76 ||
ālepevāgbhagavatī [kh: ālāpe] tat sparśe dharmadevatā |
aśvinau nāsikābhāge tvoṣṭhe ca paramāmṛtam || 8/77 ||
daṃteṣu caṃdrakiraṇāḥ pulakebhūtirujvalā |
svede ca vāruṇī devī kāmanāthastvahaṃ kṛtau || 8/78 ||
vasaṃtanāthaḥ keśeṣu kaṃṭhe saṃgītayoginī |
stanadvaye merugirirnniḥ śabdaṃ brahmamadhyame || 8/79 ||
65k) anaṃto bhagavān bhātiromarāji pade punaḥ |
valitraye ca trailokyaṃ nābhigarbhe guhāṃbikā || 8/80 ||
karāṃgulīṣu [kh: -liṣu] kāmasya paṃcasāyaka devatāḥ |
nakheṣu śītāṃ śu kalādormmūleṃkuśadevatāḥ || 8/81 ||
pāśaśaktirbāhuyuge phāle śṛṃgāra devatā |
kāverī caiva niśvāse godāsītkāra karmaṇi || 8/82 ||
nādabrahmamahollāsaṃ jāgartimaṇitadhvanau [kh: jāgartī-] |
amalālāpakṛtyeṣu cittaviśrāṃti nāyakā || 8/83 ||
utsāhe bhairavī sākṣādullāse tripurāṃbikā |
vākptiḥ sukhahuṃkāre gīṣpatīkākukādhvanau || 8/84 ||
tasmādvilokanād devī varttate manaspatiḥ |
iṃdraścittasamunnatyāmagnirvastu [kh: -tyā-agnir-] grasītviṣi || 8/85 ||
ānṃdamūrcchā velāyāṃ kālikā parameśvarī |
hastādicālane yajñastvāśleṣe yajñapūruṣaḥ || 8/86 ||
viśvedevāścuṃbane tu tāḍane vasavaḥ [kh: -va] smṛtāḥ |
lāsyaṃ ca tāṃḍavaṃ devi tasyā dehādi ghūrṇane || 8/87 ||
tisraḥkoṭyardhakoṭīśca [kh: -rthakoṭīṃ ca] tīrthānibhuvanatraye |
tānisarvāṇi varttate [kh: varttaṃte] tasyāgaṃḍūṣavastuni || 8/88 ||
anekarājasūyānāṃ somapānena yatphalam |
tatphalaṃ labhate dūtī sudhāgaṃḍūṣa sevanāt [kh: seva * ?] || 8/89 ||
66k) priyayā yanmudāmattamānaṃda madalolayā |
gaṃḍūṣamādiraṃ [kh: gaṃḍūṣaṃ-] devī vīrasyānaṃda varddhanam || 8/90 ||
ānaṃdanāthaścānaṃda bhairavībhagamālinī |
nityaklinnāsudhā devī rati prītiśca tadbhage || 8/91 ||
iththaṃ dūti vapuḥsubhru devatā tīrtharūpadhṛk |
ānaṃdadeva pūjārthaṃ yogyatvaṃ pratipadyate || 8/92 ||
iti devī tava proktā jīvasyāṃtara saṃskṛtiḥ |
ārādhanakramaṃ cāśu liṃgeśasya bravīmite || 8/93 ||
yena [kh: ena] vijñātamātreṇa kṛtakarttavyatāṃ brajet |

ityānaṃdataṃtre aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ samāptaḥ [kh: nāsti] ||




navamaḥ paṭalaḥ

athānaṃda mayecakre cittamākramyatiṣṭhati |
cakrasthāṃ lalitā [kh: -tāṃ] devīmātmanyud vāsayet priye || 9/1 ||
nidhāya devīṃ hṛdaye sahasradalapaṃkaje |
uparyadhomukhāṃbhoja [kh: -thomukhāṃ-] saṃpuṭīkṛta maṃṭape || 9/2 ||
cidratnadīpakālika [kh: -kalika] nirastatimiracchade |
parāmṛtadhārābhirddhārāyaṃtramanohare [kh: paramāmṛtadhārā-] || 9/3 ||
sākaṃkāmeśvareṇaiva sadāsaṃbhogaśālinā |
praveśayennijagṛhaṃ prakṛṣṭānaṃdanirbharaḥ || 9/4 ||
svayaṃ tasyāḥ prabhājālaiḥ sarvato rūṣitākṛtiḥ [kh: rūpitā-] |
tasyā evānaṃda kalāmanusaṃdadhadāśraye || 9/5 ||
tasya [kh: tasya] eva parāṃ vyāptiṃ ciṃtayanviśadāśayaḥ |
69k) kṛtamudrā namaskāro yāgarājagṛhaṃ viśet || 9/6 ||
praskhalat padavinyāsāṃ ghūrṇamānāṃ madaśriyā |
kādaṃbarī rasāṃbhoja karaṃbitamukhāṃbujām || 9/7 ||
dūtikāṃ śayanasthāna mānīyamadanonmadaḥ [kh: māniyya-] |
śayyāyāstu caturdikṣu yajeddevī catuṣṭayam || 9/8 ||
madanāmadanonmattā madirākṣīmadālasā |
prathamāpītavarṇā syāt dvitīyā [kh: dvitiyyā] śyāmalākṛtiḥ || 9/9 ||
tṛtīyā paṭalājñeyā mahāsvetā caturthikā |
ānaṃdanāthadevasya catasraḥ paricārikāḥ || 9/10 ||
caturvargaphalaprāpti hetu bhūtāmanīṣiṇām |
tāsāṃ maṃtreṇa naijena pūjanaṃ samudīritam || 9/11 ||
ādaunavātmamithunaṃ paścāt tannāmakīrtyate |
pādukāṃ pūjayāmīti saptākṣaryā mahārhayā || 9/12 ||
śrīpūrvayā yojayecca catvāromanavastvimāḥ |
etairmaṃtraiḥ prahṛṣṭātmā gaṃdhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ || 9/13 ||
arcayedathatanmadhye yejecchṛṃgāra pīṭhikām |
śṛṃgārapīṭhikāmaṃtro samūhyaḥ [k: * pyūhyaḥ] pūrvavatpriye || 9/14 ||
nāmamaṃtrasya sarvasyāpyayamevakramaḥ [kh: -ma] smṛtaḥ |
bījamādau tatoṣṭārṇaṃ madhye nāma prakalpanam || 9/15 ||
evamevakrameṇāṃba nāmamaṃtrāḥ prakīrttitāḥ |
sarvatracakrapūjāsu viniyojyā hi bhūtaye || 9/16 ||
68k) śṛṃgāra pīṭhīmabhyarcya [kh: pīṭhi-] tanmadhyedīptavigraham |
paṃcakāmamahāyaṃtraṃ bhāvayitvārccayennaraḥ || 9/17 ||
māyāṃ prathamamālikhya kāmarājena viṣṭayet |
tataḥ ṣaṭkoṇamālikhya blūṣaṭkaṃ tatra [kh: * *?] saṃlikhet [kh: *?likhet] || 9/18 ||
strībījena [kh: strīṃ-] samāveṣṭya [kh: -veṣṭyā] yaṃtrametat samuddharet |
evaṃ viciṃtya tadyaṃtraṃ [kh: -tra] niryaṃtraṇa sukhāspadam || 9/19 ||
āroheddūtikāyukto hṛṣṭaḥ śṛṃgārapīṭhikām [kh: śriṃ-] |
kāmayaṃtroparivased dūtikā yadi pārvatī || 9/20 ||
tadā tayāvapuḥ sarvaṃ smararāgamayaṃ bhavet |
asaṃkhyātā sunāḍīṣu ye cānaṃdāḥ prakīrttitāḥ || 9/21 ||
te sarve vivṛtākārā bhavaṃti svātmatṛptaye |
kaṃdarpa yaṃtra vedena jāyate divyabhoginī || 9/22 ||
sarvakāmakalājāta [kh: -tā] rahasyajñā bhavet priyā |
iththaṃ tayā samāsādya gātrasaṃgamahotsavam || 9/23 ||
yāgarājaṃ vidhitsuścatadvighnasyopa śāṃtaye |
śayyāyā uttare bhāge sthāpayitvā sadāmṛtam || 9/24 ||
nūtnavastrasamāyuktaṃ mukhepallavaśobhitam |
pūrṇakuṃbhaṃ tasya mukhe paṭmamekaṃ [kh: paṭna-] viciṃtayet || 9/25 ||
tat paṭmakarṇikāmadhye [kh: paṭna-] yajecchakti gajānanam |
tasyadhyānaṃ pravakṣyāmi sāvadhānā śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] priyai [kh: priye] || 
9/26 ||
69k) udyadādityasaṃkāśo madaklinnakaṭasthalaḥ |
sadāmadanamattātmā pāśāṃkuśalasatkaraḥ || 9/27 ||
pānapātrākṣamāle ca vahannanyakaradvaye |
aṃkasthitāyā mattāyāḥ siddhilakṣmībhagāṃtare || 9/28 ||
saṃpraveśita tuṇḍāgra praṇālī pītatadrasaḥ |
tasyāśca karapadmena [kh: -paṭnena] navapallavacāruṇā || 9/29 ||
sa kaṃkaṇaninādena gṛhyamāṇonnatadhvajaḥ |
siddhilakṣmī bhagasrota śīdhupānamadālasaḥ [kh: śīthu-] || 9/30 ||
araktanayanopāṃtaḥ sadāsaṃbhogarūpadhṛk |
siddhilakṣmīrati sveta [kh: svedaṃ] vinudankarṇatālanaiḥ || 9/31 ||
īdṛśaḥ śaktigaṇapodhyātavyo vighnaśāṃtaye |
siddhilakṣmī patedeva śaktiherambavigraha [kh: -hā] || 9/32 ||
atrānaṃda mahāyāge niṣpratyūhaṃ samācara |
ityanenaiva maṃtreṇa kṛtapuṣpāṃjali kriyaḥ || 9/33 ||
ānṃdakārikāpāṭhaṃ kurvanneva svavigrahe |
dūtyāśca vigrahe puṣpamekamevaṃ [kh: -mekaṃ] vinikṣipet || 9/34 ||
ya ānaṃdomanuṣyāṇāṃ mānuṣīṇāṃ ca varttate |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/35 ||
gandharvāṇāṃ ya ānaṃda tathaivāpsarasāmapi |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/36 ||
70k) pitṛṇāṃ [kh: pitṝṇāṃ] pitṛpatnīnāṃ ya ānaṃda maheśvaraḥ |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/37 ||
devānāṃ deva patnīnāṃ ya ānaṃdamaheśvaraḥ |
vasūnāṃ vasupatnīnāṃ ya ānaṃdamaheśvaraḥ || 9/38 ||
dānavānāṃ ca daityānāṃ tattacchaktiṣu ya [kh: yat] sthitaḥ |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/39 ||
iṃdrādilokapālānāṃ yacchacyādiṣu śaktiṣu |
ātmasvarūpasaṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/40 ||
saptarṣīṇāṃ marīcyatrimukhānāṃ yaḥ svaśaktiṣu [kh: * * ?ktiṣu] |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/41 ||
yo brahmaṇaḥ sarasvatyāṃ sāvitryāmapi bhāsate |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/42 ||
yo viṣṇau kamalāyāṃ ca bhūdevyāmapi varttate |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/43 ||
umāpatāvumāyāṃ ca yaścetasi kṛta sthitiḥ |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/44 ||
kāmeśvareya ānaṃdaḥ kāmeśvaryāṃ vyavasthitaḥ |
ātmasvarūpa saṃsiddhyaitamānaṃdamihānaye || 9/45 ||
annena jāyate sarvamannenaiva vivarddhate |
tasmādannamayaṃ kośaṃ ānaṃdamayatāṃ nayet || 9/46 ||
71k) prāṇā jīvātavaḥ proktāḥ prāṇaiḥ sarvo'pi ceṣṭate [kh: veṣṭate] |
tasmāt prāṇamayaṃ krośamānaṃdamayatāṃ nayet || 9/47 ||
manaspṛśati karmāṇi mana eva vṛṣāpatiḥ |
tasmānmanomayaṃ kośamānaṃdamayatāṃ nayet || 9/48 ||
vijñānarūpamakhilaṃ vijñāne ca pratiṣṭhitam |
tadvijñānamayaṃ kośamanaṃdamayatāṃ nayet [kh: naye] || 9/49 ||
ānaṃdojīvitaṃ sākṣādānaṃdaḥ parameśvaraḥ |
tadānaṃdamayaṃ kośaṃ varddhatāṃ varddhatāṃ mama || 9/50 ||
ānaṃdakārikāpāṭhamiti kurvanvicakṣaṇaḥ |
yauvanollāsamakhilaṃ yāgakarmaśanairnayet || 9/51 ||
ratitaṃtre mayāproktāśceṣṭāḥ śṛṃgāra yonayaḥ |
tatsarvamihasuśroṇi sayāyāṃtyaṃgabhāvanām [kh: samā-] || 9/52 ||
bhinna bhinna prakāreṇa kriyābakhya smarāgame |
tāsāmaṃsaṃkhyān kovaktuṃ prakāran bhuvanekṣamaḥ || 9/53 ||
tatsarvaṃ ca kriyājālaṃ dūtikāyāṃ samācaret |
taiḥsarvairvyaktimāpnoti svātmānaṃdamaheśvaraḥ || 9/54 ||
āliṃganairbahuvidhaiścuṃbanairdaṃtaghāṭanaiḥ |
nakhasaṃlekhanaiścitraiḥ kṣobhaṇairanuraṃjanaiḥ || 9/55 ||
cāṭūpakalpitairlīlā kopaiḥ priyakathā kramaiḥ |
karṇe curucurānaṃdairlocanasya pracuṃbanaiḥ || 9/56 ||
72k) capeṭapatanairnnābhau nakhamārgairnakhāṃkuraiḥ [kh: nakhamārger-] |
stana saṃmardanairbāhu mūlaprotkṣepaṇairapi || 9/57 ||
tālulālikayākarṇe tālukikkiṭinasvanaiḥ [kh: -nīsvanaiḥ] |
kākakāraṇḍavādīnāṃ sārasānāṃ ca pakṣiṇām || 9/58 ||
haṃsānāṃ mallikākṣāṇāṃ kapotānāṃ ca pārvati [kh: -tī] |
nānāvidhaiḥ kalairnādaiḥ kaṃṭhagolavinirgataiḥ || 9/59 ||
uparyadhastādbhramaṇairupariṣṭācca pātanaiḥ |
kacagrahaiḥ karāghātaiḥ [kh: -tar] jihvāyuddhaiśca bhūribhiḥ || 9/60 ||
protsāhanaiḥ priyakaraiḥ [kh: -rair] vākyaiḥ premaparigrahaiḥ |
rati pāṭava saubhāgya ślāghābhiśca pade pade || 9/61 ||
hasitairbakasallāpairanyairapi viceṣṭitaiḥ |
nānāvidhaprakāreṇa dūtikāmanuraṃjayet || 9/62 ||
yajñe yathā prayājānūyājādyāḥ [kh: -dyā] aṃgatāṃ gatāḥ |
tadvadānaṃda yāge'smin āśleṣādyaṃ gamiṣyate || 9/63 ||
dūtyāḥ kucadvayīyūpaḥ keśāḥ prastaratāṃ gatāḥ |
nakhakṣata jvalannagniścuṃbanaṃ somapānavat [kh: ?]  || 9/64 ||
sruvopabhṛdbāhuyugmaṃ [kh: sṛvo-] juhūrdormmūlamucyate [kh: jahūrdormmū 
* * ?te] |
pariṣeka [kh: -kaḥ] svedajalaṃ bhajaveṣṭiḥ paristṛtiḥ || 9/65 ||
paridhānaṃ parispaṃdo yajñavedi ca madhyabhūḥ |
nābhīkuṇḍaṃ ca cātvālaṃ gārhapatyo mukhaṃ smṛtam || 9/66 ||
73k) dakṣiṇāgniśca cubukaṃ ca masaḥ karapallavam |
sabhyāvasadhyau tasyāśca pārśvadvitaya [kh: pārśa-] saṃsthitau || 9/67 ||
sākṣādāhavanīyaṃ [kh: -vaniyyaṃ] syādārdraṃ madanamaṃdiram |
samidhoghaṭṭanā [kh: samito-] proktā hotāliṃga maheśvaraḥ || 9/68 ||
yajamānaḥ parānaṃdaḥ [kh: -naṃda] udgātāmaṇitadhvaniḥ |
adhvaryurathasītkāro neṣṭāmadhurabhāṣaṇam || 9/69 ||
prastotāsuratāraṃbhaḥ pratiharttārasodayaḥ |
maitrāvaruṇa utsāhaḥ potāhuṃkāra ucyate || 9/70 ||
cālana brāhmaṇācchaṃsi brahmāvibhramaceṣṭitam |
ucchāvākaḥ kākurutaṃ śuklasyaṃdomahāhaviḥ || 9/71 ||
iṃdriyāṇi sadasyāḥ [kh: -syā] syurmahāyāgo'yamucyate |
ānaṃda svarūpatva paśuṃhatvā maheśvarī || 9/72 ||
tatpuroḍāśa bhavanānmahāyāgaḥ pravarttate |


śrīdevyuvāca 


nakhakṣatairdaṃtapātairāghātairapi śaṃkara || 9/73 ||
kṣobhaṇaiḥ pātanaiścāpi tīvraiḥ kiṃdeva sādhyate |
prayojanaṃ vadaiteṣāmanyathā [kh: -dhā] niṣphalāḥ kriyāḥ || 9/74 ||
dūtikāyāḥ [kh: -yā] stanauvīrairyadarthaṃ tadvidhīyate [kh: -dhiyyate] |
tāḍana pramukhaṃ karmataṃ hetuṃ kathaya prabho || 9/75 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ 


sādhu tvayāpṛṣṭhamidaṃ [kh: -pṛṣṭamidaṃ] svabhāvonmadanonmadāḥ |
mohaṃ kṣaṇe kṣaṇedūtyā bhajaṃte narmakarmaṇi || 9/76 ||
74k) mohamugdhāna paṭavobhāgaceṣṭā [kh: -bhogaceṣṭā] kadaṃbake |
tasmādalasatāṃ prāptā na dravaṃtyeva dūtikāḥ || 9/77 ||
stimitatvamanuprāptastāsāṃ cetasimanmathaḥ |
vilaṃbate ratiṃ dātuṃ tadvilaṃbāt sukhaṃkutaḥ [kh: sukaṃkutaḥ] || 9/78 ||
tasyānāvirbhavanato vighnaḥ [kh: vighna] syāt suratādhvare |
tāḥ prabuddhāḥ samutsāha mārabhaṃte ratādhvare || 9/79 ||
vīrairbahu vidhairitthaṃ [kh: -riththaṃ]  karmabhiścopalālitāḥ |
dūtyodravati bahuśonavanītamivāgninā || 9/80 ||
yuktayaṃtraṃ prakurvīta sarvametatkriyākulam |
layamarthalayaṃ cāpi tadā ghaṭṭalayaṃ priye || 9/81 ||
kṣobhaṇaṃ ghūrṇanaṃ caiva liṃgena kramaśo bhavet |
acarandūtikāgātramānaṃdamayatāṃ nayet || 9/82 ||
upacārairbahuvidhaistāmeva drāvayansudhīḥ |
svayamatyaṃta dhīrātmā bhāvayan liṃgakuṃcikām || 9/83 ||
taralasya rasasyāṃtarnniruṃdhāno vinirgatim |
nityānaṃdamayaṃ tatvaṃ jagadvyāptaṃ viciṃtayat [kh: -yan] || 9/84 ||
iyamevaguroḥ pūjā śivārccanamidaṃ smṛtam |
ayameva mahāyajñastapaścedamanuttamam || 9/85 ||
atratyena pramodena toṣamāyāṃ tu devatāḥ |
idaṃ vrataṃ mahāpuṇyamānaṃdādvaitadāyakaṃ || 9/86 ||
75k) iti vīromahāyāgaṃ kramaśaḥ saṃprasārayan |
yathā na cyavate biṃduryathā hṛṣṭaṃ [kh: dṛṣṭaṃ] ca mānasam [kh: mā * *?] || 
9/87 ||
yathā dravati sā dūtī tathā kṛtyaṃ samācaret |
biṃduvikṣaraṇaṃ nāma yajñāvabhṛtha [kh: -bhṛda] ucyate || 9/88 ||
pradhānāhuti rapyeṣā na madhye karttumarhate |
yathā yathā kāladairghyaṃ dhairyabaṃdhena bhūyasā || 9/89 ||
tathā kuryān mahāyāgamānaṃda paripūrtaye |

ityānaṃdataṃtre navamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ 
śrīdevyuvāca


kathaṃ na kṣarate biṃduryonisaṃge maheśvara |
