agnirmukhe pāradavat na dhairyamanudhāvati || 10/1 ||
atyaṃta taralaṃ svacchamati caṃcalaveṣṭitam [kh: -vīṣṭitam] |
kathaṃ bhavati caṇḍīśa prastabdhaṃ śuklamaṃgake || 10/2 ||
atropāyaḥ [kh: pāyaṃ] pravaktavyo bhavatābhavabhaṃjana [kh: -nam] |
atyaṃtāścaryajanakaṃ [kh: aṃtyaṃtā -] yadiśuklasya dhāraṇā || 10/3 ||
vāyoḥ saṃdhāraṇaṃ yuktaṃ yuktamākāśakhaṇḍanam |
natukenāpi yatnena jāyate śukladhāraṇā || 10/4 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ 


pṛṣṭhametattvayeśāni [kh: pṛṣṭa-] sthāne tat pravivicyate |
kāmataṃtreṣu bahuśobhogibhiryadanuṣṭhitam || 10/5 ||
yadatyāścaryajanakaṃ triṣulokeṣu durlabham |
tadvadāmi mahābhāge staṃbhanaṃ rasasaṃpadām || 10/6 ||
staṃbhatye sakalaḥ kālastanyate ca vayonaraiḥ |
naṃdavyate kathamapi prauḍhamekorasobudhaiḥ || 10/7 ||
76k) atyaṃta capalotyaṃta vegidhāturasaṃ priye |
sarvepi dhātavodevī svasvasthāne kṛtāśrayāḥ || 10/8 ||
asya saptamadhātostu vā sonajñāyate kvacit | [k: nāsti]
rasanādoyamuddāmassarvavyāpi sanātanaḥ || 10/9 || 10/[k: nāsti]
abhivyakto'yamāhārairnāhāreṇa tu janyate |
nāḍīcakreṣu sarveṣu vasannapi raseśvaraḥ || 10/10 ||
asakta eva sarvatra svātaṃtreṇaiva khelati |
kecidvadaṃti bhrūmadhye keciddhṛdiparemaṇau [kh: kecidhṛdi-] || 10/11 ||
kecit svāvāsasīmāyāṃ kecidāhāragolake |
decidūrvostaledevi [kh: -vī] decijjaghanasīmani || 10/12 ||
kecit kaṭyāṃ sthitiṃ prāhuḥ kecilliṃgamaheśvare |
anye vadaṃti manasi paremāruta ūcire || 10/13 ||
paṃceṃdriyeṣvapipare vividhāstarka vibhramāḥ |
matānyetānyacāruṇi vicāre saṃpravarttite || 10/14 ||
ākarṇayamataṃ tatvaṃ [kh: tatva] mayākathitamaṃbike |
manaso marutaścaiva saṃghaṭṭe vīryasaṃśrayā || 10/15 ||
hṛnmarudghaṭanaṃ [kh: hṛnmarut-] kāryaṃ vīryadhāraṇamicchatā |
tayostu ghaṭanārthaṃ ca yogataṃtraṃ [kh: -tra] mayākṛtam || 10/16 ||
nigṛhītuṃ dhārayituṃ vīryaṃ vyāpayituṃ tanau |
rajasā vāyojayituṃ dhīmarudghaṭanaṃ prabhu || 10/17 ||
vahavaḥ kīrttitāyadyapyupāyā vīryarakṣaṇī |
taṣāmaṃto na cāsyevataiḥ phalaṃ na hi saṃbhavet || 10/18 ||
77k) liṃgalepaiśca vividhairvividhairasabaṃdhanaiḥ |
ugraśakti madhusvādairuttarottaramuttaraiḥ || 10/19 ||
na dīrghakālaṃ bhavati durllabhaṃ śukladhāraṇam |
gaulī saṃparkabalinā dhīmarudghaṭanātmanā || 10/20 ||
upāye naiva kalyāṇi niḥśaṃkaṃ śuklabaṃdhanam |
vidyamāne tu hṛdaye nilaye ghaṭane punaḥ || 10/21 ||
gaulīvivarjanaṃ tattatkāryaṃ naiva hi sādhayet |
gaudhīvāyutaghaṭā [kh: gauladhī-] tasmāt kāryaṃ [kh: kārya] vipaścitā || 10/22 
||
anityayorghaṭannāma tadabhyāsavatāṃ bhavet |
īṣaddhivāyughaṭanā sasonaiva prayojakaḥ || 10/23 ||
sā gaulī sahitaṃ kāryaṃ saṃsādhayati pārvati [kh: -tī] |
ataścittasya maruto yogaḥ prathamamucyate || 10/24 ||
tattaṃtraṃ bahudhā proktaṃ pādāṃjali vapurbhṛtā |
pādāṃjaliṃ vā dhātuḥ sa yoge taddurllabho bhavet || 10/25 ||
pādāṃjalānunaraṇāt [kh: -ṇād] yājñavalkyādi [kh: yājña * *?dyādi] 
yogibhiḥ |
nādaiścagorakṣamukhaistaṃtrametadvitānatam || 10/26 ||
te yogamāyā guptāṃgā yoginībhiśca saṃyutāḥ |
yogena stabdhavīryāśca sadābhogānupāsate || 10/27 ||
ādināthaḥ prathamataḥ paścādudayanāmavān |
matsyeṃdranāgorakṣaścaturaṃgī [kh: -nāthagorakṣaśca-] ca kaṃdalī || 10/28 ||
78k) koṃkaṇeśoruṇagirirjalaṃdho bhāgavardhanaḥ [kh: bhoga-] |
capeṭiko cūḍabālastathā gobāla [kh: gopāla] eva ca || 10/29 ||
muddāyirevaṇaścaiva rāmānāgārjunāvapi [kh: rāma-] |
vyāloheraṇḍa nāmā ca śūrpapāyī tathā paraḥ || 10/30 ||
siddhakukkurapāyī [kh: -kukkarapāyī-] ca tathānyaścādima prabhuḥ |
etedhīrmaruto ghaṭṭaṃ kālasaṃkarṣaṇakṣamam || 10/31 ||
prāpyāmṛtena tṛptāṃgāḥ saṃcaraṃtyaparepi ca |
khaṇḍayitvā kāladaṇḍaṃ brahmāṃḍeṣu caraṃti te || 10/32 ||
teṣāṃ samasta [kh: samastaḥ] siddhānāṃ bhinneṣu matavartmasu |
tatsārabhūtaṃ tadvīryarakṣārthaṃ matamucyate || 10/33 ||
nīrakṣīra vadatyaṃtā śleṣoyaddhīsamīrayoḥ |
tameva yogaṃ paramaṃ yoginaḥ samacakṣate || 10/34 ||
ubhāvetau  mattagajā vubhāvetau [kh: ubhā-] mahābalau |
ubhaujagatsṛṣṭikarau yanmanomāruteśvarau || 10/35 ||
kṛṣṇārjunā vivodagrau bharatādivadurddharau |
mahāsaṃvit prakāśātma svarūpavasunigrahau || 10/36 ||
parasparāti śayitaprauḍhaśakti parākramau |
ubhau nāśayato deha krameṇa pariśīlitau || 10/37 ||
amaraṃ kuruto jīvamubhautau pariśīlitau |
mṛtyubhūtā vimāveva prāṇabhūtā vimau punaḥ || 10/38 ||
sukhabhūtā [kh: pāpa-] vimāveva duḥkhabhūtā [kh: puṇya-] vimau priye |
pāpabhūtā [kh: sukha-] vimāveva puṇyabhūtā [kh: duḥkh-] vimau priyai [kh: 
priye] || 10/39 ||
79k) sūkṣmabhūtā vimāveva vyāpakau cāpi pārvati [kh: -tī] |
bahunā kimihoktena dvaumānasasamīraṇau || 10/40 ||
ānaṃdadvāraviṭakā ghaṭodghāṭanakuñcikau |
tayoḥ saṃyojanaṃ vakṣye sāvadhānā śruṇu priye || 10/41 ||
iḍayāṣoḍaśa pibenmātrā mārutamāṃbike [kh: -maṃbike] |
catuḥṣaṣṭyā tu mātrāṇāṃ pūrayenmāruteśvaram || 10/42 ||
dvātriṃśanmātṛkābhistu [kh: dvātṛṃśan-] recayetpiṃgalādhvanā |
paṃcalaghvakṣaroccāra kālomātreti kathyate || 10/43 ||
manastu dhārayedvāyau vāyumeva prapūrayet [kh: -yet?] |
pūrakaḥ kuṃbhakaścaiva recakaśca maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 10/44 ||
sūryojaḥ śītalaṃ caiva hastā cā bhramarī punaḥ |
kākolakamukhaścava vāyupūraṇa bhadajāḥ || 10/45 ||
yuktaṃ yukta tyajedvāyuṃ yuktaṃ yuktaṃ ca pūrayet |
yuktaṃ yuktaṃ ca gṛhṇīyā devaṃ siddhimavāpnuyāt || 10/46 ||
aśanaiḥ pūraṇaṃ vāyoḥ śanaiśca pratirodhanam | 
śanaiḥ punarmokṣaṇaṃ syādyato vāyu jayobhavet || 10/47 ||
vāyuratyaṃtabalavān sarvaṃ karṣati sarvaśaḥ |
sarvaṃ vyāpnoti ca vapuḥ sukhakārīvaśīkṛtaḥ || 10/48 ||
avaśīkṛtavāyostu duḥkhameva pade pade |
sarveṣvapīhopāyeṣu maruddhāraṇakarmaṇaḥ || 10/49 ||
80k) prabalopāyamadhunā pravakṣyāmi śṛṇu [kh: śruṇuṃ] priye |
dvāradṛṣṭirjjāgarīkavimarśaścāpi dhāvanam || 10/50 ||
amībhavaṃti paryāyān manaso vāyusaṃgame |
cāradṛṣṭiṃ yadā kuryāt tadā vāyu jayobhavet || 10/51 ||
sādhakasya pracāretu sadādṛṣṭirdhiyoyadi |
tadācoratvamujjhitvā pavanaḥ sakhyamīhate || 10/52 ||
idaṃ śarīraṃ suśroṇī navadvāraṃ mahāgṛham |
gṛho bhavati jīvo'yaṃ gṛhamedhīcidaṃganaḥ || 10/53 ||
gṛhiṇī tena yadrakṣya māyā rūpaṃ mahādhanam |
hetvaṃtamīhate vāyurmitatvacchanna [kh: -mitvatva-] taskaraḥ || 10/54 ||
mṛṣāmaitriṃ dhiyaḥ kṛtvā bahiraṃtaścaranmarut |
raṃdhra prahārīhartuṃ [kh: -rihartuṃ] tadāyurvittaṃ samīhate || 10/55 ||
eṣā [kh: eṣāṃ] maitrī sarvajaṃtorāyuṣā marutaḥ sahā [kh: -saha] |
tātvikī tu bhaven maitrī yogināṃ [kh: -nā] dhīsamīrayoḥ || 10/56 ||
lokekapaṭamitraṃ hi raṃdhrapraharaṇodyatam |
ataścora [kh: ataḥ coraḥ] samīroyaḥ pratipālya prayatnataḥ || 10/57 ||
aṃtarviśati niḥśaṃkaṃ bahiścaiva pradhāvati |
rakṣatī va vapurgehaṃ kāpaṭyādvāyutaskaraḥ || 10/58 ||
evamevaṃ katipayairdivasairaṃdhralaṃpaṭaḥ |
manaḥ pramādamālokya kadācilluṃṭate dinam || 10/59 ||
81k) bahirabhyāgataścoro rakṣaṇīyo bhavannaraiḥ |
ataḥ prakṛti corastu samūlamavakṛṃtati || 10/60 ||
mithyā vinītomithyaiva viśvastaḥ kapaṭīśaṭhaḥ |
aṃtaḥ prakṛti corastu prakṛtīkāramarhati [kh: -tī] || 10/61 ||
tasmādasyasamīrasya dhānārthe [kh: -rthye] vahataḥ sadā |
amoktavyaḥ sa yatnādvāpya pekṣyevaparāṃgatim || 10/62 ||
vinā yena niketasya kāryacaryā na vidyate |
satu coropi dharttavyaḥ samavekṣya pade pade || 10/63 ||
yadi kiṃcinmanoge hi gehegehagataṃ dhanam |
anavekṣya bahiryāti tadā coro jihīrṣati [kh: jihirṣati] || 10/64 ||
tasmādasyaiva corasya cāraṃdhīryadi paśyati |
tadā sa vāyuścoropi kālena sakhitāṃ brajet || 10/65 || 10/
evaṃ sakhye samāpanne sopi pārvati mārutaḥ |
āyurddhanasya rakṣāyāṃ svayameva pravarttate || 10/66 ||
yada [kh: yadī] prakṛtyā coropi jane kutrāpi rajyati [kh: ra* *? |
tasya prāṇavyayenāpi hitaṃ kāryaṃ samīhate [kh: sa * * *?] || 10/67 ||
tasmānmano hi nityaṃ hi cāradṛṣṭiṃ samācaret |
tena mārutanādasya vaśīkaraṇamaṃbike || 10/68 ||
vikṣepamadhikaṃ muktvā vibhramaṃ ca samīraṇam |
manasaikarasībhūya bhūyastanmayatāṃ brajet || 10/69 ||
82k) anāyāsena suśroṇi yogisiddhiṃ yadicchasi [kh: -ti] |
cāradṛṣṭyā vaśīkuryādanyatra na gatāśayaḥ || 10/70 ||
citte calepi calati citte tiṣṭhati tiṣṭhati |
vāyustanamayatāṃ yātaḥ kāryasiddhiṃ prayacchati || 10/71 ||
vāyucale [kh: vāyau-] vicalati vāyau tiṣṭhati tiṣṭhati |
cittaṃ tanmayataṃ yātāṃ siddhidvāraṃ prayacchati || 10/72 ||
etasya nāśādaparo naśyatyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
etat pravṛtterapara pravṛttiśca maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 10/73 ||
ekasya kṣobhaṇe jāte kṣobhamanyāvalaṃbate |
manomārutanāśo hi tatsaṃdvāravihīnatā || 10/74 ||
sucirābhyāsayogena sa sidhyatinagātmaje |
manoyatra prasarati tatra vāyuḥ pradhāvati || 10/75 ||
yatra pradhāvati maruttatraśuklamanuttamam |
pathāseṣu sthalebiṃdurnniyate manasāmunā || 10/76 ||
vāyunā sakhyavaccitraṃ yatra yatra pradhāvati |
tatrāti taralobiṃduranudhāvatya saṃśayaḥ || 10/77 ||
vatsaṃgaurivagauridrāganudhāvan samīraṇam |
manonātha sthirayate śuklaṃ caṃcalamapyasau || 10/78 ||
anyonya manusattārā vanyonyamupakāriṇau |
āyuḥ saṃgrahaṇārthāya nigṛhṇīto raseśvaram || 10/79 ||
83k) bindunnahī na hi prāṇabiṃduryadya savaḥ [kh: sava] sthirāḥ |
sabiṃdu sthiratejāyuḥ sthirī bhavati pārvati [kh: -tī] || 10/80 ||
āyurutsāha-ullāso vidyānaṃdo mahadbalam |
kāṃtiḥ puṣṭiśca tuṣṭiśca rasanāthe pratiṣṭhitāḥ || 10/81 ||
rasegalati tatsarvaṃ galatyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
rase [kh: rasaṃ] tu stabdhatāṃ yāte stabdhaṃ tatsarvamaṃbike || 10/82 ||
rasanā vi pramokṣastu yajñāṃte'va bhṛthātmanā [kh: bhṛdā-] |
aṣṭāhutīnāṃ paryaṃte phalārthaṃ kriyate budhaiḥ || 10/83 ||
aṣṭāhuti kramo yāvalliṃgāgnau na samātyate |
tāvadannavimokṣe tu yajñavicchedavīśasaṃ || 10/84 ||
ataḥ śaileṃdratanaye dhīmarudghaṭanaṃ purā |
vidhāya paścādgaulībhiḥ kuryātkabalabṛṃhaṇam [kh: -kubala-] || 10/85 ||
gaulīnāmamahāyogo gulādyairvastubhiḥ kṛtā [kh: kṛtaḥ] |
atitīvromarudbhoktā manodhairyavivarddhanaḥ || 10/86 ||
nānāvidhairasadravyairyojitaiśca kaṣāyitaiḥ |
adhastādabhipātena prakvādhe satibhūyasā || 10/87 ||
vidhānabhāṃḍa saṃlagne staddhūmaireṣadhātmabhiḥ |
vidhānanālikā dvārā prāpyā sidhyati gaulikā || 10/88 ||
tasyāḥ parīkṣaṇaṃ vacmī sāvadhānā śṛṇu priye |
tenāktavastraḥ pātrādau vahnirjvalati bhūyasā [kh: * * *?] || 10/89 ||
84k) vastrādikaṃ nadahyeta jvalatāhya buvahninā [kh * * * *?] |
tasmādatilaghusvacchā sarvavyādhivipāninī [kh: -tinī] || 10/90 ||
śleṣmaduṣṭā [kh: -duṣṭa] gajavyāli pitapaṃkaravi [kh: pitta-] prabhā |
anilasyo dhṛtiharī manasodhairyavardhanī [kh: -varddhinī] || 10/91 ||
drāṇiṇī dūtikā yoneretaḥ [kh: -ta] staṃbhanakāriṇī |
upāsaniyā [kh: -niyyā] satataṃ [kh: -ta] mahāyāga pravarttibhiḥ || 10/92 ||
gaulī rasanasaṃpanna satvaumānasamārutau |
aṣṭakāṣṭakaparyaṃtamapi biṃdurnniyacchataḥ || 10/93 ||
anena vāyuyogena dhairyeṇāpi vicetasaḥ |
gaulīrasasya prābalyāt stabdhavīryāmagātmaje || 10/94 ||
staṃbhe sati raseṃdrasyadhīmarudgaulimelanāt |
yāgarāja pradhānāṃgaṃ labhyateṣṭāhuti kramāt || 10/95 ||
eṣate kathito yogo vīryadhāraṇasādhakam |
aparaṃ kinnukalyāṇi praṣṭumutkaṃṭhitaṃ manaḥ || 10/96 ||
saṃdagdhatā yatra cate tatra subhru [kh: subhrū] vivicyate [kh: vivicchyate] |

ityānaṃdataṃtre daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||



ekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ 

śrīdevyuvāca


aṣṭāhuti kramodeva sūcitena [kh: -to na] prakāśitaḥ |
tasya svarūpaṃ nirvaktu bhavāniṃdu śikhāmaṇe || 11/1 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ


sulabhenālyatapaso [kh: sulabhonālya-] devyaṣṭāhuti saṃbhavaḥ |
aṣṭāhutīravikalāḥ karoti sumahāśayaḥ || 11/2 ||
85k) ekāṣtake tu bhāgyaṃ syāt dvyaṣṭamena mahadyaśaḥ [kh: mahā-] |
tryaṣṭakāhutayaḥ puṃsastrailokyaṃ sādhayaṃti vai || 11/3 ||
evamādi prakāreṇa subhageṣṭāṣṭakāvadhi |
ye kurvaṃtyāhutiḥ puṇyāḥ [kh: puṇyā] jvalite liṃgapāvake || 11/4 ||
teṣāṃ mokṣaḥ karasthāyī teṣāṃ bhogaḥ pade pade |
teṣāṃ samastamaiśvaryaṃ saṃkalpādevasidhyati || 11/5 ||
ekāṣṭakaṃ samārabhya yāvadaṣṭāṣṭakaṃ priye |
uttarottarameteṣāṃ nirvāṇa sukhahetutā || 11/6 ||
ekā'ṭakāvasāne tu mahāyaga maheśituḥ |
tadekādūtikākuryādvyaṣṭake dūtikādvayam || 11/7 ||
evamaṣṭāṣṭake vāpi samāpye yāgaśekharī |
aṣṭau dūtyastu karttavyāḥ pūjanīyāśca [kh: -niyyāśca] tattvataḥ || 11/8 ||
ekāṣṭakāhutīnāṃ tu yaḥ kramaḥ kathyate mayā |
sa evābhyūhyatāmaṃba krameṇadvyaṣṭakādiṣu || 11/9 ||
āhutīnāṃ svarūpaṃ yo yoni darvīmukhecyutāḥ |
nānāvidhābhiḥ suśroṇi [kh: -ṇī] tasya vyarthāvibhūtayaḥ || 11/10 ||
dūtikāyonimadhyasya ṣaḍaṃgulipuṭopari |
anaṃgamaṇināmasti sirāgṛddhirmanoharaḥ || 11/11 ||
samāśritya pravṛttaikā caṃdrocaṃdābjadhāsirā [kh: -caṃdrābja-] |
pradhitasyaṃ dinīceyaṃ vaśinī vāgvibhūtidā || 11/12 ||
86k) aṇimāsiddhirūpā sā varttate taṭidujvalā |
sādhuliṃgamaheśasya saṃghaṭṭana vitānataḥ || 11/13 ||
vitatī kurute vaktraṃ  vimuṃcatimadadravam |
tamālaśyāmalacchāyāmīccaiva kaṣāyitam [kh: * * * *?] || 11/14 ||
īṣatpicchilitaṃ subhru sahyajā saṃprakīrtitā [kh: saṃ * * * *?] |
mahānadīṣu sarvāsu viśiṣṭā parikīrttitā || 11/15 ||
caṃdronmattāmṛtadroṇyā yonidarvī mukhacyutaiḥ |
yastarpayati laiṃgāgniṃ sasarvatvaṃ samṛcchati || 11/16 ||
sarveṇādhiṣṭhitaṃ bhūmitattvaṃ cātmī karotisaḥ |
ātmī kṛtya ca bhūtattvaṃ sarvatrāskhalitaṃ gatim || 11/17 ||
parvateṣvapi vṛkṣeṣu kuḍyeṣu marubhūmiṣu |
labhate nityaniściṃtaḥ saṃcāraṃ yogamāyayā || 11/18 ||
mahāliṃga maheśāna caṃdronmattāmṛtadravaiḥ |
arcayāmi bhavadgātra mataḥsiddhiṃ prayacchame || 11/19 ||
maṃtreṇānenamidhunā śliṣṭe nāgni priyājuṣā |
arcayelliṃgamīśānamiyamādyā mayāhutiḥ [kh: mahā-] || 11/20 ||
tasyāstu caṃdronmattāyā arthāṃgulamathopari |
caṃdragarbheti kāpyasti nāḍīnalinalocane || 11/21 ||
nīvāraśūkavattanvī pītāpeśalavigrahā |
tatratyamamṛtaṃ viddhi kāmeśvaryuktibhūtidam || 11/22 ||
87k) mahimāsiddhijanakaṃ tuṃgabhadrāmayaṃ priye |
īṣatpāṭalavarṇaṃ ca yūthikāsamasaurabham [kh: yūdhikā-] || 11/23 ||
tenāmṛtena yo liṃgakṛśānuṃ tarpayiṣyati |
sabhavatyaṃ samāpanno jaladatvaṃ vaśīkriyāt || 11/24 ||
nārṇaveṣu na kūpeṣu na tadākena vā hṛde [kh: hrade] |
na sarodeva svāteṣu [kh: ghāteṣu] skhalitaṃ gamanaṃ bhavet || 11/25 ||
pūrvokte naiva mantreṇa havanaṃ parikīrttitam |
caṃdonmattā [kh: caṃdron-] padasthāne caṃdragarbhā prakīrttitā || 11/26 ||
eṣādvitīyāhutiḥ [kh: -huti] syādadvaitānaṃdadāyinī |
atradūtyāḥ śarīrasya pūrvatrāpi daśābruve || 11/27 ||
ādyāhutau bhaveddūtyā gaṃḍayoḥ pulakāṃkuraḥ |
īṣadāraktatānetre kiṃcidbhūvallitāṃḍavaṃ || 11/28 ||
kiṃcidghuṇaghuṇotkāraḥ kiṃcitcimicimītkṛtiḥ |
ānaṃdanātha bhavana bāhyakakṣyā vigāhanam || 11/29 ||
dvitīyāhuti [kh: dvitiyyā-] kāle tu dūtyāḥ sukhavighūrṇitā [kh: -tām] |
stanayoḥ pulakāṃkūraḥ gaṃḍayoścānuraktatā || 11/30 ||
biṃbādharasya [kh: -syu] sphuraṇa cetanāyabhramaḥ kriyān |
evaṃ prakārā sā dūtī caṃdragarbhāmṛtadravaiḥ || 11/31 ||
ānaṃdasaṃstyāno pādikakṣyāṃ tasyāvigāhate |
dūtyāstu sukhacinmātraṃ nāyakasya tu siddhayaḥ || 11/32 || 11/
pūjopakaraṇatvena sā paraṃ svīkṛtābudhaiḥ |
tasmātasyāṃ [kh: tasmā * * ] phalotpattau nirbaṃdho na vidhīyate [kh: vidhiyyate] 
|| 11/33 ||
yadisāpi prabuddhārtha vīravadbhāvanāvati |
88k) yajedānaṃdanāthaṃ sā vīravatphalamaśnute || 11/34 ||
athānyā caṃdriṇī nāma caṃdragarbhāsirā mukhāt |
arthāṃguli samunnamra taptakāṃcanasannibhā || 11/35 ||
aratrayasamāyuktā cāṃtaśveta sphuranmukhī |
tasyāścaṃdriṇikānāyā [kh: -nāḍyā] bhūyoliṃgeśatāḍavāt || 11/36 ||
svacchaṃ laghutaraṃ phullabaṃdhuka sadṛśa prabham [kh: * *?] |
kādaṃbarī rasāmodīmānmadaṃtoya mudbhavet [kh: mu * *?] || 11/37 ||
eṣā sudhāparānaṃda rūpiṇīśailanaṃdinī |
godāvarī tīrthamayī tayā siṃcenmaheśvaram || 11/38 ||
āhutyātvanayā prīto liṃgarūpo maheśvaraḥ |
rudratvasīmābhaktasya vahnitattva vaśīkṛtim || 11/39 ||
modinī vāgvibhūtiṃ ca laghimāsiddhimeva ca |
tanoti bhagavāneṣaḥ mahānaṃda maheśvaraḥ || 11/40 ||
na jvālā na viṣajvālānoṣṇāṃśorapi raśmayaḥ |
umedāhaṃ prayacchaṃti svayamagni mayoyataḥ || 11/41 ||
eṣā tṛtīyāhutiḥ [kh: -huti] syāttṛtīya [kh: syātṛtiyya] puruṣārthanā |
asyāmaṃtrastu suśroṇī śrūyatāmabhidhīyate [kh: -dhiyyate] || 11/42 ||
mahāliṃgamheśāna caṃdriṇī nāḍikādrutaiḥ |
arcayāmi bhavadgātra mataḥ siddhiṃ prayacchame || 11/43 ||
navātmadaṃpatiyuta svāhāṃtoyaṃ mahāmanuḥ |
athānyanāḍikādevī yā sā caṃdramahodayā || 11/44 ||
89k) caṃdriṇyarthāṃgula sthānā navakuṃkumapāṭalā |
aṃtaḥ kiṃcicchyāmavarṇātriṣu sthāneṣu vakritā || 11/45 ||
sadyaḥ prasūtabhujaga bhogabhāgamanoharā |
līlānamrita gaurāṃgī valadbhrūvallibhāsurā || 11/46 ||
sātu liṃgamaheśasya bhramarī vṛttalīlayā [kh: vṛtta-] |
vidāritamukhī bhūyavimuṃcati parāmṛtam || 11/47 ||
taccatāpiṃchakusumapṛṣṭabhāgasamadyutim [kh: -pṛṣṭha-] |
purāṇasīdhusaurabhyā nirbharaṃ śītalaṃlaghu || 11/48 ||
vimalāvāgdevatāyā rūpaṃ  garimasiddhidam |
sarasvatī śivalinītīrtha vaccāpi pāvanam || 11/49 ||
anena labhate vīraḥ siddhimugratva rūpiṇīm |
vāyutattva samatvaṃ ca vaśīkuryādayatnataḥ || 11/50 ||
svesaṃcāracaturo [kh: sveccāsaṃ-] mahābalaparākramaḥ |
hāṭhātvṛkṣasya [kh: haṭhāt-] bhaṃgeṣu hāṭhācchaila prakaṃpane || 11/51 ||
haṭhātprakṣobhananane nagaragrāmavalliṣu |
caturāmnāya taṃtrokta sarvakarmaphalapradā || 11/52 ||
etadāhuti kāle ca pūrvasya dvyāhutāvadhi |
dūtyāśarīralatikādaśāmapi śiveśṛṇu  || 11/53 ||
stanāgre kakṣayoraṃse cibuke sphuraṇaṃ muhuḥ |
īṣadviśrāṃnti saṃcāra tārakā tāṃḍavaṃ dṛśoḥ || 11/54 ||
90k) saśvāsakaṃpanaṃ mūrdhnaḥ sukhapuṣṭita gātratā |
maṃdasmitasamunmeṣo dṛśorānaṃda biṃdavaḥ || 11/55 ||
vīrasyaślāghanaṃ bhūyo halāvīreti bhāṣayā |
etādṛśāguṇāṃ proktāstṛtīyāhuti karmaṇi [kh: -ṇī] || 11/56 ||
kadaṃbagolakacchannamiva sarvaṃ vapurbhavet |
prasaktapulakāṃkūra daṃturatvānniraṃtaram || 11/57 ||
sītkṛtā vividhāśrāvyā muralīnāṭapeśalāḥ |
pīyaṃte [kh: piyyaṃte] bhogibhiḥ karṇairatyādarasamarpitaiḥ || 11/58 ||
īṣaduttarapakṣmāgra svarśitāṃtīrayordvayoḥ [kh: -dṛśoḥ] |
atīvapāṭalacchāyā gaṃḍayornnetrayorapi || 11/59 ||
uttālanaṃ nitaṃbasya bhūyobhūyaḥ priyaṃ prati |
nijapārṣṇi praghātena todanaṃ nātha pṛṣṭhataḥ || 11/60 ||
pāritoṣika [kh: -ṣaka] rūpeṇa gāḍhāśleṣa samarpaṇaiḥ [kh: * * * *?] |
priyasya raṃjanaṃ bhūyaḥ premārdraskhalitaṃ [kh: -skha * *?] vacaḥ [kh: * *?] || 
11/61 ||
kvacillanaṃ kvacidbhrāṃtiḥ kvacidānaṃdamurcchitaḥ |
vahatāmanasā samyagānaṃdānnapraveśanam || 11/62 ||
harasiprāṇa sarvasvaṃ mahatvaṃ sukhasevadhīḥ |
ahotvamānaṃda mayo māṃ majjayasi cinmukhe || 11/63 ||
naṃdāmināthanaṃdāminaṃdāmi svargajaṃ sukham |
tathā kuru tathā nātha tathāmardayamardaya || 11/64 ||
91k) gṛhāṇamāmupāroha pātayāthaḥ pata priya |
diśame sukhasarvasvamiti jalpaśatānyapi || 11/65 ||
tanoti dūti sarasā caturthāhuti karmaṇi |
ityaṣṭakamahāhoma catuṣkaṃ samudīritam || 11/66 ||

ityānaṃdataṃtre ekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||



dvādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

caturthyāyābhidhā [kh: caturthāyābhidā] proktā yā nāḍī śubhalakṣaṇā |
tasyāstu śubhage nāḍyāḥ pronnatārthāṃgulāvadhau || 12/1 ||
caṃdravatyāhūyā caṃdrabiṃduniṣyaṃdasuṃdarā |
vibhinnasphaṭikākārā sadā pulakitākṛtiḥ || 12/2 ||
susparśā kuṇḍalī bhūtā sarvabhogamanoharā |
sadā sphuraṃtī dehasya kāṃtyunmeṣavivarddhinī || 12/3 ||
tasmāt sravati pīyūṣaṃ [kh: piyyūṣaṃ] mānmathadravalakṣaṇam |
liṃgaśaṃkarabhūyiṣṭha cāritāṃḍavaḍaṃbaraiḥ || 12/4 ||
aṃtarmaṇi caturdikṣu śrīliṃgeśvaramaulinā |
ghaṭanaṃ kramaśo yat tat cāritāṇḍavamācaran || 12/5 ||
kriyāsamabhihāreṇa cāritāṇḍavamucyate |
caṃdravatyamṛtaṃ sarvaṃ pracyāvayati suṃdari [kh: -rī] || 12/6 ||
vidrumadrumasaṃkāśa sephālīpuṣpasaurabham |
kiṃcit piccilitāpannaṃ kiṃcidutsūkṣmabudbudam || 12/7 ||
92k) kumārīdalasaṃbhūta dravatulyaṃ ca nirmalam |
atyānaṃdakaraṃ devi [kh: devī] sparśanādeva bhoginām || 12/8 ||
sā sudhā vimalasphūrttiraruṇā vāgvibhūtidā |
śoṇābhidhā mahātīrtha mayaṃśokaviśoṣaṇam || 12/9 ||
īśitvasiddhijananī [kh: īsitva-] tayā siṃcenmaheśvaram |
liṃgarūpiṇamuddāmarāgajvālājaṭākulam || 12/10 ||
taddhārāhutikāle tu maṃtrametat samuccaret |
mahāliṃga maheśāna rasaiścaṃdravatī bhavaiḥ || 12/11 ||
arcayāmi bhavadgātramataḥ siddhiṃ prayaccha me |
ityanenaiva maṃtreṇa juhuyālliṃgapāvake || 12/12 || 12/
tayā hutyā ca suprīto bhagavān liṃgaśaṃkaraḥ |
bhaktasyeśānabhāvaṃ ca sūryatatvavaśīkṛtim || 12/13 ||
sūryavacca prakāśatva prākṛtāmapi connatam |
iṃdriyāṇāṃ prabalatāṃ dūradṛṣṭimadāṃbike || 12/14 ||
dūrīśrutiṃ ca sarvatra sūryastaṃbhanakautukam |
prayacchatyakhilāṃ siddhiṃ taijasīṃ viśvabhāsinīm || 12/15 ||
eṣāhuti mahādevī kṛṣṇāveṇīmayī [kh: kṛṣṇa-] smṛtāḥ |
anayā paṃcamāhutyā paṃcānanasamo bhavet  || 12/16 ||
prapaṃca [kh: -caṃ] bhāsayatyeva tejasā bhānumāniva [kh: mā * *?] |
paṃcamāhutikāle tu dūtyavasthā nivedyate [kh: ni * * *?] || 12/17 ||
93k) tejaḥ kiṃcidanirvācyaṃ jāyate netra harṣadam |
muhuḥ pakṣma [kh: pakṣmaḥ] praveśaśca netratārakayordvayoḥ || 12/18 ||
bhrūmadhye sphuraṇaṃ caiva dīptatādaṃta paṅktiṣu |
utkaṇṭhataḥ kapotastrīgalagadgadabhāṣitam  || 12/19 ||
ānaṃdanātha saṃsthāna [kh: saṃstthāna] paṃcakakṣyā vyatikramaḥ |
alamālāparacanaṃ punarutsāha bhāṣaṇam || 12/20 ||
nātha nātha manonātha vidravāmi mriyemuhuḥ |
na kiṃcidapi jānāmi jātāhaṃ tava kiṃkarī || 12/21 ||
ahaṃkṛtābhavadbhogairahaṃlagnā bhavatpade |
ahaṃ tvadekaśaraṇārakṣarakṣa dṛḍhaṃjahi || 12/22 ||
ityanargalaniḥśaṃka bhogālāpa paraṃparāḥ |
kiṃcidyonyavasādepi manasaḥ sumahodyamaḥ || 12/23 ||
aṃtargarbbhakaramyāṇāṃ keśānāmavakīrṇatā |
sarvesvapi ca gātreṣu madagaṃdhasamudbhavaḥ || 12/24 ||
lalāṭapaṭṭesu svaṣṭā svedāṃbukaṇamauktikam |
kāmārtta kurarīkaṃṭha rutavanmaṇitadhvaniḥ || 12/25 ||
atiprītyānāyakasya gaṃḍayorabhicuṃbanam |
ityādayo guṇāḥ proktāḥ paṃcamyāmāhutau punaḥ || 12/26 ||
caṃdrarekhā parānāḍī nāḍī nalinamecakā |
arapaṃcaka saṃyuktā svastikākāraśālinī || 12/27 ||
aṃtastāpiṃca gucchābhādvāradvitayaśālinī |
ṣoḍaśagraṃthisahitāgraṃdhaugraṃdhau [kh: -graṃthisahitāgraṃ-] ca sarvataḥ || 
12/28 ||
kalāḥ ṣoḍaśa caṃdrasya kāmākarṣaṇikāmukhāḥ |
bibhratī caṃdraśiśirātasyā mastyamṛtaṃ vidhoḥ || 12/29 ||
nāladvayā kṛteścāsyānmukhamanyonya saṃgatam |
mardanāpekṣaṇī bhūyāstasmādeyā ciradravā || 12/30 ||
kadācinna dravatyeva yadidravati pārvati [kh: -tī] |
liṃgeśvarasyaiva puramuddhatairūrdhvatāṇḍavaiḥ || 12/31 ||
taireva sahasābhinnanāladvitaya baṃdhanā |
sravatyamṛtavicchardamaiṃdavaṃ [kh: sravacya-] harṣavardhanam || 12/32 ||


śrīpārvatyuvāca [kh: śrīdevyuvāca]


kimidaṃ liṃgadevasya prabravityūrdhvatāṇḍavam |
tasya svarūpamadhunā vivicyakathayasvame [kh: viviccakatha-] || 12/33 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ 


ūrdhvatāṇḍavakannāma mahāliṃgasya pārvati [kh: -tī] |
upariṣṭād dūtikāyāṃ sthitāyāmeva jāyate || 12/34 ||
rāgajvālājaṭājvālairmaṃḍitaṃ [kh: -jālairmaṃḍitaṃ] cottamāṃgabhūḥ |
kiṃciccaracchuklabiṃdu kapaṭeṃduvibhūṣitaḥ || 12/35 ||
vakṣyamāṇāmṛtasroto gaṃgānirjharaśekharaḥ |
yonigolakasaṃchannaḥ prakarotyūrdhvatāṇḍavam || 12/36 ||
idameva vidhānaṃ tu devyaṣṭavidhatāṃgatam |
puruṣāyitaśabdena ghuṣyate madanāgame || 12/37 ||
95k) vīrāyitamiti prāhurvīravaddūtikāpi yat |
uparyullasati svairamutkaṃpi kucamaṃḍalā || 12/38 ||
etena caṃdrarekhāyā mukhamudghāṭyate śive |
tatastu cāṃdramamṛtaṃ śivatvasya pradāyakam [kh: * * * *?] || 12/39 ||
jayinī vāgdevatāyā rūpamābibhradujvalam [kh: -bi * * * *?] |
caṃdrabhāgānadī puṇya mahātīrthādipāvanam [kh: mahātī * * * *?naṃ] || 
12/40 ||
tayaiva sudhayādūtī yo nidarvīmukhasthayā [kh: nidarvī * ?khastayā] |
juhuyādāhutiṃ ṣaṭhīṃ maṃtreṇānena sādhakaḥ || 12/41 ||
mahāliṃga maheśāna caṃdrarekha sudhāmṛtaiḥ |
arcayāmi bhavdgātra mataḥ siddhiṃ prayacchame [kh: prayaccame] || 12/42 ||
evamārādhito devo liṃgarūpo hutāśanaḥ |
somāmṛtaṃ paraṃ pītvā cāgniṣomasvarūpadhṛk || 12/43 ||
atyaṃtahṛṣṭobhagavān sādhakāya prayacchati |
mahādevasya saṃsiddhiṃ somatattvasya paśyatām || 12/44 ||
sahibhaktastayāhutyā prīṇayitvā maheśvaram |
labhate caṃdravat kāṃtiṃ kāṃtyāviśvavimohinīm || 12/45 ||
daśavarṣasahasrāṇi caṃdraloke ca khelanam |
caṃdrasaumya mukhatvaṃ ca viṣanāśanapāṭavam || 12/46 ||
sadāśuklābhipūrṇatvamakṣamatvaṃ ca retasaḥ |
vājīkaraṇalābhaṃ ca vināmaṃtrairvinauṣadhaiḥ || 12/47 ||
96k) sūkṣmasyāpi ca liṃgasya sthūlatāṃ dīrghatāmapi [kh: dīrggha-] |
bhogeṣva navasādaṃ ca prāpnotyānaṃda pūrṇatām || 12/48 ||
ṣaṣṭhāhuti [kh: ṣaṣṭā-] vidhekāle dūtikāsthitirucyate |
ekākāreṇa sarveṣāmiṃdriyāṇāṃ [kh: -ṣāṃ miṃdri-] vilīnatā || 12/49 ||
locanadvayapakṣmāṃtaścirāvāsaśca tārayoḥ |
apāṃgemukulībhāvaḥ kāṃtirunmeṣiṇīmukhe || 12/50 ||
tatkālānaṃda hāsaśrīstasyānāyaka cakṣuṣī |
atiśītalakarpūra parāgairivaliṃpati || 12/51 ||
pratyaṃgamadhikaḥ kaṃpaḥ pratyaṃgapulakāṃkuraḥ [kh: -kuruḥ] |
anaṃgarathacakrasya kalakāṃcikalāpinaḥ || 12/52 || 12/
priyānitaṃbabiṃbasya pracaṃḍāstāṃḍavaḥ kramāḥ |
kaṃpracūcukacārubhyāṃ pūrṇābhyāṃ pulakāṃkuraiḥ || 12/53 ||
vakṣaḥ sīmanināthasya kucābhyāṃ kutukāyitam |
padavikṣepasakṣepa protkṣepaṇa vidhāśataiḥ || 12/54 ||
muktahaṃsakalasvānāramyonūpuraḍambaraḥ |
haṭhātka ca grahābhartturhaṭhācca [kh: -ṭhāśca] paricuṃbanam || 12/55 ||
hāṭhād [kh: haṭhād] daṃtanipātaśca vīraśekharagaṃḍayoḥ |
muhurutkṣipyanāthasya kaṃṭhaṃ tatra radakṣatiḥ || 12/56 ||
jihvāpallavamākṛṣya nāthasya parikarṣaṇam |
tatkālenātha gātreṣu caṃdrāvasthā vilokanam || 12/57 ||
97k) jñānasyārthāvaśeṣatvaṃ māṃdyamārohatāṃḍave |
ayināthamanonātha prāṇanātha priyāpriye [kh: priyātpriya] || 12/58 ||
kiṃrodimipi bāmitvāṃ khādāmitvāṃ vimuṃcamām |
madaṃbānāthamannātha reremuṃcavimuṃcamāṃ || 12/59 ||
nāhaṃ muṃcāmi muṃcāmi yadimuṃcavimuṃcamām |
kāhaṃ kastvaṃ kasyagṛhaṃ etat kinnumayocyate || 12/60 ||
kimarthaṃ vayamāyātāḥ [kh: -tā] kenedaṃ sukhamarpitam |
na kiṃcidapi jānāmi nāthabodhayabodhaya || 12/61 ||
vilīyaṃta [kh: viliyyaṃta] ivāṃgāni muhyaṃtyeveṃdriyāṇi me |
etat saukhyanidānāya svastiliṃgāya śaṃbhave || 12/62 ||
namamedṛk sukhaṃ jātamasmin janmani [kh: janma *?] janmani [kh: * * *?] | 
śivārpaṇamidaṃ bhūyāt punarastu  śivārpaṇam [kh: * * * *?] || 12/63 ||
ityevamullapaṃtī ca mūrcchaṃti sukhamūrchayā [kh: sukha * * *?] |
mlānā padmamṛṇālī va mālikevāsya [kh: -ke * *?] vakṣasi [kh: * * si?] || 12/64 ||
patatyuttiṣṭhati punardormūlaṃ daśatīvrabhoḥ |
līnalīnākṣaraṃ paścānmaṃdamaṃdākṣaraṃ punaḥ || 12/65 ||
samullapaṃtī yat kiṃcinniṣpaṃdādūtikā [kh: -niṣyaṃdā-] bhavet |
evamādiguṇādūtyāḥ ṣaḍāhuti [kh: ṣaṣṭhāhuti] mahotsave || 12/66 ||
caṃdrarekhā garbhakalā [kh: garba-] pīyūṣāhutirīritā [kh: piyyūṣā-] |
ṣaṣṭhiṣaḍrasasaṃpanna bahubhojanadāyinī || 12/67 ||
98k) ṣaḍāmnāya praṇītānāṃ karmaṇāṃdāśuṣī phalam |
ṣaḍaṃgadevatā rūpā sarvadākriyatāṃbudhaiḥ || 12/68 ||
etadāhuti karmāṃte yonauśrāṃtiḥ prajāyate |
nāyikāyāśca vidṛtyā sukhaśrāṃtiḥ pravartate || 12/69 ||
tatastatraiva samaye dūtikāyainivedayet |
śarkarāmṛtasaṃmiśraṃ śuddhiyūṣasanādhitam || 12/70 ||
ādimaṃ pātrabharitaṃ bhagasthe liṃgaśaṃkare | 
svayaṃtadavaśiṣṭaṃ ca pibed bhakti bharānataḥ || 12/71 ||
iththaṃ kṣaṇaṃ tu viśramya navātmamithunaṃ smaret |
maṃtraṃ cānaṃdadevasya śataśovāsahasraśaḥ || 12/72 ||
saṃyuktayaṃtra evāsau japet pārvatisādhakaḥ |
ayuktayaṃtraḥ prajaped devatāśāpamāpnuyāt || 12/73 ||
bhagapraviṣṭa liṃgatvaṃ yuktayaṃtratvamucyate |
evaṃ japte parānaṃdarūpi liṃgamaheśvaraḥ || 12/74 ||
sadānaṃdamayaṃ bhāvaṃ dehāṃtepi prayacchati |
yāvajjapati vīroyaṃ dūtiviśrāṃti hetave || 12/75 ||
tāvat sā nāyikā kuryādalibhiḥ [kh: -bhi] svātmatarpaṇam |
iththaṃ krameṇa viśrāṃtiṃ gatāyāṃ dūtikātanau || 12/76 ||
saptamīmāhutiṃ karttumārabheta vicakṣaṇaḥ |
caṃdrarekhārthāṃ gulinā pronnatā caṃdracaṃdrikā || 12/77 ||
99k) kārpāsataṃ tu varttīvamṛdulāpeśalākṛtiḥ |
atasī puṣpasaṃkāśā trikoṇākāratāṃ gatā || 12/78 ||
aṃtarabhrakasiṃdūra rajorājīva piṃjarā |
ciradravāpi nāthaika kluptairaṃguṣṭha cuṃbanaiḥ || 12/79 ||
kapolāsvādanaiścāpi biṃboṣṭhasyānipīḍanaiḥ |
nābhaucapeṭa patanairnitaṃbe hastamardanaiḥ || 12/80 ||
nābhī puṭīnāyakasya jihvālolanakelibhiḥ |
nijaṃrasaṃ sravaṃtyeva nāyikācaṃdracaṃdrikā || 12/81 ||
tasyāstu dravapīyūṣamīṣat [kh: -piyyūṣamīṣat] kaṭukaṣāyitam |
paiṣṭimadyasamāmodaṃ bāṇapuṣpolbaṇadyuti || 12/82 ||
asakṛlliṃganāthasya caṃḍakaṃḍūti vardhanam |
prāptisiddhiprajananaṃ sarvaiśvaryākhyavāṅmayam || 12/83 ||
yamunātīrtharūpaṃ ca sarvapāpanikṛṃtanam |
samastavyādhiśamanaṃ laghuśītalanirmalam || 12/84 ||
pūrvoktaiḥ [kh: -kair] drāvakaiḥ krīḍā viśeṣairyadinadravet |
tadājapeditaṃ [kh: -japoditaṃ] maṃtraṃ ca palāṃbādhidaivatam || 12/85 ||
māyāṃ prathamamuccārya capale capaleti ca |
citte iti padaṃ procya reto muṃcadvayaṃ vadet || 12/86 ||
drāṃ drīṃ klīmiti saṃbhāṣya blūṃ sa ityeva kīrtayet |
evaṃ maṃtraṃ japanneva liṃgatāṃḍavamācaret || 12/87 ||
100k) tena saṃbhṛta saṃkṣobhā nāḍikā caṃdracaṃdrikā |
sphuradgupigupitkāraṃ vimuṃcati parāmṛtam || 12/88 ||
tenaliṃgamayaṃ vahniṃ tarppayed darpamaṃdharam |
yamunāsalilairīśāḥ saṃśekaphalamaśnute || 12/89 ||
anena tarppite svaira mayamānaṃdadāyakaḥ |
datte paśupatatvaṃ ca yajamānamayīṃ sthitim || 12/90 ||
tadāsau nityahotā syāt svātmāgnau viśvahavyakaiḥ |
sarveṣāmevamaṃtrāṇāṃ svayameva prakāśatā || 12/91 ||
vedādyaṣṭādaśa mahāvidyānāṃ svairabodhanam |
kavitvasya parākāṣṭhā rasasaubhāgyaśālinī || 12/92 ||
sarvajñatvaṃ ca jāyeta paracittaṃ ca budhyate |
pravṛttiṃ ca nivṛttiṃ ca bhūtānāmiva [kh: -ha] sarvaśaḥ || 12/93 ||
svayaṃ vijñānadṛṣṭyaiva [kh: -dṛṣṭaiva] jānāti jagadaṃbike |
rasasiddhiṃ pādukāyāḥ siddhiṃ nidhyaṃjanaṃ [k: ni * janaṃ] tathā || 12/94 ||
khaḍgasiddhiṃ vacaḥ siddhiṃ bilasiddhiṃ ca viṃdati |
ākāśagamane caiva parakāya praveśane || 12/95 ||
dṛṣṭyā vidrāvaṇe nāryo dṛṣṭyā parvatakaṃpane |
pratimācalane [kh: -cālane?] caiva bhūtavetāla vīkṣaṇe [kh: śīkṣaṇe] || 12/96 ||
adṛśyakaraṇe sarvaviṣasaṃjvaranāśane |
pāṭavaṃ paramaṃghatte [kh: -datte] saptamāhuti sādhakaḥ || 12/97 ||
101k) ātrāhutaumaṃtramidaṃ pravadāminagātmaje |
mahāliṃgamaheśāna svarasaiścāṃdracaṃdrikaiḥ || 12/98 ||
arcayāmibhavadgātra mataḥsiddhiṃ prayacchame |
saptamyeṣāhuti proktā saptalokasukhapradā || 12/99 || 12/
saptajihvā svarūpasya liṃganāthasya saṃbhavā |
saptakūpāraparyaṃta vāpikīrti vivardhanī [kh: vivarddhinī] || 12/100 ||
saptasvaramayoddāma saṃgītāmnāya bodhinī |
etadāhuti velāyāṃ dūtikā va sthitiṃ bruve || 12/101 ||
caitanyaṃ pātramātreṇā va sthitiṃ bhavati dhruvam |
ānaṃda sukhasaṃbhārādati maṃtharatātanau [kh: maṃdhara-] || 12/102 ||
pūrvakāṃti tirohatyā [kh: -hityā] kāṃtyaṃtara samudgamaḥ |
mahānaṃdapayorāśau majjanaṃ tu kṣaṇe kṣaṇe || 12/103 ||
kṣaṇe kṣaṇe samuddhānaṃ paribṛṃhaṇamaṃgake |
ramaṇasyabhujā śleṣaṃ kiṃcit chlathanatā [kh: ślathanatā] tathā || 12/104 ||
netrayorāsya deśe ca nābhikuṃḍe ca pārvati [kh: -tī] |
īṣadānaṃdapīyūṣa [kh: -piyyūṣa] sūkṣmabiṃdu samudgavaḥ [kh: -maḥ] || 
12/105 ||
sarvatrāpi ca tadgātre śirīṣamṛdutā bhavet |
nitaṃbabiṃbakaṃ paścāt [kh: paśca] kāmacchatrasya narttanam || 12/106 ||
vīṇātaṃtrisamasvānamaṇi tad dhvanisaṃbhavam |
vacovicchinna vicchinnapunaruktaṃ [kh: vicchinnaṃ-] ca bhūyasā || 12/107 ||
102k) pade pade skhaladvarṇaṃ kuṃṭhitaṃ kaṃṭhasīmani |
asaṃbaṃdhārthanaṃ kiṃcit kiṃcicchuṣkaṃ ca rodanam || 12/108 ||
sadaivānaṃda hāsaśca harṣeṇāṃgasya ve pathuḥ |
alaṃ nātha namastubhyaṃ punasteracitoṃjaliḥ [kh: -rajitoṃjaliḥ] || 12/109 ||
kṛtanānasimāṃdāsī [kh: -vānasimāṃdāsi] metairbhogairmanoharaiḥ |
na sahebhavato vegaṃ na sahebhavatoratim || 12/110 ||
kimānaṃdamayo'sitvaṃ kiṃtvaṃ maccittataskaraḥ |
kimarthaṃ māṃrasadroṇyāṃ majjayasyevanirghṛṇā || 12/111 ||
pibāmi bhavatogaṃḍaṃ pibāmibhavato'dharam |
khādāmi kiṃte sarvāṃgaṃ muṃcamāṃ muṃcavallabha || 12/112 ||
pāpapātayamāṃtalpe [kh: papa-] śaśaśātayamāmiha |
vivimocayamāṃ nātha cu cu cuṃbāmi me [kh: te] dhvajam || 12/113 ||
ityanrgalamullapya rāgeṇataralīkṛtā |
huṃ huṃ huṃ dehi dehīti [kh: -tī] tyaktvāpallavacāruṇā [kh: tyuktvā-] || 12/114 ||
kaṃkaṇakvaṇitāḍhyena pāṇinānijayonitaḥ |
saptamāhutisusnātaṃ liṃgamākṛṣya rāgiṇī || 12/115 ||
jhaṭityeva parāvṛtya mukhe nikṣipya cuṃbatī |
cuṃbatī ca mukhe liṃgaṃ prāptasaptābhiṣecanam || 12/116 ||
ānaṃdarājyasāmrājyapadaviṃ yāti dūtikā |
mukhe nikṣipyataṃ devaṃ daśanena vidaghnatī [kh: vidadhnatī] || 12/117 ||
103k) anekapakṣiṇāṃ śabdān vividhān pratipādayet |
dhvajasya karṇikābhāgaṃ jihvayā [kh: jihvāyā] ca vilolayet || 12/118 ||
nāthasya ca kaṭīdeśe nābhimūle ca pārvati [kh: -tī] |
pārśvoścanakharekhābhirurarī [kh: pārśve ca-] kurute ratim || 12/119 ||
muhurānaṃda mūcchābhiḥ [kh: mūrcchābhir] ghūrṇayaṃti nijaṃśiraḥ |
pibaṃtī [kh: -ti] vacataṃ nādaṃ [kh: -thaṃ] bahulīlāṃ samācaret || 12/120 ||
iti te saptamāhutyā dūtyavasthā niveditā |
atha caṃdrakalānāḍyāḥ [kh: -nāḍyā] svarūpaṃ kathayāmi te  || 12/121 ||
sā caṃdracaṃdrikānāḍyā arthāṃgulisamunnatā |
anekavarṇaśāvavre [kh: -śovavre] vibhūṣitanijākṛtiḥ || 12/122 ||
kṛtaḥ [kh: kṛṣṇaḥ] śabalavarṇā [kh: -varṇa] ca mukhamekaṃ ca bibhrate |
atyaṃtaṃ mṛdulākāraṃ madhye kiṃcit sthalīyasi [kh: - sthavīyasi] || 12/123 ||
pārśvayostanutā pātā biṃbīphalasamākṛtiḥ |
yavākāramanuprāptā bhūyaḥ pulakitākṛtiḥ || 12/124 ||
valībhīratitanvībhirmṛdvībhirapi [kh: vali-] vācikā |
anyāsāmapi nāḍīnāṃ saptabhogasudhākirām || 12/125 ||
bahūnāmiva vallīnāṃ [kh: -nā] mūlikaṃdatvamāsthitā [kh: mūlakaṃdatva-] |
samastānaṃda bharitā kāmālaṃbanayaṣṭikā || 12/126 ||
tasyāmastyamṛtaṃ divyaṃ taruṇādityapāṭalam |
ajarāmaratāhetu masyāvidrāvaṇakriyām || 12/127 ||
104k) sureṣu vānareṣvaṃbana [kh: vānereṣvaṃbana] vā kinnarajātiṣu |
navāsidheṣu [kh: sidhyeṣu] sādhyeṣu jānaṃti na varānane || 12/128 ||
dūtyā ūrvoruruyugmaṃ jānunorapi jānuni |
jaṃghayorapi jaṃghedve vakṣasyapi bhujāṃtaram || 12/129 ||
bāhvoścaiva [kh: bāhveścaiva] bhujadvaṃdvaṃ mukhe caiva mukhaṃ tathā |
adhare cāpi biṃboṣṭhaṃ nāsāyāmapi nāsikām || 12/130 ||
jihvāyāmapi jihvāṃ ca samyak saṃveśyamardayet |
sarvāṃgīṇamidaṃ nāma mardanaṃ parikīrtitam || 12/131 ||
yadicaṃdrakalā sīdhuranenāpi [kh: sīthu-] na vidravet |
karṇamūlasirayugme [kh: -sirāyugme] sa cuṃbanaradakṣatam || 12/132 ||
karṇaśabkulikāvyomni jihvāgraparilolanam |
kiṃciddaśanadaṃśena cūcukadvaṃdva cūṣaṇam || 12/133 ||
dormmuledaṃtapātaśca karāṃguṣṭha vimarśanam |
nābhīpuṭe phūtkaraṇaṃ bhrūmadhye cāpi sītkṛtiḥ || 12/134 ||
kuṃcitāṃgulihastena todanaṃ śroṇisīmani |
āsanasya pradeśe ca maṃdaraṃ muṣṭitāḍanam || 12/135 ||
karadvayena tat pādatalabhūyiṣṭamardanam |
etānyupācared vīro dūtikādrāvako bhavet || 12/136 ||
anenāpyabhyupāyena naceccaṃdrakalādravam |
pūrvokta [kh: -ktaṃ] cāpalaṃ maṃtraṃ bhagamālinikāmanum || 12/137 ||
105k) nityaklinnāmanuṃ caiva bāṇapaṃcakameva ca |
kāmeśvarīmanuṃ caiva paṃcavidyāmanusmaret [kh: -manuṃsmaret] || 12/138 ||
tenadravatiyoginyā rasaścaṃdrakalāsirā |
anenāpi ca yogena yadi sā na hi vidravet || 12/139 ||
phaṇiśīrṣaṃ  kāmasṛṇāṃ tadā karikara kriyām |
prāyuṃjīta vidhānena japannamṛtanāyikām || 12/140 ||
yadyanenāpi na drāvaḥ [kh: -vas] tatopāyaḥ prakīrtyate |
ghaṭṭālīlakayāmātaḥ kṣobhayenmadanālayam [k: -nāla * m] || 12/141 ||
ānaṃdanātha [kh: -nāda] vidyāṃ tu manasāsaṃsmaret sudhīḥ [kh: - sudhiḥ] |
tena dratyatīcīra dravācaṃdrakalāsirā || 12/142 ||
yadi tenāpi na drāvaḥ [kh: -vas] tadā drāvaṇakārikāḥ |
atiguptamahāvidyā svarūpāḥ śailanaṃdinī || 12/143 ||
aṣṭaudṛṣṭapratyayāśca paṭhet sairaṃ [kh: svairaṃ] guruṃ smaret |
yadi satyaṃ jagaddhātā paṃcakṛtyadharaḥ śivaḥ || 12/144 ||
tena satyena pīyūṣa [kh: piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakaledrava |
yadi satyaṃ samastānāṃ śāstrāṇāṃ gatirātmadṛk || 12/145 ||
tena satyena pīyūṣaṃ [kh: piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakaledrava |
yadi satyaṃ ratiḥ kāmo vijayetetvayā jagat || 12/146 ||
tena satyena pīyuṣa [kh: piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakaledrava |
yadi satyaṃ dehabhājāṃ sarveṣāṃ sukhadaḥsmaraḥ [kh: -dasmaraḥ] || 12/147 ||
106k) tena satyenapīyūṣa [kh: -piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakaledrava |
yadi satyaṃ sudātvaṃ ca paramānaṃda dāyake || 12/148 ||
tena satyena pīyūṣa [kh: piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakale drava |
yadi satyamidaṃ śāstraṃ sāṃvidaṃ paramāmṛtam || 12/149 || 12/
tena satyena pīyūṣa [kh: piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakaledrava |
yadi satyaṃ sarvapūjyā siṃhāsana maheśvarī || 12/150 ||
tena satyena pīyūṣa [kh: piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakaledrava |
yadi satyaṃ gurorājñā sarvatrāskhalitodayā || 12/151 ||
tena satyena pīyūṣa [kh: piyyūṣa] caṃdracaṃdrakaledrava |
ityaṣṭakārikāpāṭha samanaṃtarameva sā || 12/152 ||
sarvadā vidravatyeva śilāmayyapidūtikā |
tenāmṛtena śuddhena śītalena laghīyasā [kh: -ghiyyasā] || 12/153 ||
prākāmyasiddhimūlena kaulinīvākpradāyinā |
gaṃgodakasvarūpeṇa tarpayelliṃgapāvakam || 12/154 ||
ayaṃ pūrṇābhiṣekaḥsyādiyaṃ [kh: -bhiṣekasya-] pūrṇāhutiḥ parā |
idaṃ yajña pradānāṃgamīyaṃ kaivalyapaddhatiḥ || 12/155 ||
iyamīśvarapūjāsyādidaṃśakteśca tarpaṇam [kh: darpaṇam] |
ātmavidyāphalaṃ caitadātmavidyāphalaṃ [kh: cetadā- -phala] tvidam || 12/156 ||
yadasāvaṣṭamāhutyā pūjyate liṃgaśaṃkaraḥ |
tena saṃpūjitaścāsau bhīmatvaṃ tasya yacchati || 12/157 ||
107k) bhīmādiṣṭhānabhūtaṃ [kh: -diṣṭāna-] khaṃsasvāmī [kh: -svātmī] 
kurutekhilam |
sabhaktaḥ paramākāśa rūponirmalatāṃgataḥ || 12/158 ||
ati sūkṣmaḥ sarvagato nirllepaścāpi sarvataḥ |
bhūmyādi sarvabhūtānāmavakāśa [kh: -nāṃ mava-] pradasvayam || 12/159 ||
ātmānaṃdaika niṣṇātaścetyamukti cidātmakaḥ |
na puṇyairnna ca pāpaiśca na dharmādharmabhaṅgibhiḥ || 12/160 ||
na śītauṣṇairnakhā [kh: śītoṣṇairnnavā] duḥkhasukhairnomitra śatrubhiḥ |
nāpakāropakāraiśca na dāridryavibhūtibhiḥ || 12/161 ||
na ca nairāśya tṛṣṇābhyāṃ na ca dūṣaṇa bhūṣaṇaiḥ |
na ca baṃdhavimokṣābhyāṃ aspṛṣṭaścitsudhāmayaḥ || 12/162 ||
sarvaṃ svātmanisuśroṇi viśvacakraṃ vilokate |
gaṃdharva nagarākāramapi cānubhavatyataḥ || 12/163 ||
devaṃ paśyati sarvatra sarvaṃ deve ca paśyati |
bahunākimihoktena sākṣādānaṃdadāyakaḥ || 12/164 ||
atrāhutau pūrvamaṃtrairasaiścaṃdrakalābhavaiḥ |
iti dvitīyapātasya maṃtrasannāma īritam || 12/165 ||
aṣṭamyāhutirevai ṣā strī liṃgeśāna tarpaṇī |
aṣṭabhogavatī dhatte mahāsāmrājya nāthatām [kh: nādhatām] || 12/166 ||
atrāhutau [kh: -tā] dūtikāyāḥ śarīrāvasthitiṃ bruve |
sarvākāreṇa caitanya layaḥ sarvāṃgasuṃdarī [k: sundandarī] || 12/167 ||
108k) amīlitaṃ sukhodrekā dasyālokanapaṃkajam |
viśvakṣati [kh: -kṣiti] ca yatkiṃcinna ca niryāti vakrataḥ || 12/168 ||
sarveṣvapi ca gātreṣu pulakāṃkura [kh: -kākura] gātratā |
na ceṣṭā na ca saṃraṃbho na ca mohana kūjitam || 12/169 ||
na saṃbaṃdha pralāpaśca na śleṣo na ca cuṃbanam |
na daṃta pātanomuṣṭi ghātena [kh: ghātona] ca ka ca grahaḥ || 12/170 ||
na nitaṃbasya nṛttāni na tulākoṭi siṃjitam |
na mekhalā kalakvāṇo na līlāsaṃgamaṃgayaḥ [kh: -bhaṃgayaḥ] || 12/171 ||
nālamālāparacanaṃ [kh -na] nasmigdharati pāṭavaḥ |
na pātanaṃ na votthānaṃ [kh: voddhānaṃ] na vā kāraṇasaṃpadaḥ [kh: karaṇa-
] || 12/172 ||
kevalaṃ dūtikā vakṣaḥ kṣiptāṃhāra latāṃkṣipan |
hṛdayasthāna janitaḥ kaṃpaḥ paraparāyate || 12/173 ||
etādṛśamavajñānaṃ tasyāḥ [kh: tasyā] jñatvāvicakṣaṇaḥ |
ekāṣṭakamahāyāga pakṣebiṃduṃ samutsṛjet || 12/174 ||
dvyaṣṭakādi prasaṃgeṣu na madhye tadvimokṣaṇam |
aṣṭāṣṭakamahāyāge dūtyaṣṭakamataḥ param || 12/175 ||
īśasya vamanaṃ kāryamaṃtarānaṃda saṃpadām |
iyameva hi śakteśca śaṃbhorekatayā sthitiḥ || 12/176 ||
idamevamadīyānāṃ [kh: -diyyānāṃ] bhaktibhājāṃ parāyaṇam |
itthamānaṃda [kh: iththa-] yāgo'yaṃ kathitaste maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 12/177 ||
samastasaubhāgya karaḥ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi |


ityānaṃdataṃtre dvādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||



trayodaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

109k) śrīdevyuvāca


svāminniṃduśikhāratna ciratnaiḥ puṇyakarmabhiḥ |
bhavato vadanāṃbhojāt suśruto'ṣṭāhuti kramaḥ || 13/1 ||
natṛptirjāyate deva mamakarṇāmṛtārpaṇaiḥ [kh: mamakarṇa-] |
śrotuṃ vāṃcchāmi deveśa yāgānaṃtarajaṃ kramam || 13/2 ||
mahārthyaṃ [kh: mahārthaṃ] ratiyāgasya kathayasva mama prabho |


kāmeśvaraḥ 


śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] devi mahābhāge yanmāṃtvaṃ paripṛcchasi || 13/3 ||
tasya vijñānamātreṇa sarvajño deva [kh: devi] jāyate |
vīraḥ suciranṛttena śrāṃtaliṃgamaheśvaraḥ || 13/4 ||
viśrāṃti kalayāyoktumārabheta śanaiḥśanaiḥ |
atha pradūṣita [kh: -te] pāte pūrite puṇyavastubhiḥ || 13/5 ||
tarpayelliṃgamīśānaṃ devīmānaṃdabhairavīm |
ānaṃdanāthasya manuṃcāṣṭāviṃśativārakam || 13/6 ||
svasyāthadūtikāyāṃ ca milite śuklaśoṇite |
dakṣahastasya culuke dhārayitvā vicakṣaṇaḥ || 13/7 ||
aṃguṣṭhā nāmikābhyāṃ ca vāmahastasya sādhakaḥ |
saśuddhibhyāṃtayorbiṃduṃ kārikāḥ prapaṭhan kiret || 13/8 ||
tṛpyaṃtu bhairavāḥ sarve tṛpyaṃtu vaṭukādayaḥ |
etairānaṃdayāgāṃta śuklaśoṇitabiṃdubhiḥ || 13/9 ||
tṛptostu bhagavān kāmaḥ tṛptāsturati devatā [kh: -tāḥ] |
etairānaṃdayāgāṃta śuklaśoṇitabiṃdubhiḥ || 13/10 ||
110k) saṃviddevyaśca tṛpyaṃtu mamadehe vyavasthitāḥ |
etairānaṃdayāgāṃta śuklaśoṇitabiṃdubhiḥ || 13/11 ||
śivādyavaniparyaṃtaṃ tṛpyatāṃ tattvamaṃḍalam |
etairānaṃdayāgāṃta śuklaśoṇitabiṃdubhiḥ || 13/12 ||
ānaṃdabhogadeva śrīḥsadā sarvatra [kh: śrīstadāḥ sarvatra] tṛpyatu |
etairānaṃdayāgāṃta śuklaśoṇitabiṃdubhiḥ || 13/13 ||
saṃvid devi prāṇanāthaḥ sadā tṛpyatu śaṃkaraḥ |
etairānaṃdayāgāṃta śuklaśoṇitabiṃdubhiḥ || 13/14 ||
tṛpyaṃtu svātmanāthāṃ ghriśekharāḥ sādhakottamāḥ |
etairānaṃdayāgāṃta śuklaśoṇita biṃdubhiḥ || 13/15 ||
nāthakeṃdraḥ paṭhanniththamaṣṭau tarpaṇakārikāḥ |
tarpayedakhilaṃ viśvaṃ svātmānaṃdamaheśvari [kh: -rī] || 13/16 ||
ayameva mahāyāgaḥ procyate va bṛthaḥ [kh: bṛdhaḥ] priye |
athatāṃdūtikāmastaceṣṭāṃ niśvāsamātriṇīm [kh: vi -] || 13/17 ||
atyaṃta bhogavegena srastāṃgalatikāṃ tathā |
ānaṃdamātranirmagnāṃ anivācye [kh: anirvācye] rase sthitām || 13/18 ||
brahmānaṃdānubhūterapyadhikaṃ [kh: braṃhā-] harṣamāpnuṣīm |
bodhayecchiśirairhṛdyairūpaśāraiḥ [kh: -cāraiḥ] śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 13/19 || 13/
saṃvījayet [kh: -ye] tālavṛtairmukhe svedānapāmṛśet |
mṛduphūtkāramarutā [kh: -pūtkāramarutā] mukhacaṃdraṃ praharṣayet || 13/20 
||
111k) nirhāragaṃdhavastūni [kh: -nī] nikṣipennāṅgakāmukhe [kh: -
nāgnikāmukhe] |
nābhimūlaṃ [kh: nābhī-] sakthimūlaṃ padapallavakomalam || 13/21 ||
saṃvāhayet svahastena sphoṭayecca padāṃguliḥ |
adhiskaṃdhaṃ patedasyā vidhāyakaramarddanāt || 13/22 ||
suvakṣomarddanādūrū [kh: -ru] daṃḍaṃ saṃvāhayet sudhīḥ |
viśeṣāt smaragehasya paritomṛdupāṇinā || 13/23 ||
saṃvāhaṃ [kh: -ha] saṃprakurvīta tannāmnāpi ca bodhayet |
uttiṣṭhot tiṣṭhakalyāṇi yāgo'yaṃ caritārthakaḥ || 13/24 ||
ānaṃdasya parākāṣṭhāmadhirūḍhastvayāsmyaham [kh: parāṃ-] |
śirīṣadalahṛdyāni tavāṃgāni talodari [kh: -darī] || 13/25 ||
śivājñā parataṃtreṇa marditānimayā'dhunā |
kṣamasvatāni sarvāṇi bhūyo bodhamavāpnuyāt || 13/26 ||
yāgarājaḥ samāpto'yaṃ tvayā sarvāṃgasuṃdari [kh: -rī] |
tadutthāya [kh: taduththāya] mahotsaṃga maṃgī kurvītibodhayet || 13/27 ||
tasyacāṭubhirīdṛgbhiḥ prabuddhā kuladūtikā |
atyānaṃdāt pāravaśyaṃ [kh: pa-] vyaṃjatī nijavarṣmaṇā || 13/28 ||
śanairunmīlyanayane bhogarāgakaṣāyite |
ahonānaṃda [kh: aho ānaṃda] sarvasvamanubhūtaṃ mayādhunā || 13/29 ||
ahovīra purogāminnadbhutaṃ tava pāṭavam |
aholiṃgamaheśasya saṃtarpaṇamahāmakhaḥ || 13/30 ||
112k) aho ānaṃdadevasya darśanasya parākramaḥ |
ahotṛptāsmimattāsmi [kh: ahotṛptosmi-] caritārthāsmi bhūyasā || 13/31 ||
kāntavopakṛtiṃ kurvevarākī tava kiṃkarī |
ājanmamaraṇaṃ tvatkaṃ caraṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ mama || 13/32 ||
tavopakāroyadyevaṃ na śakyaṃ pratyupakriyā |
svayaṃ gṛhasamāśleṣāṃ māmathāpyurarīkuru || 13/33 ||
ityullapya kṛtaślāghāpariraṃbhaṇa saṃbhramām |
svayamapyayamāśliṣya vīrastāṃrajanīṃ [kh: vi-] nayet || 13/34 ||


ityānaṃdataṃtre trayodaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||



caturdaśapaṭalaḥ

athavakṣyāmi deveśi mahāyāgāṣṭakākṛteḥ |
māhātmyaṃ sakalebhyo'pi kratubhyo'pyadhikaṃ phalam || 14/1 ||
mahāyāgo yāgarāja ānaṃdakratureva ca |
ratādhvarojīvanmuktirmahāhutiritīśvarī || 14/2 ||
ete yajñasya paryāyāḥ phalasvargasya muktatā |
phalenibaddhabhāvānāmatyeṃdra padasaṃbhavaḥ || 14/3 ||
cittaśuddhividhānārthaṃ yajatāṃ muktatā bhavet |
iṃdrasyeṃdratvametena mahāyāgena suṃdari [kh: -rī] || 14/4 ||
viṣṇorapi ca viṣṇutvaṃ brahmaṇobrahmatā [kh: braṃhaṇo-] tathā |
rudrasya rudrādhikṛtirjaleśatvaṃ pracetasaḥ || 14/5 ||
anyeṣāmapi devānāmadhikāraśca tādṛśaḥ |
guptenānena mārgeṇa durgesamaraso bhavet || 14/6 ||
113k) tasmādayaṃ mahāyāgoyaṣṭavyaḥ sukhakāṃkṣibhiḥ |
kriyamāṇaḥ phalaṃ dhatte kṛtaśca phaladāyakaḥ || 14/7 ||
śrūyamāṇopi pāpāni samūlamavakṛṃtati |
ucyamāno'pi nudayā sahabādhnāti mitratām || 14/8 ||
kriyamānāḥ prasaṃgāsya phalamādhvaramarpayet [k: phalamā * ra-] |
nānena sadṛśaṃ karma nānena sadṛśaṃ tapaḥ || 14/9 ||
nānena sadṛśo bhogo nānena sadṛśī tataḥ |
ayaṃ hi mokṣamārgāṇāmadhvāsulabha saṃcaraḥ || 14/10 ||
anenapuruṣobhāgyā mativelaṃ prapadyate |
guptādguptataraṃ karma guhyādguhyataraṃ tvidam || 14/11 ||
aprakāśyamavīreṣu kulabhraṣṭeṣu [kh: -braṣṭeṣu] pāpiṣu |
durātmasudrācāreṣvati krodhāṃdhabuddhiṣu || 14/12 ||
avalepapramatteṣu śālineṣvaghaśāliṣu |
hitāhitānabhijñeṣu vṛthākalahakāriṣu [kh: vṛdhā-] || 14/13 ||
parihāsaikaśīleṣu guruśapteṣu kuṣṭhiṣu |
akālajñeṣu mūrkheṣu harabhakti virodhiṣu || 14/14 ||
paracittānabhijñeṣu kurūpeṣvāmayāviṣu |
ārūḍhapatiteṣvaṃba dīrgghasūtreṣu sādhiṣu || 14/15 ||
stabdheṣvatīvalubdheṣu paradārānusaṃgiṣu |
malimluceṣu maṃdeṣu tuṃdumātrābhi poṣiṣu || 14/16 ||
114k) viśvāsapāpakāreṣu baṃdhudrohiṣu māniṣu |
kutsiteṣu kuśīleṣu śaktidrohiṣu śaktiṣu || 14/17 ||
unmādiṣu ca dhūrtteṣu jihvāśiśnaikavṛttiṣu |
cārvākamatalagneṣu devadrohakareṣu ca || 14/18 ||
śrīmadānaṃdadevasya niṃdakeṣvapi pārvati [kh: -tī] |
na praśyamidaṃ bhadre yāgarājamahāmṛtam || 14/19 ||
ānaṃdanātha yājīyāmavatiṣṭhati [kh: yājiyyā-] medinīm [kh: -nī] |
daśayojanaparyaṃtaṃ sā puṇyā parikīrttitā || 14/20 ||
sakṛjjanmani yenedamānaṃdaṃ vāsanaṃ kṛtam |
kuladvaṃdvamanenaiva bhūṣitaṃ bhūdharātmaje || 14/21 ||
kāṃtiḥ pravarddhate tasya kaṃdarpamahahāriṇī |
yoṣitāṃ nayanānaṃda siṃdhūttaṃbhanacaṃdrikā || 14/22 ||
vaśinyādi vacodevyā saṃtataṃ tanmukhāṃbuje |
bhṛṃgāṅganājavoddāma gītibaṃdhāśca kā sati || 14/23 ||
praṇamaṃti mahīpālā makuṭaiḥ pīḍitāṅghrayaḥ |
ālokanenāsya paraṃ kṛtārthaṃ manyamānasāḥ || 14/24 ||
ājñāśaktiṃ pratihatiṃ na kvacit tasya viṃdati |
vajradhārevaśakrasya parvateṣviha pārvati [kh: -tī] || 14/25 ||
vasyatāṃ tasya saṃyāṃti maṃtrāmaṃtrādhipaiḥ saha |
aṇimādiṣu saṃsiddhiṣva vipāṭava mṛcchati|| 14/26 ||
115k) dravaṃti darśanāt tasya navanīta vadaṃganāḥ |
vahnarapyanuvarttinyo [kh: vahnera-] jātuṣyā iva putrikāḥ || 14/27 ||
dhanaṃ ca vastujātaṃ ca svātmānaṃ jīvitāni ca |
upāyanīkṛtya ca taṃ sevaṃte rājayoṣitaḥ || 14/28 ||
brahmahatyādi pāpāni naśyaṃtyasya vilokanāt |
parvatāneṣu kurvaṃti ye mahāyāgamuttamam || 14/29 ||
militvā khecaraiḥ sārdhaṃ [kh: sārthaṃ] jāyate khecarāśca te |
araṇyamadhye ye yāgamarddharātreṣu kurvate || 14/30 ||
pūrvoktenaiva vidhinā viṃdaṃti balamapyamī |
prāpya tatra balasthānaṃ dīptaratneṣu veśmasu || 14/31 ||
nāgaloke nāgakanyā bhogamarpati bhuṃjate |
tasmādidaṃ mahāyāgaṃ durllabhaṃ marutāmapi || 14/32 ||
śāstrādvāguruvaktrād vā jñātvā kurvanjayī bhavet |
anyathākaraṇād [kh: anyadhā-] devī vinipātaḥ prajāyate || 14/33 ||
viśvāsamūloyajñe'yaṃ viśvāso yajñajīvanam |
prāmāṇike'smin suśroṇi mārgejīvati viśvasan || 14/34 ||
viśvāsa sadṛśaṃ loke na bhūtaṃ na bhaviṣyati [k: -ṣvati] |
maṃtre maṃtre tu karttavye bheṣaje daivate gurau || 14/35 ||
viśvāsameva kurvāṇo yathoktaṃ phalamaśnute |
aviśvāsācca saṃdehāt [kh: -hād] vṛthātarkabhramādvṛthā [kh: vṛdhā- -
vṛdhā] || 14/36 ||
aśraddhayānanuṣṭhānāt patatyeva narādhamaḥ |
paśyaviśvāsamāhātmyaṃ  śilādārumṛdādikam || 14/37 ||
116k) dharmārthakāmamokṣāśca yat prasādāt [kh: -dā] prayacchati |
acetanaṃ jaḍadravyaṃ kena siddhiṃ prayacchati || 14/38 ||
tatramūlaṃ gaveṣyaṃ syād viśvāsastatra kāraṇam |
karmaśraddhā saṃpradāyā vubhāvetau [kh: -tā] pravarttakau || 14/39 ||
tābhyāṃ saṃvarddhitaḥ siddhiṃ viśvāsaḥ saṃprayacchati |
sadāmalīmasā mūrkhāścaṃḍālādyāśca jātayaḥ || 14/40 ||
viṣanāśanamaṃtreṣu viśvāse naiva tatphalāḥ |
nācāro na ca vā śaucaṃ na vaiduṣyaṃ na dhīguṇāḥ [kh: -ṇaḥ] || 14/41 ||
kāraṇaṃ maṃtrasiddhīnāṃ viśvāsastatra kāraṇam |
viśvāso nāmamanaso dṛḍhatāmaṃtrasādhane || 14/42 ||
sarvathātramamābhīṣṭa [kh: sarvadhātra-] vastusiddhirbhavediti |
evaṃ viśvāsamāpannaḥ phalārthiphalavigrahaḥ || 14/43 ||
śāstreṇa paryaṭastasya vidvāṃstāvakatāṃ vrajet |
brāhmaṇakṣatriya viśāmadhikāro'traśāsanam || 14/44 ||
na śūdrasyāṃ yajātervā [kh: tya-] teṣāṃ śusrūṣaṇaṃ [kh: śusroṣaṇaṃ] 
param |
vīraśuśrūṣaṇaṃ [kh: śusroṣaṇāṃ] teṣāmabhiṣṭaphalasiddhayaḥ || 14/45 ||
brāhmaṇasya sadā kāryaṃ kṣatriyasya raṇānmukhe [kh: raṇon-] |
vaiśyasya paṇyalābhārdhaṃ [kh: -rthāṃ] sudrādernakadācana || 14/46 ||
dūtyastu [kh: dūtyasyastu] sarvadā grāhyā nirbaṃdhonāsu jātitaḥ |
ekaivajātirnārīti grāhyā [kh: grāhya] lakṣaṇasaṃbhave || 14/47 ||
117k) dūtilakṣaṇayuktitve [kh: dūtī-] grāhyāniḥsarvatrayoṣitaḥ [kh: 
grāhyāḥsarvatra-] |
pūjāsādhanametā hi mayāsṛṣṭā mṛgīdṛśaḥ || 14/48 ||
vaṃśenayaḥ [kh: vaṃśīnayaḥ] kalaṃkotsinasatā [kh: -kostrī-] svehavidyate [kh: 
sviha-] |
gāvastu pṛṣṭhatomedhyā medhyāvaktreṇa vādinaḥ [kh: vājinaḥ] || 14/49 ||
brāhmaṇaḥ [kh: bāhmaṇāḥ] pādatomedhyāḥ [kh: -dhyā] striyomedhyāśca 
sarvataḥ |
strīmukhaṃ tu sadā śuddhirnocciṣṭaṃ [kh: -cchiṣṭhaṃ] tatra vidyate || 14/50 ||
sadaiva pūjanīyāstu [kh: -niyyāstu] sarvasiddhāṃta varttibhiḥ |
tābhirvinā na yajño'sti na tapo'stina vā vratam || 14/51 ||
nārcanaṃ paramaiśvaryā ānaṃdakratureva vā |
aṣṭāhutirmahāyāge pradhānaṃ dūtikāḥ [kh: -kā] smṛtāḥ || 14/52 ||
pīṭhasya [kh: vīrasya] nāyakasyāsya vīralakṣmī tu nāyikām |
ānīyaghaṭiyatyeṣā [kh: -ghaṭayatyeṣā] tasmād dūti [kh: -tī] prakīrtitā || 14/53 ||
dūśabdaḥ pāpavācī [kh: -ci] syāt triśabdastannirodhanam [kh: tiśabdastan-] |
dūtī tena budhaiḥ [kh: vidhaiḥ] proktā ciṃtā saṃtāparodhinī || 14/54 ||
dūtikākulaśaktiśca pūjāpūrtirmaheśvari [kh: -rī] |
bhoginībhogalakṣmīśca tasyāḥ paryāya sūktayaḥ || 14/55 ||
vinītāmadhurālāpā dūtikārūpaśālinī |
na prāpyate [kh: -ta] janaiḥ sarvairakṛtvā duṣkṛtaṃ tapaḥ || 14/56 ||
tasmāt tāsu na vidveṣo dūtikāsu samācaret |
tāsāṃ na kīrttayed doṣā na priyaṃtāsu saṃcaret || 14/57 ||
118k) marmāṇi na spṛśet tāsāṃ marmāvidbhirudīritaiḥ |
yāṃ kāñcidapi [kh: kāṃcvidapi] dṛṣṭvā tu devatāmeva bhāvayet || 14/58 ||
pūjāyāyogyāsu dūtīṣu sarvadaivaṃ samācaret |
anyatra kāmacāraḥ [kh: -ra] syāt tāsumārdavamācaret || 14/59 ||
mṛdutvaṃ sarvanārīṣu puṇyaṃ saukhyaṃ ca yacchati |
jīrṇabhojenamātreyaḥ [kh: -bhojana-] kapilaḥ prāṇināṃdayām || 14/60 ||
bṛhaspatiraviśvāsaṃ [kh: brahaspatera-] pāṃcāli striṣumārdavam |
yāgakālaviyogepi [kh: -viyyogepi] nikaṭetāṃ niveśayet || 14/61 ||
paśyannevamukhaṃ tasyā ānaṃdasya manuṃjapet |
tayāvinā yadi japedanaṃgamadalolayā || 14/62 ||
araṇyacaṃdrikevāsya tadvidyāniṣphalā bhavet |
tasmādidaṃ mahāyāga māhātmyaṃ samudīritam || 14/63 ||


aparaṃ kinnukalyāṇi suśroṇi śrotumīhase |



paṃcadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ

śrīdevyuvāca


deva deva śrutaṃ sarvaṃ mahāyāgasya lakṣaṇam |
māhātmyaṃ ca tvayā proktaṃ śrutaṃ śrotṛrasāyanam [kh: srotra-] || 15/1 ||
dūtipradhāno yāgo'yaṃ dūtimānaraṃjane [kh: -kāyānuraṃjane] |
ka-upāyo maheśāna [kh: -nā] raktāstāḥ khalusiddhitāḥ [kh: -dāḥ] || 15/2 ||
araktā na sukhaṃdadyurviraktā apiśaṃkaram [kh: -ra] |
anuraktābhiretābhiḥ kāryaṃ karmaṇi vidyate || 15/3 ||
kenā tu raṃjanaṃ tāsāṃ svabhāvāt tāsulolatā |
119k) bahudoṣagrahagrastāḥ kauṭilyopahatāśayāḥ || 15/4 ||
sadaiva vakraśīlatvādvāmā ityeva yāḥ [kh: yā] smṛtāḥ |
atropāyaṃ vadeśāna śuśrūṣitamidaṃ mama || 15/5 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ 


kalabhāṣiṇi tat sarvaṃ kathayāmyavabudyatām [kh: -budhyatām] |
striyaḥ [kh: -ya] svabhāva capalāḥ [kh: -lā] bahudoṣaparigrahāḥ || 15/6 ||
tāsāṃ tu raṃjaneṣvaṃba na kiṃcit kāraṇaṃ matam |
iyaṃ mayyanurakteti viśvāso na hi yoṣiti || 15/7 || 15/
akṛtrimānurāgastu sarvatra strīṣudurllabhaḥ |
kiṃciccitte [kh: kiṃcit citte ] mukhe kiṃcit kiṃcillocana veṣṭite || 15/8 ||
kiṃcidālāpa līlāsu viśvāso yoṣitāṃ kutaḥ |
bahumāyākalāvatyo bahunāyakalaṃpaṭāḥ || 15/9 ||
bahuvibhramahāriṇyo haraṃti prasabhaṃ manaḥ |
nānāvidhena bhāvena tuccānāṃ [kh: tucchānāṃ] tu mṛgīdṛśām || 15/10 ||
puruṣaṃ vivaśī kṛtya nānurāgaṃ vibhaṃjate |
puruṣasyaikabhāvasya [kh: -syeka-] śuddhasphaṭikasannibhaḥ || 15/11 ||
akṛtrimamihākūtaṃ [kh: akritri-] striyaḥ kapaṭavṛttayaḥ |
raktā iva viraktāḥ [kh: -ktā] syuḥviraktā [kh: syur-] iva cotsukāḥ || 15/12 ||
strīṇāmaṃtargataṃ tattvaṃ nakenāpyavabudhyate [k: * * * naivabudhyate] |
nakhīnāṃ ca nadīnāṃ [kh: * dīnāṃ] ca śṛṃgiṇāṃ [kh: śriṃ-] 
śastrapāṇinām || 15/13 ||
strīṇāṃ [kh: strīṇṇāṃ] rājñāṃ ca viṣaye viśvāsomūlakṛṃtanam |
natairbahuvidhaiḥ patyā [kh: prītyā] pūritā apyanukṣaṇam || 15/14 ||
120k) avasyamanyacittatvaṃ vāpinyo'pi [kh: vāpinyovi] bhratistriyaḥ |
na bhogena na vittena nāvirodhanivedanāt || 15/15 ||
na śastreṇa na śāstreṇa sarvadhā kuṭilāstrayaḥ [kh: * * * * *] |
sveduḥkhena [kh: kheduḥkhena] nipīḍyaṃte sukhaṃ teṣāṃ niraṃtaram || 15/16 ||
doṣaḥ kasya kule nāsti keṣāṃ vaśyāmṛgīdṛśaḥ |
nirmānuṣepi vipine sthāpitāḥ śaradāṃśatam || 15/17 ||
manasā puruṣaṃ sṛṣṭvā [kh: spṛṣṭvā] bhajaṃte tatranivṛtim [kh: -nirvṛtim] |
nārīṇāmiva vidyānāṃ mukhaṃ kairnābhicuṃbitam || 15/18 ||
hṛdayagrāhiṇo [kh: -ṇa] strīṇāṃ [kh: stāsāṃ] viralāḥ saṃti mānavāḥ |
parirabhya tu bhartāraṃ kvaṇat kaṃkaṇadorlatāḥ || 15/19 ||
śroṇimanyasya yacchaṃti jārasya jagatistriyaḥ | 
na brahmaṇā na śakreṇa na mayā na ca viṣṇunā || 15/20 ||
tāsāmātmagataṃ tattvaṃ vivektuṃ naivaśakyate [kh: -śaktyate] |
api prākāraśatakairanukṣaṇa surakṣitāḥ || 15/21 ||
vanitā madanonmattā ramayaṃtya parān janān |
strīṇāṃ duḥsilakaraṇe [kh: duḥśīla-] paravyāmohane'pi ca || 15/22 ||
utpattiśiṣṭaṃ sāmarthyaṃ na tu śikṣāvyapekṣaṇam |
kenāpi na gṛhastāsāṃ cittasyātī [kh: -ti] valolataḥ || 15/23 ||
vāyu matraniroddhavyo gṛdyanyekenavīcayaḥ [kh: gṛdyanneke-] |
gajakarṇātta-uddhāmnaścala [kh: gajakaṇṇataṭiddhāmnaścala] patrācca pārvati 
[kh: -ti] || 15/24 ||
121k) atyaṃta [kh: taś] capalaṃ cittamaṃganānāṃ svabhāvataḥ |
na pūtirmanasaḥ [kh: pūrttirmma-] kvāpi na tṛptiḥ kvāpikarmaṇi || 15/25 ||
na vāpuruṣa koṭyāpi bhogyatṛptirmṛgīdṛśām |
anyamanyaṃ ciṃtayaṃto gūḍhābhiprāyaceṣṭitāḥ || 15/26 ||
kṛtrimeṇāsurāgeṇa [kh: -ṇānurāgeṇa] mohayaṃti naraṃstriyaḥ |
na tu tāsāṃ manoglāniḥ prāṇaparyaṃta sāhasaiḥ || 15/27 ||
ca [kh: na] pāpabhīrutābhīru na dharmeṣu samādaram |
nāgnistṛpyati kāṣṭhānāṃ nāpagānāṃ [kh: -nā] mahodadhiḥ || 15/28 ||
nāstīha [kh: nāṃtikaḥ] sarvabhūtānāṃ napuṃsāṃ vāmalocanāḥ |
strīṇāṃ dviguṇamāhāro buddhiścāpi caturguṇam || 15/29 ||
sāhasaṃ [k: * * *] ṣaḍguṇaṃ [k: * * *] proktaṃ kāmastvaṣṭa vidhomataḥ |
mahāyāga prasaṃgena proktaṃ kāmāṣṭakaṃ mayā || 15/30 ||
caṃdronmattādi nāḍīnāṃ mukhodghāṭanadhārayā |
tasmādala [kh: tasmādarala] dṛṣṭīnāṃ marālacikurabhruvām || 15/31 ||
aṃganānāṃ manopi syānnārālaṃ kena hetunā |
tāsāṃ yadi manopuṃsi kutrāpi parilipyate [k: * * * * *] || 15/32 ||
tadāsyalapanaṃ bhogairna ca saukhyaṃ samudbhavaḥ |
na ca dravati bhogāṃbu na ca vibhramasaṃpadaḥ || 15/33 ||
na saukhyaṃ jāyate puṃsāṃ strīṇāṃ anyagate [kh: vinyagate] hṛdi |
tasmānmanaḥ paraṃ vakṣya manyato hariṇīdṛśām || 15/34 ||
122k) kalatrarakṣāṃ kurvīta [kh: parvīta] nānāvibhramaceṣṭitaiḥ |
sauṃdaryavibhramālāpa saukhyaśṛṃgārasaṃpadaḥ || 15/35 ||
nirarthā eva jāyaṃte strīṇāmanyatra saṃgamāt |
avahiththāvaśenaiva parapūruṣalolatā || 15/36 ||
gopāyaṃtyo raṃjayaṃti vidagdhānapi ceṣṭitaḥ |
mūḍhāstabdhā na jānaṃti bhāvaṃ kṛtrimaceṣṭitam || 15/37 ||
svānurakteti matveva samīhaṃte vṛthāsukham [kh: vṛdhā-] |
aho mūḍhena durllakṣyaṃ strīṇāmākṛticeṣṭitam || 15/38 ||
vidagdhā eva jānaṃti kṛtrimākṛtrimakriyām |
vaṃcakānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ vaṃcakā vamalocanā || 15/39 ||
ahaṃyayopi puruṣāṃ [k, kh: -ṣā] vaṃcyaṃtyetāśirīdṛśāḥ [kh: -
bhirīdṛśāḥ] |
api śāstravivettāraṃmapi [kh: -rāmapi] śāstreṇa laṃpaṭāḥ || 15/40 ||
api nītividāṃśreṣṭhamapi dīrghāvalokinām [kh: dīrghavilokinam] |
api rajjupraharaṇamapi rakṣaṇadakṣiṇam || 15/41 ||
apyabhīmānasaṃpannamapyutsāhasamuddhatam |
apyuddhatamapi kṛddhamapi coramapiprabhum [kh: -bhiprabhum] || 15/42 ||
apidevamapi prauḍhasamarasya ca va sthitam |
yastrīṇāṃ hṛdayavyālagrahaṇe [k: * davyāla-] cāhituṃḍikaḥ || 15/43 ||
tāsāṃ tu cittagrahaṇaṃ dūtikānāṃ maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
vīrasyeva prakṛṣṭena samācāreṇajāyate || 15/44 ||
123k) samācāraśca dūtīṣu savistāraḥ prakāśyate |
vīreṇa tāsu karttavyo heturaṃjanahetavaḥ || 15/45 ||
samācārābahuvidhāmārataṃtre niyaṃtritāḥ |
ācāryamāṇairyastrīṇāṃ ananyaśaraṇaṃ manaḥ || 15/46 ||
mārdavaṃ paramaṃ tāsāmanuraṃjanasādhanam |
skhalaneṣu titikṣā [kh: titīkṣā] ca doṣasya punarīkṣaṇam || 15/47 ||
natāḍanaṃ na pāruṣyaṃ raktavyaṃtāsu kutracit |
daṇḍanītirnna tu strīṣu prapātayitu marhati || 15/48 ||
yadi kṛdhyaṃtitā [kh: kṛdyaṃtitā] devi [kh: devī] tṛṇavajjivitavyayāḥ |
paśyasāhasametāsāṃ svargate nijabharttari || 15/49 ||
jvalitodahana strīṇāṃ raktāṃbujavanāyate |
khedatāḍanapāruṣyairmuṃcantyeva [kh: -cyatyeva] svajīvitam || 15/50 ||
na tu satvadasaṃcāramālaṃbaṃte mṛgīdṛśaḥ |
ataścamārdavaṃtāsu prathamaṃ cittaraṃjanam || 15/51 ||
apekṣitāni vastūni dāyaṃdāyaṃ kvacit kvacit |
atyaṃtamabhivṛddhiṃ ca nayaccettāsusādhakaḥ [kh: -cchettāsu-] || 15/52 ||
upekṣitā na hi sukhāstrīyonāpyatya pekṣitāḥ |
madhyamena prakāreṇa [k: pra * * *] darśanaṃ [k: * * *] pradiśidbudhaḥ || 15/53 ||
ākāra saṃvṛttiḥ kāryānuraktenāpimāninā |
raktaḥ [k: raka] parebhavaṃ [kh: pari-] yāti paribhūtaḥ kathaṃ priye || 15/54 ||
124k) svarāgagopanaṃ kāryaṃ tāsurāgaṃ [kh: tāsāṃ rāgaṃ] vivarddhayet |
vṛṣasyaṃtīṣu tāsvaṃba na vai mukhyaṃ ca cārayet || 15/55 ||
sadā prasannamadhuraṃ tāsubhāṣaṇamācaret |
grāsānapi ca vāsāṃsi yathā śakti nivedayet || 15/56 ||
puruṣasya samācārāstāsu tādṛśavṛttitā |
tasmād vīrajanādhīnā dūtikā prakṛti [kh: -tiḥ] śive || 15/57 ||
anaucityaṃ na kurvītamarmacchedaṃ ca nācaret |
daṃpatyorghaṭanaṃ brūyat puruṣārthaikakāraṇam || 15/58 ||
yatrānukūlyaṃ [kh: -lya] daṃpatyo strivargastatravarddhate |
anyonyamanurāgeṇa mahatsaukhyaṃ prajāyate || 15/59 ||
nirayodehināṃ śreyaḥ pratikūlāṃganārateḥ |
tasmāt tadānukūlyāya svānukūlyaṃ prakāśayet || 15/60 ||
ratiśrāṃte nitaṃbe ca caraṇeṣu tathoruṣu |
āliṃganacchalenaiva sukhaṃ [kh: sukha] saṃvāhanaṃ caret || 15/61 ||
raktaśuklaṃ ratibhavaṃ vāmahastena sādhakaḥ |
ādāyalimpeccaraṇe dūtyāḥ sā nānyagābhavet || 15/62 ||
suratāṃtetha vairāgyaṃ [kh: -gya] mṛdulī [kh: murarī] kṛtyadūtikām |
jugupsamāno na bhavedvairāgyaṃ tena jāyate || 15/63 ||
suratādau yathā rāgāccuṃbanāśleṣaṇādayaḥ |
tadā [kh: tathā] radhasya [kh: ratasya] paryaṃtepyavaśyaṃte [kh: -tai] rupācaret || 
15/64 ||
125k) śikṣayacca satīdharmānmadhureṇottivartmanā [kh: -vartmnā] |
satilokeṣu [kh: nati-] saṃcāramutsavaṃ ca pade pade || 15/65 ||
satidharmaprasaktena tasyaiśāstreṇa ghoṣayet |
vividaiḥ [kh: vividheḥ] sarasaiḥ pakvaiḥ phalaiḥ puṣpaiḥ sugaṃdhibhiḥ || 15/66 ||
vicitrairapi vāsobhiḥ [kh: -bhi] bhūṣaṇaiścamanoharaiḥ |
udvarttanaiścatāṃbūlairaṃjanairmukhavāsitaiḥ || 15/67 ||
yathā vasaradattaiśca vastubhiścātiśibhitaiḥ |
raṃjayedramaṇīṃ [kh: -ṇiṃ] dhīmānbhogairdīptaiśca pārvati [kh: -tī] || 15/68 ||
alaṃbhāvaṃ na yāvattāḥ [kh: -ttā] bhajaṃteratikarmaṇi [kh: -ṇī] |
tāvaddhiramanā eva bhogabhedā tu [kh: nu] pācaret || 15/69 ||
na ślāgheta kriyāṃ tāsāmaṃtaḥ prīto'pivallabhaḥ |
ślāghayājāyate tacca sadarpāviphalāstriyaḥ || 15/70 ||
aṃgevalepanaṃ strīṇāṃ na tu citte praśasyate |
doṣaduṣṭāpi [kh: -duṣṭhāpi] ca girāhṛdyayāśikṣayet [k: girā * dyayā-] striyam 
|| 15/71 ||
evaṃ kṛta samācāro vīraiḥ samaya vedibhiḥ |
avaśyamanuraktatvaṃ janayanti mṛgīdṛśaḥ || 15/72 ||
aparo'pi prakāro'stimaṃtreṇāsāṃvaśīkṛtau |
sadā japati [k: jyapanti] ye śyāmāṃ kadaṃbavanavāsinīm || 15/73 ||
aśvārūḍhāṃ [kh: -ḍhaṃ] na vā deviṃ [kh: - devīṃ] na vā siṃhāsaneśvarīm 
|
nityaklinnāṃ navā devīṃ navātāṃ [kh: -taṃ] bhagamālinīm || 15/74 ||
126k) kāmarājamahāvidyā mathavānaṃdabhairavīm |
athavā bālikāṃ devī [kh: -vīṃ] japanvaśayati striyaḥ || 15/75 ||
maṃtrayogastvayaṃ prokto dhyānayogastu kathyate |
sadā kāmakalārūpaṃ dhyāyenmūrddhaniyoṣitām || 15/76 ||
hṛdaye [k: * dayau] paṃcakāmāṃśca yaṃtrarūpānanusmaret |
athavā svaṃvapurdhyātvā rāgapravatasannibham || 15/77 ||
abhraṃlihamahājvālamati [kh: -mahajvālāmati] pāṭalavigraham |
tatraiva sarvanārīṇāṃ kaṃtarpanilayāṃtarāt || 15/78 ||
syaṃdamānarasasrotaḥ paripātaṃ viciṃtayet |
anena dhyānayogena vahnayagre [kh: vahnyā?gre] navanītavat || 15/79 ||
vidravaṃti vimūrcchāṃti vivaśāśca bhavaṃtitām |
athavānijamākāraṃ dhyāyenmadanagopavat || 15/80 ||
muralīvādarasikā muktābhūṣitavigraham |
udayattapanaprakhyamurjjitaṃ kāṃtisaṃpadām || 15/81 ||
anekakoṭigopībhirmattābhirmadanodyamaiḥ |
digaṃrābhiḥ prasyaṃdayonibhinna svamūrttibhiḥ || 15/82 ||
kādaṃbarī pātralasatkara paṃkajacārubhiḥ |
śṛṃgārarasamattābhiḥ pulakāṃcitamūrttibhiḥ || 15/83 ||
pāhipāhi jagannātha vayaṃdāsyaṃ tavāgatāḥ |
īṣatkaṭākṣaṃ vikiretyuccalaccāṭucārubhiḥ || 15/84 ||
127k) samādṛtaṃ [kh: sa * dṛtaṃ] sadollāsamānaṃdamayarūpiṇam [kh: -ṇīm] |
piṃcchāvataṃ sasukhadaṃ premārdrakalabhāṣaṇam || 15/85 ||
hāsāsvādaṃ manohārī laharīṣaṭkaṣāyiteḥ [k: -ṣa * ṣāyitaiḥ] |
locanaiḥ sasmitākūtaiḥ pītagopāṃganādṛtim || 15/86 ||
ātmānaṃ ciṃtayeditthaṃ kāmagopālasannibham |
tato vaśayatikṣipraṃ dhārayaṃtiṃ śivāśrayet || 15/87 ||
idaṃ tu lalitā dhyānaṃ trailokya strivimohanam [kh: strī-] |
āsurīrapi [kh: āsarīrapi] gaṃdharvīryakṣi [kh: -kṣīḥ] siddhavilāsinīḥ || 15/88 ||
mānuṣīnāgataruṇirapi [kh: -ṇīrapi] sarvāsamāhvayet [k: * * * * *yet] |
lalitā dhyānayogena vṛddhopimadanāyate || 15/89 ||
lalitāmanujaptāraṃ [kh: alitā-] lalitādhyānadhāriṇam |
paśyaṃti lalitākārā nāryaḥ kāmamivāparam || 15/90 ||
jarāviśīrṇadehopi viraso'pi jaḍo'pi ca |
lalitājapanadhyānaiḥ [kh: -nai] strīṇāṃ bhavati vallabhaḥ || 15/91 ||
aparā [k: ahya *?] api muhyaṃti [k: mo * ti] kiṃ punaḥsvakulāṃganāḥ [kh: 
punastvaku-] |
tathaiva [kh tadeva] dhyānayogena pūrvoktairapi [kh: pūrvokterapi] ca kramaiḥ || 
15/92 ||
anuraṃjitadūtīko mahāyāgaṃ samācaret |
anyathā [kh: anyadhā] viphalaṃ tasya ratādvarasamarccanam [kh: -dhvara-] || 15/93 ||


ityānaṃdataṃtre paṃcadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||



ṣoḍaśapaṭalaḥ
128k) śrīdevyuvāca


deva deva triśūlāṃkajagadānaṃdadāyaka |
strīṇāṃ vyavasthā sarvāsāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ vyavasthitiḥ || 16/1 ||
śāstrokte naiva mārgeṇa kalpitāṃ tāṃ vada prabho |
caturāśīti lakṣāṇijātayaḥ saṃprakīrttitāḥ || 16/2 ||
tāsu sarvāsvapi ca na vyavasthā kāpi vidyate |
asyāḥ [kh: asyā] striya amuṣyāstu bhartāyamiti śaṃkara || 16/3 ||
maryādā kalpanākena [kh: -nā] pūrvamādau pravarttitā [kh: prakīrttitā] |
jāti sāmyena ye dharmāste dharmā narajātiṣu || 16/4 ||
adhikaṃ kimidaṃ deva puṇyapāpavyavasthitiḥ |
dharmādharmavyavasthā ca vedaśāstrādiḍaṃbaraḥ || 16/5 ||
kimarthaṃ narajātestu viśiṣṭā vṛttayaḥ kṛtāḥ |
atrakāraṇamatyarthaṃ niścityabrūhi cinnidhe || 16/6 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ 


satyamuktaṃ tvayā deviḥ [kh: devīḥ] sarvāḥ [kh: sarvā] surjātayaḥ [kh: syur-] 
samāḥ |
sarvāsāmapi jātīnāṃ jagdhiyābdhisuṣuptayaḥ || 16/7 ||
aparaṃ nādhikaṃ kiṃcinmānuṣyasyādhikaṃ [kh: -mānuṣasyādhikaṃ] bahu |
etat hetukaṃ devī hetustatra nigadyate || 16/8 ||
ahetukaḥ samāraṃbho na kvāpibhavati priye |
iṣṭasādhanataṃtrajñāḥ [kh: -jñā] na sarvatrāpi jātiṣu || 16/9 ||
mṛgāderapyayaṃ [kh: -tavyayā] bodhastṛṇabhakṣekṣudhāśamaḥ [kh: 
bodhāstṛṇa -] |
pakṣīṇāmapi sarveṣāṃ svāhāre potapoṣaṇam || 16/10 ||
129k) gavāderapi doṣoste [kh: -sti] tṛṇahastaṃ naraṃ prati |
āyāṃti daṇḍahastaṃ tu dṛṣṭvāmithyā [kh: -bhītyā] pravarttate || 16/11 ||
nijavatse prītipoṣyastadarthaṃ kṣīrakāṃkṣiṇam |
dhyātvāvatsaṃvano [k: dyātvā-] pāṃtādhyāne naiva samāśamaḥ [kh: -
gamaḥ] || 16/12 ||
paśvāderjñānarāhityamiti kecidacetanam [kh: -nāṃ] |
kathaṃ jñānasya rāhityaṃ varttate girinaṃdini [kh: -nī] || 16/13 ||
iṣṭasādhanatājñānaṃ sarvatrāpi samasthitam [kh: sabha-] |
paśvāderapi bodhastu manuṣyavadudīkṣyate || 16/14 ||
tiryagjñāte [kh: -jāte] pratyutāsmānmanuṣyātpāṭavaṃ bahu |
tathā hi cuṃcugrastaistu tṛṇairnidaṃvinirmati [kh: -niḍaṃvinirmati] || 16/15 ||
atyaṃta ghanatā tasya nīḍasyāpi vicitratā |
aṃtargupta pradeśatvaṃ varṣavātānupadravam || 16/16 ||
paśyād bhutamidaṃ taṃtra paṃkaṃcaṃcvānidhāya ca |
tatra paṃkalavelagne khadyate [kh: khadyote] niśibhāsvare || 16/17 ||
tanmayena pradīpena dīpite nīḍamaṃdire |
pakṣiṇī [kh: -ṇā] bhogasahitaḥ pakṣīnirviśatidṛvam [kh: -dhruvam] || 16/18 ||
kośakārasya kīṭasya paśyalūtāvinirmitam |
ātānaṃ ca vitānaṃ ca tat jñānaṃ nādhikānnatām [kh: -konnatām] || 16/19 ||
anya kīṭaṃ samādāya bhramaratvātmatāṃ [kh: bhramaranvātma-] nayan |
nivedayati suśroṇi svajāti gatapāṭavam || 16/20 ||
130k) vajrakīṭasya paṭutāṃ paśyaparvatanaṃdinī |
ekarūpāṇi cakrāṇi stālagrāmeṣu [kh: sāla-] tanvataḥ || 16/21 ||
haṃsasya paśyasubhage nīrakṣīravivecanam |
brahmaṇāpi [kh: braṃhaṇāpi] na tacchakyaṃ pratijātīdṛśīgatiḥ || 16/22 ||
gāḍhāṃdhakāranirate sarvatrāpi digaṃtare |
rātrāvulūka [kh: -pulūkaḥ] suśroṇi ghaṭādīnpratipadyate || 16/23 ||
tāsāṃ jātiṣu sarvāsu parajātyadhikaṃ kramaḥ |
anyajāteraśakyaśca varttate varavarṇini [kh: -nī] || 16/24 ||
evaṃ kṛto kathaṃ [kh: jano] mūrkhaḥ paśuṣvana va buddhatām |
varṇayiṣyati tadvādo vṛthāsaṃbhūtaḍaṃbaraḥ [kh: vṛdhā-] || 16/25 ||
catuṣṭayaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ dehināṃ sadṛśaṃ priye |
āhāraṃ caiva nidrā ca bhayaṃ maithunameva ca || 16/26 ||
tasmāt kutānumūrkhānāmabhimāna [kh: kutonu-] durāgrahaḥ |
sarvadaika svarūpā syurjātayomartyavarjitāḥ || 16/27 ||
pratikālaṃ kramakṣobho martyajāteḥ paraṃ viduḥ |
kṛte saṃpūrṇadharmāḥ [kh: -rmā] syuḥsarve pārvati mānavāḥ || 16/28 ||
tretāyāṃ tripado dharmomāna vānāṃ maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
dvāparetvartha rūpasyāt kalopāṭena [kh: kalaupādena] śaṣṭatā [kh: śiṣṭhatā] || 
16/29 ||
etaccaturyugā vṛtyā cittānāṃ bhinnavṛttayaḥ |
bhavaṃti ca vilīyaṃte [kh: viliyyaṃte] pratāyaṃte punaḥ punaḥ || 16/30 ||
131k) saṃkucaṃti ca kālena martyajāti pravṛttayaḥ |
yathā [kh: yadā] dharmeṇa tiṣṭhaṃti kṛtādi samaye narāḥ || 16/31 ||
tadā na drohalolatvaṃ na cāpalyaṃ na pauruṣaḥ |
navā klabyaṃ navādainyaṃ na dhairyaṃ na ca matsaraḥ || 16/32 ||
tṛṣṇālobhaśca pāpāni durācāra [kh: -rā] durāgrahāḥ |
etepi doṣā na kṛte puṇyarāśiḥ [kh: puṇyā-] pravarddhate [k: * * * *] || 16/33 ||
kṛtannāma [k: * * * *] yato [k: * *] yasmāt [k: * * ] sarveṣāṃ [k: * * ṣāṃ] 
kṛtakṛtyatā |
tatrakevalamadhyātma ciṃtārthaṃ [k: -rdaṃ] svasvaśaktiṣu || 16/34 ||
tadāmaṃtramayo [kh: -mayā] devi [kh: devī] sarvadāsannupāśyate |
tadā [kh: tathā] paśūnāṃ vyāvṛttirūhāpohavivarjjitā || 16/35 ||
saivateṣāmmanuṣyāṇāṃ na tvāpi kuṭilakramaḥ |
tasminkṛtādisamaye yannije cetase [kh: -si] sthitam || 16/36 ||
tadeva vāciya [k: vāci *] yacchaṃti na tu dhīvākyabhinnatā |
ata eva samācārastisṝṇāmapi pārvati || 16/37 ||
puruṣāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ kṛtādi samaye sthitaḥ |
anāvṛtāḥ [kh: -tā] striyaḥ sarvāḥ [kh: sarvās] tadāsan svairavṛttayaḥ || 16/38 ||
na tu tāsāṃ nirodho vā niyatyaṃ tena bhartṛbhiḥ |
yadā kadācit puṇyārthamapatyārthaṃ pravarttate || 16/39 ||
tadā sayāṃ kāṃcidapi striyaṃ ramayati svayam |
tatrāpatyaṃ samutpādya nijavāṃchāṃ prapūrayan || 16/40 ||
132k) punastiryagvadevāsau saṃcaratya kṛtāgrahaḥ |
upabhuktāmaye yaṃ stritya [kh: stri *] abhimāno na kaścana || 16/41 ||
yatastatra kṛte kāle durguṇānāṃ na saṃbhavaḥ |
samastajāti sāmyena tadā sarve sthitānarāḥ || 16/42 ||
punaḥ kālavaśāt tasyānnarajātau samudgatau |
araṇyorvyāmivoddīptau rāgadveṣa hutāśanau || 16/43 ||
kālena parivākotha [kh: pākotha] rāgadveṣādipātratā |
yat samasteṣu martyeṣu svabhāvādupajāyate || 16/44 ||
kālasyaiva svabhāvo'yaṃ na svabhāvasya codanā |
rāgadveṣa mahādāvadahyamāna itastataḥ || 16/45 ||
kṛṣṇāṃgārāni vā pārāṃdurgiṇo na punarnarāḥ |
dvāparādyeṣu kāleṣu rāgadveṣa vimūrchitaḥ [kh: vimūrcitaḥ] || 16/46 ||
tadutthitaiścaturbhāvairyuktaciddaṃ [kh: taduddhitaiścaturbhāvairyukta itthaṃ] 
vibudhyate [k: vibudyate] |
mamaivasakalābhūmi [k: -lamūmi] viṣayatvena tiṣṭhatu || 16/47 ||
mamaivabhogavastūni savāṇyapi ca jāgratu |
mamaiva sakalānārīrati karmaṇi tiṣṭhatu || 16/48 ||
ayaṃ durātmāmadbhuktāṃ bhūmiṃ bhoktumihāgataḥ |
yat pṛthivyāṃ vrīhiyavaṃ hiraṇyaṃ paśavaḥ [kh: -va] striyāḥ [kh: -yaṃ] || 16/49 
||
tatsarvaṃ hi madarthaṃ syāt māṃvināko paraḥ prabhuḥ |
madbhuktāṃ vāma nayanāmeṣa paśyati durmatiḥ || 16/50 || 16/
iyaṃ ca duṣṭāmāṃtyaktvā [kh: -tyaktā] sa vilāsaṃ tamīkṣate |
ato'sya śāsanaṃ kāryameṣaduṣṭo nihanyatām || 16/51 ||
133k) ahaṃ tu prabalaḥ prauḍho nipuṇobahusādhanaḥ |
imaṃ tu durbalaṃ hatvā bhogān bhuṃje manoharān || 16/52 ||
iti rāgeṇa bhogyeṣu tatsaṃgīṣvapi cerṣayā [kh: cerṣyayā] |
anyonyakalahaṃ kṛtvā paraspara vimardanaiḥ || 16/53 ||
parasparaṃ kṣayaṃ yātāḥ bahavomānavāḥ priye |
nivārakonako'pyasti sarvepyuddāna buddhayaḥ || 16/54 ||
ātmanaḥ kṣayamajñātvā kalahaṃ manujāpyadhuḥ |
na mūrdhnodeha pīḍāṃvānātmaśṛṃgasya moṭanam || 16/55 ||
janitakrodharaktākṣau [kh: jānīta- -kṣā] mṛdnaṃtāpūraṇauraṇe [kh: 
mṛdhnaṃtā-] |
vṛthādveṣeṇa [kh: vṛdhā-] kinnāśaṃ vyayaṃyāma iti priye || 16/56 ||
na tat kāle vicāro'sti sarvepyuttānabuddhayaḥ |
tretādikālakakṣyāyāṃ [k: -ṭikāla-] yāvatī dīrghadīrghatā [k: ṭīrghaṭīrghatā] 
|| 16/57 ||
sarvepuruṣa cittānāṃ tāvatīduṣṭaduṣṭatā |
ato'nyonyasya hananamanyonyasya ca māraṇam || 16/58 ||
vidhāyanāśaṃ prāpteṣu martyeṣu girinandini [kh: -nī] |
manvādayastu [kh: manmādayastu] lokasya [kh: lokeśa] vilayeviklabāśayaḥ [kh: -
triklabāśayāḥ] || 16/59 ||
maryādābaṃdhanaṃ cakrusvasatvadvastadurmadāḥ [kh: -dhvastadurmadāḥ] |
varṇāśrama vyavasthāṃ ca niyamāṃśca śarīriṇām [kh: -ṇam] || 16/60 ||
nyāyasya ca vyatigamādṛṣṭeṣvapi [kh: -ṣi] ca daṇḍanam |
dharmaśāstraismṛtiḥ śāstrairācārasya pravarttanam || 16/61 ||
134k) tanmārgalaṃghane caiva rājabhirdaṃḍapātanam |
prativarṇāśramaṃ caiva bhinnabhinnā vyavasthitiḥ || 16/62 ||
svastrīṇāṃ rakṣaṇaṃ caiva parareto nipātanāt |
paradāraprasaṃgeṣu yathā varṇaṃ [kh: vaṇṇa] ca daṇḍanam || 16/63 ||
etādṛśā yathānyāya [kh: yathānyāṃśca] kramānmanvādayojaguḥ |
yataḥ prajāmanodoṣa nivṛtyaidaṇḍakalpanam [kh: -kalpanā] || 16/64 ||

evaṃ kṛtena mārgeṇa maryādā kalpanātmanaḥ || 16/68 ||
varttamānā api nṛṇāṃ [kh: -ṇā] doṣāḥ sarvenirudyamāḥ |
varttaṃta eva citteṣu doṣāste tu hinātmaje || 16/69 ||
daṇḍanityā [kh: -nītyā] prakāśyaṃte na kadācana pūruṣaiḥ [kh: -paiḥ] |

||
gūrṇamānāśca na bahiryāṃti daṇḍanaśaṅkayā |
manvādikṛtaśāstrāṇi pramāṇīkṛtyabhūrayaḥ || 16/71 ||
135k) duṣṭasaṃśīkṣaṇārthāya [kh: -saṃsī-] kṣatriyābahavobhavan [k: -bha *] |
teṣāṃ bhaya bhaṭaśreṇi dvīpasādhanasaṃpadām || 16/72 ||
vimarddaśaṃkarānyāyya [kh: -śaṅaka-] mānayatyanarādadhuḥ |
rājādyagocarāṇāṃ tu keṣāmapi tanūbhṛtām || 16/73 ||
cittadoṣaśamojajñe yamadaṇḍādighoṣaṇaiḥ |
yamadaṇḍādi śāstrāṇya pramāṇī kṛtyavallatām [kh: -valgatām] || 16/74 ||
nṛṇāmmanodoṣaśamaḥ parasparakṛtaiḥ samaiḥ |
rājadaṇḍabhayādeke yamadaṇḍabhayātpare || 16/75 ||
parasparabhyādeke narāḥ pāpaṃ na kurvate |
uttamā madhyamā nīcāḥ [kh: -cās] tasmin kāledha'māḥ dhamāḥ || 16/76 ||

teṣāṃ krameṇa sarveṣāṃ cittadoṣanivāraṇe || 16/77 ||
upāyā lokarakṣārthaṃ śāstreṇoktāścaturvidhāḥ |
tatra sāmopadeśasya lakṣaṇaṃ saṃpracakṣmahe || 16/78 ||
manumukhyā idaṃ procustasminkāle tarottamān |
rāgaṃ tyajati sarvatrasvaiva bhāryāvalaṃbatām || 16/79 ||
svaiva bhūmiḥ paraṃgrāhyā [kh: -hya] svaveśmanyevacoṣyatām |
ātmīye naiva [kh: neva] labdhena vastunā hṛditṛptatā [kh: -taptatā] || 16/80 ||
paradravyanirīkṣā tu śreyase naiva ca smṛtā |
vidyāṃbhajata sācārāḥ śuddhasvātmavimṛṣṭaye || 16/81 ||
136k) iti sāmopadeśokta raktameṣāmanurvṛdhāt |
iththaṃ gṛhīta dharmārthā vimṛśyanvātmanodhiyā || 16/82 ||
kalahena [kh: -hona] bahukṣobhamātmanaścalayaṃ punaḥ |
tanmūlaṃ ca mahāduḥkhaṃ vimṛśyanyāyamasthitā || 16/83 ||
adhamyā [kh: athamyā] sādayasvīyaiḥ [kh: sātha-] purāṇagrathanakramaiḥ [kh: -
gradhana-] |
bhedopadeśamārgeṇa vyādiśannaramadhyamān [kh: -madyamān] || 16/84 ||

śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] te tadvacomatkaṃ bhobho puruṣamadhyamāḥ || 16/85 ||

vṛthaiva [kh: vṛddhaiva] tṛṣṇayāmūḍhāḥ kaṭākṣān saṃprayacchatha [kh: -ya] 
|| 16/87 ||
asminnarthe vadaṃtī mamitihāsaṃ purātanam |
astirāmonāmahansūryavaṃśe mahīpatiḥ || 16/88 ||
sa tu nirvāsitaḥ pitrā satyasaṃdhenakānane |
tamanvagāyayaurāmamanujo dharmaśālinam || 16/89 ||
ekābhāryā [kh: eka-] ca sītākhyā [kh: -khya] śuddhacāritryaśālinī [kh: -
cāritra-] |
tayā ca sahitorāmaḥ kaniṣṭhena  ca kānane || 16/90 ||

karṇā karṇikayā karṇyasītāṃ [kh: karṇyā -] saundaryaśālinīm || 16/91 ||
137k) jahārabhikṣuveṣeṇa kaṇṭīdaśakaṃdharaḥ |
nyāyamārgaratasyāsya bhāryāmanyāyyamārgakaḥ [kh: -manyāyamārgagaḥ] || 
16/92 ||
trailokyasamrādilokeśo [kh: -samrāṭ lokeśo] doṣeṇānena nāśitaḥ |
