parabhūmi prasaktiśca natvayātāṃ [kh: satva-] kvacit kvacit || 16/94 ||
dvādaśākṣauhiṇī [kh: -kṣohiṇī] bharttā mahāmānī suyodhanaḥ |
ekākī pādacārī ca gṛdhrāṇāmabhavadbaliḥ || 16/95 ||
svajñātīnāṃ pāṇḍavānāmaṃśabhūtā [kh: -ta] mahīścayā |
etādṛśavinīpātamagamaddhṛtarāṣṭrajaḥ || 16/96 ||
tanmātvamapyanyabhūmau vanyavastuṣu mādṛśam |
nikṣipasvamihācāramanuniṣṭhagatajvaraḥ || 16/97 ||
iti vyāsādibhiḥ kḷpta [kh klipta] satyāsatyā [kh: satvāsatvā] yathā kramaiḥ |
bhetopadeśanirbhiṇāścittadoṣa [kh: -rbhinnāścitta-] nirāgrahāḥ || 16/98 ||
nyāyamārge pravarttaṃte sarvemanujamadhyamāḥ |
atha nīca narāṇāṃ tu dānarūpopadeśataḥ || 16/99 ||
vyavasthājananaṃ vacmi sāvadhānā śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] priye |
dānaṃ nāmeṣṭavastūnāṃ satyāsatyasvarūpiṇām || 16/100 ||
hastaprāptatva kathanaṃ lokāṃtaritajanmani |
tatra vyāsādayaḥ procurevaṃ nīcanarānprati || 16/101 ||
138k) arekimiti pāpiṣṭhā bahutṛṣṇāparāhatāḥ |
durgatiṃ yamadaṇḍaṃ ca prāptumicchathaduṣkṛtaiḥ [kh: prāptṛmicchatya- ] || 
16/102 ||
brahmacaryaṃ vivaratāṃ jāyate brahmavarcasam |
paradārānuṣaṃ gācca viratyā [kh: -tyāt] puṇyasaṃpadaḥ || 16/103 ||
anākramāt [k: * * * *] parakṣoṇyāmatyaṃtaṃ [k: * ra-] bhāgyasiddhayaḥ |
paravastu grahaṇato nirutyā [kh: nirantyā] saṃpadudbhavet || 16/104 ||
maraṇānaṃtaraṃ svargastvaṃ te mokṣaśca sarvadā |
anena puṇyamārgeṇa tava saukhyaṃ pade pade || 16/105 ||
kāṃcanena vimānena śatanāgayutena ca |
vicaratvaṃ sadā lokānmāghasnānaṃ [k: lo * * * * *] samācaret [k: * * * *] || 
16/106 ||
prātasnānaṃ [k: * * * *] sadā [k: * *] kuryāt sākṣād devobhaviṣyasi [k: -ṣya *] |
upavāsaṃ [k: * * * *] kuru [k: * *] sadā [k: * *] vai [k: *] kuṇṭhe [k: * ṭhe] vā 
samāpsyasi || 16/107 ||
ityādibhiḥ priyaśatiaḥ phalalubdhānnarādhamān |
vipralobhya sadācārān vyāsādhyātānvininvire [kh: -dyātānvininyire] || 16/108 ||
phalamātreṇa lubdhāste puṣkaraṃ cāpi tatkramam |
tīvropavāsa [k: * * * *sa] duḥkhādyamapi kurvaṃti mānavāḥ || 16/109 ||
duṣṭasya vastunodānaṃ niścayāt pratipādanam |
atodānopadeśo'yaṃ nicamartyeṣu coditaḥ || 16/110 ||
narādhamādhamānāṃ tu daṇḍa evopadiśyate |
bahudoṣagrahavatāṃ kakṣyātraya vilaṃghinām || 16/111 ||
139k) tadarthe daṇḍamutpādya kṣatriyeṣvavaropitaḥ [kh: -yaiṣva-] |
teṣu tanmārgamālambya śikṣayantyadhamādhamān || 16/112 ||
netrot pāṭana nāsāgra karttanairmuṇḍanairapi |
hastacchedaiḥ padacchedaiḥ [kh: -dais] tāḍanaiḥ sūcivedhanaiḥ || 16/113 ||
turaṃgamūtranasyaiśca viṣṭhākuṇṭhīnipātanaiḥ [kh: -kuṇṭhī-] |
sudarśanaprakāraiśca jihvāśravaṇakarttanaiḥ || 16/114 ||
putradārādiharaṇaiḥ [kh: -ṇair] duḥsahairdṛḍhabaṃdhanaiḥ |
todanaiḥ pīḍanaiścaiva tadāsye dhūlipūraṇaiḥ || 16/115 ||
etādṛśai tadānyaiśca daṇḍanītiḥ pravarttitā |
anayādaṇḍitāḥ sarve sadūramadhamādhamāḥ || 16/116 ||
nyāyamārgamanuprāptā dharmesarvaṃ [kh: dame-] pratiṣṭhitam |
anyasya striyamālokya kasyanovikṛtammanaḥ || 16/117 ||
kiṃ karttavyamanāyotyāgaccatya [kh: -gacchatya] vanatānanaḥ |
vicārayati daṇḍena vimṛśatyātmanolayam || 16/118 ||
kevalaṃ tat kṛtairāgairaṃtarghuṇughuṇāyate |
evaṃ catvāra uktāye tadupāyāmanīṣiṇaḥ || 16/119 ||
taistu mānasadoṣeṣu śānteṣu nyāyavarttinaḥ |
manvādikṭamaryādālaṃghanaṃ na vitanvate || 16/120 ||
ataḥ suśroṇimaryādā kalpane heturīritaḥ |
ānaṃdanātha pūjā tu sarvajātisamā priye || 16/121 ||
140k) aniruddhānyajātiyā [kh: -tiyyā] niyamāmānaveṣviyam |
sarvasādhāraṇaścāpi mahāyogomaheśvarī || 16/122 ||
narāṇāṃ bhāvanābhājāṃ niśceyasaphalodayaḥ [kh: niśreya-] |
tiraścāṃ tu mahāyāge tṛṇabhakṣādivatparam || 16/123 ||
pravṛttirvāsanādārḍhyāṃ narāṇāṃ puṇyasādhanam [kh: -na] |
tatrāpi ye narāduṣṭāḥ paśuvanmūrkhavṛttayaḥ || 16/124 ||
teṣāṃ tu na hi puṇyāya [kh: -yā] mahāyāgaparāmṛtam |
ye vīrāgurumaṃtaśca niḥsaṃdehā dṛḍhavṛtāḥ || 16/125 ||
dṛḍha bhāvanayā [kh: bhāvana] teṣāṃ [kh: eteṣāṃ] mahāyāgo mahāphalam |
yairevapatanaṃ loke vīrastaireva siddhyati || 16/126 ||
śāstramārgānusāreṇa dṛḍhabhāvanamānasāḥ [kh: -mānasabhāvanāḥ] |
madhupānairmāṃsabhakṣaisveṣṭāhāraiśca [kh: -sveṣṭhā-] maithunaiḥ || 16/127 
||
patanaṃ ghriyate mūrkhairamūrkhairatnadarśanam |
pānādivastu jātānāṃ rūpaṃ na niyataṃ kvacit || 16/128 ||
kartavyāpārabhedena [kh: karturvyā vyāpā-] vinaddhaphaladāhine [kh: -te] |
ayastu paṇḍitaṃloke jalekṣiptaṃ nimajjati || 16/129 ||
tadevapātratāṃ nītaṃ plavitenyasya [kh: tpavite-] dhārakam |
tasmānnavastuto vastu [k: * *] yat kiṃcinniyataṃ kvacit || 16/130 ||
bhāvanaivanarasyeyaṃ tat tat phalavidhāyinī |
iti tvatpṛṣṭhamākhyātaṃ sarvaṃ sarvāṃgasundari [kh: -rī] || 16/131 ||
aparaṃ yadi tadbrūhihariṇākṣi vivicyate |


ityānaṃdataṃtre ṣoḍaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||



saptadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ
śrīdevyuvāca


ayidevajagannātha yat pṛṣṭhaṃ tatvayoditam |
pipṛcchiṣā punastvanyākācidastimamāśaye || 17/1 ||
prajānāṃ cittadoṣeṇa maryādākalpanā yadi |
tadyeka eva sarvasya śāstravastuniyāmakaḥ || 17/2 ||
eke naiva prakāreṇa sarveṣāmastu varttanam |
kimidaṃ śāstrabhedena buddhivibhramavaiśasam || 17/3 ||
ekovadati viśvasya dṛvatpaṃcāparonaraḥ [kh: dhruvatvaṃ cā-] |
adhruvatvamathovakti satyatvaṃ jagatoparaḥ || 17/4 ||
asatyarūpatāmanyodhīrūpatvamadhoparaḥ [kh: -tomanyo- -mathoparaḥ] |
paramāṇu svarūpatvamanyovadati [kh: -manovadati] śaṃkara [kh: śa-] || 17/5 ||
na hyekasyaiva bhāvasya nānārūpatvamiṣyate |
kasyavāśaṃ karamate tātvikatvamahaṃstuve || 17/6 ||
tatrāpi darśaneṣvīśa maryādāśca [kh: mayyā-] pṛthagvidhāḥ |
sarvatra bhinnamokṣāśca bhinnāḥ sarvatra ca kriyāḥ || 17/7 ||
upāsyādevatā bhinnābhinnāmaṃtrāśca tadgatāḥ |
kasiṃśca darśane deva yat puṇyatvena kīrttitam || 17/8 ||
tat pāpaṃ sādhayatvena kīrttitaṃ cānyadarśane |
tatrāpi hetavastīvrāḥ [kh: -vrā] bhūyasyoyuktayo'pi ca || 17/9 ||
atrāpi [kh: ta-] lagati nyāyastatrāpi nyāyakhelanam |
vyāmohe jāyate dūraṃ tayorālokanānṛṇām || 17/10 ||
142k) etat kimarthaṃ māyātametanme saṃśayaṃ jahi |


kāmeśvaraḥ


sthāne pṛṣṭhaṃ tvayādevi samyage tadvivicyate || 17/11 ||
śāstrāṇi sarvāṇyapi ca dhīvilāsādgatāni vai |
na kevalaṃ dhīvilāso vaikharyapi ca kāraṇam || 17/12 ||
ubhayorghaṭanaṃ kartudvausaṃkalpa [kh: karttṛṃdvau-] vikalpakau |
saṅkalpena vikalpena narajātyekavāsinā || 17/13 ||
vaikharīdhīvilāsaśca suvāte darśanāniyad [kh: -ṣaṭ] |
teṣāṃ tu darśanānāṃ yo vedaḥ prathamadarśanam || 17/14 ||
tasya miśrāṇi śāstrāṇi tadviruddhā na [kh: ni] cāṃbike |
tatrodāsīna vṛttīnicānyāni parameśvarī || 17/15 ||
apratiṣṭhasvayaṃ tarka uparyuparivarddhate |
na tu viśrāṃti vārttāpi kasyacidvādavartmanaḥ [kh: -cidvātha-] || 17/16 ||
yathā bhāti dhiyāyasya tadanyasya tadā [kh: tathā] na hi |
rucināmiha vai citryād [kh: citryā] bahudhāśāstrakoṭayaḥ || 17/17 ||
sarveṣāmihavastūnāṃ rūpaṃ dvaidhaṃ prakalpitam |
arthaśāstraṃ ca suśroṇi tābhyāṃ śāstrasamudgamaḥ || 17/18 ||
sarvaśāstrepi siddhāṃtā yuktayo hetavo'pi ca |
pratyakṣādi pramāṇāni sarvathā [kh: -dhā] kāraṇaṃ tvidam || 17/19 ||
pramāṇabahutākasyāpyeṣā kasyāpilāghavam |
kasyāpi samatā devi [kh: -vī] trividhoyaṃ pramākramaḥ || 17/20 ||
143k) pratyakṣamekaṃ suśroṇi lokāyati ka ūcivān |
saugatāścaiva kāṇādā pratyakṣānumitījaguḥ || 17/21 ||
pratyakṣamanumānaṃ ca śabdaṃ sāṃkhyāvyabhāṣata |
nyāyaika deśinaścānye sāṃkhyavat [kh: sāṃkhyā-] samudīritāḥ || 17/22 ||
pratyakṣānumatī śabdamanumānaṃ [kh: śabdamupa-] ca kecana |
arthāpatyā sahaitāni catvāryapara ūcire || 17/23 ||
abhāvena samaṃ paṃca mānānyapara īhate |
saṃbhavaitīha yuktaistaiḥ ṣaḍbhirmānaiśca kecana || 17/24 ||
vyavahāraṃ vitanvanti paurāṇikapurogamāḥ |
atrāntarbbhāvamanyonyaṃ vādinoyuktapaṇḍitāḥ || 17/25 ||
manvānā vyavaharttāraṃ kalahāyanta uddhatāḥ |
bhūyaḥ parigraho [kh: -he] yasya yasya svalpaparigrahaḥ || 17/26 ||
tayoḥsīdati pūrvastu rakṣaṇīyasya gauravāt |
ye kurvantī [kh: kurvaṃti] hamanvānāḥ śāstreṣvalpaparigraham || 17/27 ||
te yukti śūrābauddhādyāḥ [kh: -dyā] dhūnvaṃti prativādinaḥ |
teṣāṃ mataṃ ca dhūnvānāstarka granthibhirujvalaiḥ [kh: graṃddhi-] || 17/28 ||
anye saṃpratipadyaṃte svasiddhāṃta niruḍhatām |
evaṃ sati samasteṣu śāstreṣu kalahaṃ [kh: -ha] kramaḥ || 17/29 ||
ālopasatvā [kh: āropa-] satvābhyāmeva vādiṣu jāyate [k: nāsti] |
āropajaṃ nirasyaṃti kecit svarasavādinaḥ || 17/30 ||
144k) tasya ca pratibaṃdhārastvanya āropasādhakāḥ |
āropasya svarūpantu kathayāminagātmaje || 17/31 ||
āropa [kh: -paḥ] śabdamūlasyāt śabdamātramayo'thavā |
mṛttikākhalu sarveyaṃ bhūmirnnānāvidhākṛtiḥ || 17/32 ||
yatra rūpāṇibhūrīṇī [kh: -ṇā] saṃpadyante sahasraśaḥ |
etat bhūmī [kh: -mi] mṛttikaivakuṃbhakṛtpāṇimardditā || 17/33 ||
mṛtpiṇḍa iti nāmānyat prāptavatyadrikanyake |
atho sa eva mṛtpiṇḍaṃ kulālakaravarttanaiḥ || 17/34 ||
daṇḍacīvara cakrādivyāpāra sphuṭahetubhiḥ |
gabhīrite madhyadeśe tatraiva kuharīkṛtaḥ || 17/35 ||
agre ca saṃvṛtadvāraḥ [kh: saṃbhṛ-] kaṃbugrīvatayā sthitaḥ |
kuṃbha ityasya śabdasya vācyatāmavagāhate || 17/36 ||
punaḥ sa eva suśroṇi dalito daṇḍatāḍanāt |
bhūmau viśīrṇa sarvāṃgaḥ kāpālaugha tvamīhate [k: -ha *] || 17/37 ||
mṛtpiṇḍaṃ tu [kh: sa eva cacako] rākṣimusalādyavaghaṭṭanaiḥ [kh: -kuṭṭanaiḥ] |
kuṭṭitaḥ sanprabhajate dhūliśabdasya vācyatām || 17/38 ||
sa eva subhage caṇḍavegamārutaghūrṇitaḥ | 
śīrṇaśīrṇaḥ punarvegāttra [kh: -gātra] sareṇutvamicchati || 17/39 ||
teṣāṃ ca trasareṇūnāṃ madhye ye gurutāṃśritāḥ |
te patitvā mahābhūmau bhūyomṛdbhāvamāśritam [k: bhū * * * * * * *] || 17/40 
||
145k) etyantā [k: * * *] laghutāpannāsta evatra sareṇavaḥ |
atyaṃta sūkṣmadurlakṣā ghūrṇate vāyumaṇḍale || 17/41 ||
evaṃ mṛtpiṇḍakasyaiva vikārābahavaḥ [kh: -va] sthitāḥ [kh: -tā] |
anyonyaśabdanirvācyāsta āropāḥ [kh: -pa] prakīrttitāḥ || 17/42 ||
etat kenāpi vāñchanti sādhayantya pare priye |
tatrāropitarūpatvaṃ [k: -ro * * * * *] ghaṭādeḥ kārayanbudhaḥ || 17/43 ||
anyena tasya satyatva vādinā pratiṣidyate [kh: -dhyate] |
guṇāntarasamāsaṃga mātrādevakathaṃ mṛdi || 17/44 ||
ghaṭatvamaparaṅkiñcidāropya kathayet kutaḥ |
vṛthā [kh: vṛdhā] tasya samāropaṃ vṛthā [kh: vṛdhā] tasya punarlayaḥ || 17/45 ||
vedatva vivṛdhārope vairibhiḥ pratiṣidhyate |
īdṛśā eva kalahe hetavaḥ pratipāditāḥ || 17/46 ||
anyatrāpi ca biṃboṣṭhī śabdādaugatirīdṛśī |
varṇātmakāstu ye śabdāstatra nityatvamekakaḥ || 17/47 ||
anityatvaṃ parobrūtedvayostatrāpi yuktayaḥ |
ataḥ pravṛddhavairāṇāṃ vādināmabhivalgatām || 17/48 ||
svanvopasaṃgṛhītārtha [kh: svasvopa-] sādhakanyāyamiccatām [kh: -micchatām] 
|
prabhūtataradarpāṃdhāḥ pāṇḍitya grahadurdhiyām || 17/49 ||
svāṃgīkṛta matadviṣṭamatarandhragaveṣiṇām |
tadgaveṣaṇamārgeṇa tat samāna samādhinā || 17/50 ||
146k) svaśāstra randhrakathane viruddhavyavahāriṇām |
viruddhabhāṣaṇe jāti nigraha sthānakalpanaiḥ || 17/51 ||
pratijñā hāni mukhyaiścapīḍanā saṃbhṛtaṃ krudhām [kh: kṛdhāṃ] |
paścātkope sati galoccoṣakāraṇa [kh: -cchoṣakāraṇa] ghoṣaṇaiḥ || 17/52 ||
ahaṅkāreṇamahatāmuṣṭiyuddhaṃ ca manvatām |
punastena prakāreṇa keśākeśi [kh: -śī] kṛte raṇe || 17/53 ||
udvekajanake jāte punarvyāmṛśyabodhataḥ |
pādāt saṃvṛtavegānāṃ vādināṃdveṣaveśmasu || 17/54 ||
sthitvā kṛtairnnirābodhairadṛṣṭa [k: -bodaira-] prativedibhiḥ |
siddhāntitaiśca svamanaḥ śuddhairanyaiśca ninditaiḥ || 17/55 ||
matāntarairbahuvidhaiḥ svasamānaṃ kramaṃjanāt |
vyāmūḍhān kurvatāmeṣa teṣāṃ panthāniraṅkuśaḥ [kh: pandā-] || 17/56 ||
anumānasyāṅgabhūtā gautamenorarīkṛtā |
pramāṇāṃdhāḥ ṣoḍaśāye padārthaistairmahākālīḥ [kh: -kariḥ] || 17/57 ||
atovādena suśroṇi nā [kh: na] siddhāntaḥ prajāyate |
kalahaḥ kevalaṃ tatra duḥsamāpaśaracchataiḥ [kh: -daiḥ] || 17/58 ||
jātinigrahaghātena pādasyāyāti saṃkṣayaḥ [kh: ?] |
saṃprāptirūpo bhavitā tadā vāmena nirṇayaḥ || 17/59 ||
nai tat saṃbhavati prāyojātyāderapilakṣaṇe |
vivecanīye [kh: -niyye] saṃprāpte vāda eva pravarttate || 17/60 ||
147k) tasya vādasya kenasyāt saṃprāptirjāti nigrahaiḥ |
vādasya tu samāpto hi jātinigrahasiddhidā || 17/61 ||
jātinigraha siddhatve vādasya tu samāpanam |
anyonyāśraya doṣeṇa tena vādena darśanam || 17/62 ||
svāpanāmātrataṃtrāṇi śāstrāṇyevaṃ vidhāni tu |
parasparavyāhatāni na camatkārakāṇinaḥ || 17/63 ||
anyonyaṃ pratirābādhya [kh: -bodhya] bādhyabādhakabhedataḥ [kh: -bodaka-] |
anyonyamapramāṇatva khyāti vipramapattayaḥ [kh: viprati-] || 17/64 ||
tasmādetāni sarvāṇi tat tanmārgavivarttanam |
pramitānya parastatra pramāṇyaṃ nāvabudhyate  || 17/65 ||
vādināṃtadripūṇāṃ ca mahāhaṃkāraśālinām |
sannikṛṣṭārtha [k: -kṛṣṇarda] rūpatvaṃ tatra madhyastha dṛkprabhā || 17/66 ||
madhyastha pakṣayugalaṃ guṇadoṣavivecakaḥ |
tadvādi prativādyukta yuktipāṇḍitya tatvavit || 17/67 ||
sarvāndhiyāviśadayā śīlayanniti bhāṣate |
āyāta vādinaḥ sarve teṣāṃ ca prativādinaḥ || 17/68 ||
sthāpanā khaṇḍanā caiva [kh: ccheda] yuktidvārā same ime |
doṣoguṇaścaiva samoradhraṃ cāpi samaṃ smṛtam || 17/69 ||
yaścobhayoḥ samodoṣaḥ parihāro'pi tatsamaḥ [kh: vāsamaḥ] |
naikaḥ paryanuyoktavyaḥ tādṛgarthavicāraṇe || 17/70 ||
148k) naikasya doṣaghoṣaḥ [kh: -ṣa] syāt sarvasyāpi same krame |
ataḥ sāmya pradhānānāṃ [k: pradā * *] na [k: *] dveṣonyatra yujyate || 17/71 ||
pradhānasamatārūpaṃ śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] tat pratipādaye |
yat kiṃcit karmayā kāciddevatā yaccakiṃcana || 17/72 ||
vastu pūjā vidhau heturyaśca kaścanapūjakaḥ |
yaḥ kopi mokṣaḥ sarvatra śāstre sadṛśa īritaḥ || 17/73 ||
ataḥ karmakalāpādernānāvidhabhidājuṣaḥ [k: -nā * * bhi-] |
etat prakāratā pattau bhavatastu vyadhātkaliḥ || 17/74 ||
tasyā vai riṇamutsṛjya svasvaiḥ śālyagatakramaiḥ |
mamānaṃda maheśasya madhyasthasya [kh: -syā] bhavādṛśām [kh: bhava-] || 
17/75 ||
siddhāṃtamānaṃdamayaṃ sevato jñānarūpiṇam |
sarveṣvapi ca śāstreṣu jñānamānaṃdalakṣaṇam || 17/76 ||
vihāyakinnu vā rūpaṃ pratipannaṃ bhavaddhiyām |
vicāryamāṇenāstaiva [kh: -nāsyaiva] sarvavyavahṛtā vadhi || 17/77 ||
jñānādānaṃdavapuṣaḥ paramanyaṃ manīṣiṇaḥ |
jñānamānaṃdarūpaṃ tat sarvagaṃ sarvajātikam || 17/78 ||
aparicchedyavapuṣaṃ sevadvaṃtaṃ [kh: -dhvaṃtaṃ] manīṣiṇaḥ |
iti madhyastarūpasya svātmānaṃdamaheśi tuḥ || 17/79 ||
iti yuktavacomṛṣṭamanodoṣo manīṣiṇām |
tatheti pratipadyaṃte śāstraiḥ sahanijairnnavaiḥ || 17/80 ||
149k) tathā prabhṛti cārvaṃgī mahatyānaṃdadarśane |
aṃgatāṃ kilayātāni śāstrāṇi vividhānyapi || 17/81 ||
ataḥ [kh: ata] śrīlalitācakra bahiḥkoṣṭhapadāditā |
praveśaḥ kramataḥ pūjyāścārvākādinijāgamāḥ || 17/82 ||
yāni yāni bahiṣṭhāni tānitānyabalāni vai |
yāni yānyantarasthāni tānitā vudbalāni [kh: yud-] vai || 17/83 ||
athomahāgamasyāsya [kh: adho-] nānāmārgamahodadheḥ |
parivārārakṣakāśca guptyardāḥ [kh: -rthāḥ] śāstra saṃcayāḥ || 17/84 ||
svasvaceto vilāsena rūddhadādisamudgatāḥ |
śāstramārgabhavantyeva [kh: -mārgā-] vīramārgasya cāṅgatām || 17/85 ||
aṅgatvamapi caiteṣāṃ puruṣārthasya [k: * * * * *] hetutā [k: * * *] |
puruṣārthastu mukhyoyo mahāyāgopapādakaḥ || 17/86 ||
ghanarūpaṃ [kh: dhana-] cakorākṣi vyavahāra pravarttakaḥ |
puruṣārthaṃ padantyeke dharmārthaṃ [kh: dhamārthaṃ] smaramokṣagān || 17/87 
||
na tanmatammanohāri dharmomokṣaśca muktatā |
ānaṃdayogo hi paramatastat pratisādhakaḥ || 17/88 ||
puruṣaḥ puruṣārthaśca tasmād [kh: tasmā] dvitayameva na |
dharmaḥ khalu mahāyāgo dṛṣṭādṛṣṭa [kh: dṛṣṭhā-] phalapradaḥ || 17/89 ||
sa eva kāmomokṣopi bhogānaṃdamayatpataḥ |
sa eva mākṣopi śive parānaṃdamayatpataḥ || 17/90 ||
150k) ānaṃda [kh: -ndaṃ] brahmaṇorūpa [kh: -pam] iti vedavacasmṛtaiḥ |
dharmarūpī kāmarūpī mokṣarūpī ca pūruṣaḥ || 17/91 ||
tasyārdha [kh: -rtha] puruṣordhoyaṃ [kh: puruṣārthoyaṃ] dhanarūpo nigadyate |
dhanaṃkhalu maheśasya pūjāprathamasādhanam || 17/92 ||
na gaṃdhapuṣpadhūpādyā vināvītairnnasaṃbhavet |
nātyaṃta saurabhaṃ vastu na vā sūkṣmataraḥ paṭaḥ || 17/93 ||
na bhūṣaṇāninānaṃda kārīṇi [kh: -ṇī] ca sureśvari [kh: -rī] |
na phalāni ca hṛdyāni na pakvāni phalāni ca || 17/94 ||
na sādhyasādhakālokā [kh: sādhyaṃ-] na cā [kh: ca] rāmomanoramā |
dhanaṃ vinā na sidhyaṃti [kh: vināṃbasidhyanti] puruṣārthaḥ sa īritaḥ || 17/95 ||
tasmāt pūrvaṃ ya uktosau vādimadhyaḥ sa īśvaraḥ |
sa [kh: na] tu śāstrāṇi sarvāṇi nirarthānyapi nātyajat [kh: -jan] || 17/96 ||
sa ānaṃdamaheśānaḥ kalpayāmāsadehinām |
tarkādiśāstrābhyāsasya dhanadāyānidānatām || 17/97 ||
narajātereva dhanaiḥsaṃgrahaiśca prayojanam |
nānyajāti juṣojaṃtorāhārya [kh śṛṣojaṃto-] sulabhatvataḥ || 17/98 ||
utpannamātre bhūyiṣṭhā jātiranyā svapoṣiṇī [kh: -ṇi] |
narajātiriyantvetaiḥ saṃskṛtairātmapoṣiṇī || 17/99 ||
atomanujajātīnāmaśanammilanaṃ [kh: -melanaṃ] kṛtam |
duṣkaraṃ cāpi kaṃcodyat paraspara gaveṣaṇam || 17/100 ||
151k) tadgaveṣaṇamārgeṇa prajñātadhanavāṃcchanam |
atikaṣṭhataraṃ devi [kh: devī] bahuduḥkhaikabhājanam || 17/101 ||
tasmānnarāṇāmaśanot vādanāya [kh: pā-] maheśvaraḥ |
vīrāṇāṃ svātmapūjārtha dhanasaṃpādanāya ca || 17/102 ||
sarvāṇyapi ca śāstrāṇi rarakṣaviphalānyapi |
tadāpyamīgirisute [kh: tathāpya-] paravyāmoha hetave || 17/103 ||
vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa jalpantaḥ saṃcarantyaho |
sāṃkhyaśāstramahaṃ vediṃ [kh: vedmi] vediṃpātaṃ [kh: vedmi-] jalaṃ matam 
|| 17/104 ||
dīkṣatohaṃ sukarmosti nityamasmyagnihotravān |
māṃtrikohamahaṃ siddhaḥ paṇḍitohaṃ [kh: -ha] mahaṃ kaviḥ || 17/105 || 17/
ahaṃ tarkāgamajñosmi mīmāṃsāyāmahaṃ paṭuḥ |
ahaṃ vedārtha tatvajño yajñavidyākṣamosmyaham || 17/106 ||
smārttīcāsmimayājñāti [k: * * * * * * * *] jyotiṣāmayanaṃ [k: * * * * yanaṃ] 
punaḥ |
ṣaḍaṅgavedatatvajña stvahameva mahītale || 17/107 ||
ahaṃ kulīnosmi kule mamajñātā [kh: mavajñātā] manīṣiṇaḥ |
ābrahmaśuddhadāro'hamahamasmyā [kh: -smtyā] hi tuṇḍikaḥ || 17/108 ||
ahaṃ vedyastvahaṃ sarvaprāyaścitta kṛti kṣamaḥ |
ahṃ nādyarasajñosmi [kh: nāḍhya-] kāvyālāpeṣvahaṃ paṭuḥ || 17/109 ||
mahāprabaṃdha baṃdhāmāmahaṃ vedāṃtapāragaḥ |
iti garvoddhatairvākyaiḥ satyāsatya [kh: satyāḥ-] vimiśritaiḥ || 17/110 ||
152k) mohayaṃti nṛpānmūrkhān hitāhitapadacyutān |
dhaninomohadarpāṃdhā arcayaṃte vasubhiścataiḥ || 17/111 ||
tatastedhanidāttāni [kh: -dattāni] dhanānyādāya vidyayā |
kevalaṃ jagdhiyabdhyarthe mūrkhāḥ samadhicinvate || 17/112 ||
ānaṃdadevamīśānaṃ ye jānaṃti manīṣiṇaḥ  |
te tu tat tacchāstraniṣṭhāḥ pūjanārthaṃ dhanārjakāḥ || 17/113 ||
dhaninomohanārthāya śāstrāṇyetāni suṃdari [kh: -rī] |
guptārthāya [k: gurpardāya] ca hṛdyāṅgi na tu tatvāvabodhane || 17/114 ||
tatvāvabodha mārgastu varttate girinandini [kh: -nī] |
ānaṃdataṃtra evāsminnā paratra kvacinmataḥ || 17/115 ||
satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ satyamudhṛtyabhujamucyate |
tatvabhūtamidaṃ subhruyo mahāyāga īritaḥ || 17/116 ||
mahāyāga svarūpaśca taṃtresmin pratipāditaḥ |
asya taṃtrasya tatvāni siddhāntaśca pradarśyate [kh: -daśyate] || 17/117 ||
vināśo na tu kasyāpi guṇāntaragatiḥ param |
āvirbbhāvatirobhāvau varttete sarvavastuṣu || 17/118 ||
naitāvatot pattināśau vaktuṃśakyaumanīṣibhiḥ |
na devadatto bhavanānnirgacchan pūrvasaṅgataḥ || 17/119 ||
utpanna iti śabdena vyavahāraṃ kilārhati |
na devadatto bhavanāt praveśanatirohitaḥ || 17/120 ||
153k) vinaṣṭa iti śabdasya vāccatvamapi padyate |
tathā [kh: tadā] svajananī garbhād devadatto'bhijāyate [kh: -tto vijāyate] || 17/121 
||
na tatrot pattiśaṅkāpi saṃyojayitu marhati |
āvirbhāvaṃ vinotpattiranyākāpina vidyate || 17/122 ||
āvirbhāvatiriktatvamutpannatvaṃ yadiṣyate |
tadvicāre kṛte naiva cārutvamanudhāvati || 17/123 ||
na hi saṃsargamātreṇa guṇāntarbhavanena ca |
utpannatvaṃ viśiṣṭatvaṃ tadaivādau [kh: tathai-] pradarśitam || 17/124 ||
kālodikca nabhaścaiva śabdamātraikamūrttayaḥ |
na tu teṣāṃ vāstavatvaṃ vyavahāraikajanmanā || 17/125 ||
kālasyacarcane jāte kriyaiva pariśiṣyate |
diśaścacarcane deśo nabhasaścarcane marut || 17/126 ||
tasmācchabdaika śeṣatvameṣā parvatanaṃdini [kh: -nī] |
bhūmirāponalo vāyuḥ śabdaścatimiraṃ manaḥ || 17/127 ||
jīvaḥ śiva iti proktāḥ padārthāḥ parameśvari [kh: -rī] |
etad bhidaiva sarveṣāṃ padārthānāṃ vijṛmbhaṇam || 17/128 ||
etāni navaśeṣāṇi eṣā ekasyabhūrayaḥ |
saṅghatarūpakarmādyā na teṣāmasti gaṇyatā || 17/129 ||
anāśrayaṃ dvayaṃ tatra tatra saptakramāśrayam |
maruccatimirannaiva saṃścayaṃ [kh: saṃśrayaṃ] kvacidarhataḥ || 17/130 ||
154k) anyāni devi [kh: devī] śeṣāṇi svāśraya sthitimantyapi |
navadravyāṇi caitāni kīrttitāni mahāgame || 17/131 ||
śabdādiriktaṃ  rūpāṇi śabdānyanyāni sarvaśaḥ |
eteṣāmeva bhedena nānāsaṃkhyāṃ [kh: nanā-] vidhāya ca || 17/132 ||
tatvāni pratipannāni pratiśāstraṃ pṛthak pṛthak |
pratyakṣamānānubhavaḥ sarveṣveteṣu pārvati [kh: -tī] || 17/133 ||
bhūmirāpo'nalodhvāntaṃ netra pratyakṣagocaraḥ |
jaladhvānte śuklānile bhūśuciḥ śabalākṛti || 17/134 ||
śucestu sarvavarṇatvaṃ bhāsvaratvaṃ kilādhikam |
sparśaḥ [kh: sparśa] pratyakṣaviṣayaḥ pavanaḥ parvatātmaje || 17/135 ||
śrotrapratyakṣanigrāhyaḥ śabdaḥ kokilabhāṣiṇi [kh: -ṇī] |
manojīvavaśāḥ sarve pratipannāḥ [kh: -nnā] svayaṃ pramāḥ || 17/136 ||
pramāṇāni tridhoktāni śabdādyakṣānumātmanā |
tatra śabdastvāptavāco vedavidyāgamātmakaḥ || 17/137 ||
adhyakṣantu [k: adyakṣantu] dvidhābhinnaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaikadeśakam |
dṛṣṭaṃ pratyakṣameva [kh: -vaṃ] syāt dvitīyamanumāsmṛtā [kh: dvitīyā-] || 
17/138 ||
anumādhyakṣayornaiva [kh: anumādyakṣa-] vastutobhedakalpanā |
viśiṣṭabhāge sādṛṣṭe  darśanaṃ bhāginoyataḥ || 17/139 ||
yadyevaṃ na ghaṭādau ca pratyakṣācchedasaṃbhavāḥ |
dṛṣṭasarvasvarūpasya ghaṭasyādhyakṣatāsthi [kh: -dyakṣatāsthi] cet || 17/140 ||
155k) ghaṭasyā parabhāge tu netraraśme rasaṃkramāt |
dṛṣṭa sarvā kṛtitvasya ghaṭanaṃ na ghaṭe bhavat [kh: bhavet] || 17/141 ||
ato naivānayorbbhedo vāstavaḥ parikīrttitaḥ |
adhyakṣaśabdā [kh: adyakṣa-] ve vobhau pramāṇagaṇaśekharau [k: * * *gaṇa-] || 
17/142 ||
aṅgīkṛtā vihānaṃdataṃtre taralalocane |
tritvantu vyavahārasyāpyapekṣaivātra sammatam || 17/143 ||
tasmādadhyakṣabhāsyāni proktānyaṣṭau maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
navamaṃ śivatatvaṃ tu śabdabhāvyamihādhunā || 17/144 ||
yadā tu jīvanāthoyaṃ mahāyāgena sidhyati |
śabdabhāsyatvamujhitvā svādhyakṣeṇa sabhāsate || 17/145 ||
tatva vyavasthā [kh: vya * sthā] proktā sāvāśrayānāśrayau śṛṇu [kh: śruṇu] |
svāśrayo marudekasya [kh: mara-] udāsīnaṃ tamaḥ [kh: tama] smṛtam || 17/146 ||
śivavarjjaṃ saptakaṃ tu marudāśrayamiṣyate |
śivastu sarvāśrayaḥ [kh: -ya] syāt tatra samyagvivicyate || 17/147 ||
marutyeva mahīcakraṃ mahyāṃ [kh: mahyaṃ] ca pavane [kh: -naḥ] payaḥ |
mahyāṃ [kh: mahyaṃ] siddhādirūpeṇa vāyaumeghahimātmatā || 17/148 ||
tejo mahyāśritaṃ [kh: mahyaṃ -] jñeyaṃ tejaścoṣṇāti śāyitā |
śabdemaṃghījalamarut [kh: -maṃdhījala-] saṃśrayaḥ kathitaḥ priye || 17/149 ||
manojīvaśca devidvau saṃghātau śrīmatāṃgatau |
saṃghātaśca pṛthivyādeḥ [kh: -de] śarīratvena kīrtyate || 17/150 ||
tasmādbahuvidhaṃ [k: -vidaṃ] devi [kh: devī] sacinniścityatadvidhā |
prāṇena ceṣṭamānaṃ yat tat sacit kamihocyate || 17/151 ||
niścit kaṃtarugulmādi niṣprāṇaṃ karmasaṃgataḥ |
manaḥsaṃsrayaṇaṃ prāṇi sacit kavapuṣismṛtam || 17/152 ||
jīvasaṃśrayaṇaṃ devi [kh: devī] sa cinniścit kayoḥsamam |
sarveṣāmihatatvānāṃ vikṛtirguṇabhedajā || 17/153 ||
iṣṭaiva tat svabhāvena na tu paryanuyojitā |
saṃghātajāśca saṃghātā bahavonaikarūpiṇaḥ || 17/154 ||
mayāvaktuṃ na śakyaṃte pradhānānyuditāni te |
bahunā kimihoktena kintatvairaparairjaḍaiḥ || 17/155 ||
mahābale mahābhūte vāyutatve khilaṃ jagat |
acchedyo'yamadāhoya makledyośoṣya eva ca || 17/156 ||
nityaḥ sarvagatasthāṇuḥ [kh: -ṇur] dhārakobodhakopi ca |
sarvaviśvasya janakaḥ [kh: -ka] sarvaviśvasya pālakaḥ || 17/157 ||
viśvacakrasya saṃharttā viśālovimala prabhuḥ |
sa giridvīpajaladhikānanā sakalāmahī [kh: -mahi] || 17/158 ||
vāyau pratiṣṭhiṣṭhitāḥ sarvabhāradhāraṇalaṃpaṭe |
etā dṛśyastva saṃkhyātā vasudhāvāsavārcite || 17/159 ||
māruteśvara evāṃba varttamānā prakīrttitā |
jyotiścakraṃ samastaṃ ca vāyu naiva pracālyate || 17/160 ||
adhastādūrdhvamapi ca [kh: caḥ] sarvadā pravahanmarut |
sadā carati kutrāpi sadā ca sthimitaḥ kvacit || 17/161 ||
padāsthi [kh: yadāsti] mitatāṃ yāti tadā kha padabhājanam |
yadā pravahati kṣipraṃ saśabdena tatodyate || 17/162 ||
tasmādayamupāsyo hi yogibhiḥ [kh: -bhi] svātmasiddhaye |
etat tu tattvaṃ kathitamasyaivānyā vibhūtayaḥ || 17/163 ||
idamāśritya suśroṇi prayateta budhāhite |
tat tacchāstra sthitiḥ sarvā kathitā girinaṃdini [kh: -nī] || 17/164 ||
tatvasthitiśca sakalā kiṃbhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi [kh: -ti] |
 
ityānaṃdataṃtre saptadaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||




aṣṭādaśapaṭalaḥ

śrīdevyuvāca


deva deva mahādeva jagadānaṃdadāyaka |
anyatra mama yat kiṃcit praṣṭavyaṃ spṛṣṭamāśaye || 18/1 ||
jagataḥ kuta utpattiḥ [kh: -ttir] bhūbhūbhṛtsāgarātmanaḥ |
aneka jantujālena nibiḍasya nirantaram || 18/2 ||
aneka tarugulmādyairavatīrṇasya sarvataḥ |
vicitrairjātibhedaiśca vastubhiśca pṛthagvidhaiḥ || 18/3 ||
pāṣāṇamṛttikāgaṇḍa pāṣāṇādi tadātmanaḥ [k: tatātma-] |
dvacit salīlaviccharddaḥ [kh: savila-] kvaciccaiva [kh: kvacicchaiva] marusthalam || 
18/4 ||
evantu bahudhāviśvaṃ kuta utpannamīśvaraḥ |
kutra vā tiṣṭhati vibho kinnaśyati na naśyati || 18/5 ||
(156)169k) kimupādānakandeva jagaṃdetaccarācaram |
vividhairmatabhedairmedhī vyāmohaḥ pravarttate || 18/6 ||
etanme saṃśayaṃ chettuṃ bhavāne va paraṃpaṭuḥ |
tvadanyaḥ saṃśayasyāsya na cchettā kopiśaṃkara || 18/7 ||


kāmeśvaraḥ 


pravāhanityatā devi jagatosya prakīrttitā |
punarṇavanavākāraṃ pratnamapyavabhāsate || 18/8 ||
jantoḥ śarīra sā dharmyaṃ jagatopyasya suṃdari [kh: -rī] |
śarīrameva kasyāpi puṃsoviśvābhimāninaḥ || 18/9 ||
prāṇināṃ vigrahe ye ye bhāvāḥ saṃti pṛthagvidhāḥ |
te te bhāvāstatsamānā jagaddhe he vyavasthitāḥ || 18/10 ||
aṇḍe tat sakalaṃ cāpi piṇḍe yad varttate nṛṇām |
gurutvalaghutātvaṃ ca na bhedastvaṃḍapiṇḍayoḥ [k: bhedvastvaṇḍa-] || 18/11 ||
gajādīnāṃ śarīrasya maśakāditanorapi |
yadantaraṃ [k: * * * *] pramāṇena [k: * * * *] tadevahyaṇḍa [k: * * * * ḍā] 
piṇḍayoḥ [k: paṃḍayoḥ] || 18/12 ||
viśvaśaṃpratipannānāṃ vapuṣā yā dṛśī gataḥ [kh: gatiḥ] |
pravāhanityatā pāde tā dṛgevāṇḍagocaraḥ || 18/13 ||
devadattādidehāśca svabhāvena cirantanāḥ |
na nūtanaṃ pūrvamapisvaṃ pūrvākāramāśritam || 18/14 ||
pūrvākāraparākāra viśiṣṭatva bhidāvaśāt |
yadyekasmin devadatte bhedoste [kh: -sti] prakṛtepinaḥ [kh: -saḥ] || 18/15 ||
na tatra bhedoṅgi kāroloke kasyāpi vādinaḥ |
160k) kṣaṇikatvavidhoye tu mūrkhāste parikīrttitāḥ || 18/16 ||
devadattākhya yā yosau piṇḍolocana gocaraḥ |
tat pūrvākāratā garbhastat pūrvākāratorasaḥ || 18/17 ||
tat pūrvākārabhaktastu tat pūrvākāratoṣadhiḥ |
tat pūrvākāratā bījaṃ tat bījākāratauṣadhiḥ || 18/18 ||
bījāṃakura [kh: -karu] pravāho'yamūrdhvavattaḥ pradhāvati |
athānyopi pravāho me vākyā devāvabudhyatām || 18/19 ||
devadattaśarīrasya pūrvākārastu tat pitā |
tat pituḥ pūrvarūpantu tat pitā tat pituḥ paraḥ || 18/20 ||
ataḥ pravāhā kāreṇa devadattadinityatā |
pravāhāvayavākāra bhedenāpi ca nityatā || 18/21 ||
na cā vayavarāhitye'vayavīkopi vidyate |
yato'vayavi saṃghāto'vayavitvena gaṇyate || 18/22 ||
samūhānna tu bhedena samūhitvāpi dṛśyate |
yadi saṃdṛśyate kvāpi tatra śabdaikarūpatā || 18/23 ||
iyaṃ gatirdevadatta śarīrādi maheśvari [kh: -rī] |
iyamevagatiḥ [kh: -ti] sthūle viśvacakrepi yujyate || 18/24 ||
yajñadattaśarīreṇa tadgataiśca rasairapi |
tanniṣekasya sāmarthyāt tat patnīśoṇitairapi || 18/25 ||
saṃghātamapi saṃpādya garbhapiṇḍasvarūpiṇam | 
utpādite devadatto tadā tadvajjagat priye || 18/26 ||
asyāṇḍasya viśālasya viśleṣāvayavasyayaḥ |
māruteśvara sampanna saṅkalpakṣaya īritaḥ || 18/27 ||
bhūmyādiśeṣiṇāmatra svarūpaṃ bṛndameva ca |
satyaṃ [kh: sadaiva] sadaiva [kh: satyaṃ] suśroṇi śleṣāśleṣau [kh: -pau] tu 
bhedinau || 18/28 ||
yadā mārutasaṃparkāt bhūmyādyāśleṣaṇaṃ bhavet |
tadāṇḍarūpatā pattireṣā sṛṣṭiḥ samīritā || 18/29 ||
ayameveśvaro vāyurayameva śarīravān |
saṃyojayati bhūtāni viyojayati [kh: viyyojayadi] ca prabhuḥ || 18/30 ||
tejasāṃtamasāmeṣa cānyeṣāmapi vāsabhūḥ |
svataṃtra svaira saṃcāro nāsyādhikyena kaṃcana [kh: -nā] || 18/31 ||
yajjagadyāpyarūpaṃ [kh: -gadvyāpya-] syāt brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ |

karuṇā ce devameva bhavitavyaṃ maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 19/34 ||
anyathā [kh: anyadhā] tu haviṣprāśyatanniṣkrayamayaṃ [k: ha *?śya-] phalam |
yacchaṃtīnāṃ devatānāṃ vaṇijāmiva kākṛpā || 19/35 ||
172k) tasmāde te [k: nāsti] deva vādavādāmartyasamatvataḥ |
dehavatvamurīkṛtya kṛtā unmattajalpavat || 19/36 ||
devatānāmamūṣāntu svarūpaṃ [k: * * *] bhinnamucyete [k: * * * * *] |
asminnānaṃdataṃtre [k: * * nnā-] tu tatpaścāt pravivicyate || 19/37 ||
yat pūrvamuditaṃ kaiścid devatā svargasaṃśritāḥ |
havyagrahaṇakāle [kh: * vya-] tu gatvāyānti punastviti || 19/38 ||
taccāsamaṃjasaṃ [kh: -naṃ] vākyaṃ svargonāma purammahat |
yathātra bhūmivalaye grāmapallipurādikam [kh: -valli-] || 19/39 ||
tatrāpi bhūmirevāsti devajātistu kācana |
veṣābharaṇa ceṣṭāsu [kh: ceṣāsu] kiṃcidaujvalyaśālinī [kh: -jjalyaśālinī] || 
19/40 ||
martyajātyadhikatvena bhāsate tatra paṭṭane |
yadaivabhāratevarṣe yadā deśaṃ pṛthagvidhāḥ || 19/41 ||
vibhinnaceṣṭāḥ kāścicca bahavonarajātayaḥ |
na tāsu devatābhāvaḥ kopyastyanyonyamudgataḥ || 19/42 ||
tadvat suvipule bhūmi maṇḍale lakṣasātayaḥ |
vasanti vāmanayane nāntānyonyamupāsate [kh: nātrānyonya-] || 19/43 ||
na so'sti pṛthivīkhaṇḍe nā vṛtoyaḥ śarīribhiḥ |
sarvatrajātayaḥ santi nānāveṣakriyojvalāḥ || 19/44 ||
yadatyaṃta cirāyuṣṭvaṃ yannabhaścārasiddhayaḥ |
yacāpi nirnnimeṣatvaṃ yacca nāra [kh: sāra] śarīratā || 19/45 ||
173k) yaccānyaṃ narajātīnāṃ durlabhaṃ vastu kiṃcana  |
tat sarvaṃ tat tadurviṃ ca viśiṣṭauṣadhisevayā || 19/46 ||
auṣadhānāntu sarveṣāmacintyaguṇakāritā |
pūrvamevamayā proktā yadaivamaṇimaṃtrayoḥ || 19/47 ||
tasmānmanuṣyādhikya mātraṃ svarganivāsinām |
manvāmahe [kh: -ho] mahādevi [kh: -vī] devatātvaṃ na kiṃcana || 19/48 ||
teṣomerunivāsānāṃ [kh: teṣāmmeru-] narādhikyaṃtu tādṛśam |
tadbhūjavastu śaktyaiva na tu jāti svabhāvataḥ || 19/49 ||
bhāratādyeṣu khaṇḍeṣu tībraśaktyauṣadhāśinām [kh: -śaktyāṣadhāśinām] 
|
pratyakṣato na dṛśyante dīrghāyuṣṭvādi siddhayaḥ || 19/50 ||
tat phalaṃ viṣaye bhinne bhinnairapi ca vastubhiḥ |
bhinnajātairupāyaiśca na kathaṃ devi setsyati || 19/51 ||
atasteṣāṃ svarga sadāṃ vṛttānto nāvabudhyate |
nariaścanaravṛttaṃtaḥ [kh: -nta] surairvānānu budhyate || 19/52 ||
yathā dehetyaṃta sūkṣmā avijñāta [k: la-] parasparāḥ |
kimayaḥ saṃti tadvad bhūgole bhinnāścajātayaḥ || 19/53 ||
hiraṇmayāni sthānāni vṛkṣāvāṃcchitasiddhidāḥ |
sarvadā tṛptimantaśca yacca tatra divaukasaḥ || 19/54 ||
tatsarvaṃ bhūmigolasya deśa vai śiṣṭyaśaktitaḥ |
iyad bhūmirmahadbhūtaṃ viśālā sarvagarbbhiṇi [kh: -ṇī] || 19/55 ||
174k) nānājanāvaliyutā [k: nānā * * va-] nānāparvataśobhitā |
satvairbahuvidhairjjuṣṭā vahvībhirjātibhirvṛdhā [k: ba * bhir- -tā] || 19/56 ||
vāyumārge pracalatāṃ jale ca kṛtajanmanām |
sarveṣāmapi jantūnāmāśrayaṃ ca samāyuṣām || 19/57 ||
līlādhṛtabhujaṃgendra [kh: -dhrutabhujaṃ-] paryaṃkapariśāyini [kh: -nī] |
phaṇāmaṇḍalaparyaka [kh: paṇā-] nityakhelanabālikā || 19/58 ||
bahudhā parvatoddābhiḥ puṇyābhiḥ [kh: -bhi] svacchavāribhiḥ |
mahānadībhiruddāmā hārālibhirivojvalā || 19/59 ||
adhastādupariṣṭācca pravāhaśata bāhubhiḥ |
śliṣṭeva vāyunā tena śailālipulakākulā || 19/60 ||
nīlakārddamikeneva vasane nāti śobhitā |
tamālaśyāmale nābhorāśinā vidhṛtāṃbarā || 19/61 ||
antaḥ [kh: anta] sthitena gūḍhena suṣumnādaṇḍa rūpiṇā |
caturddaśajagatprātamūrtinā [kh: -prota-] meruṇāśritā || 19/62 ||
kvacit pāṣāṇa nibiḍā kvacid gulmasamākulā |
kvacid dūratarairdīrghairvīraṇaireva saṃvṛtā || 19/63 ||
kvaciccākṛṣṭapacyena sasyajālena saṃvṛtā |
kvacillohamayākārā kvacittāmramayā kṛtiḥ || 19/64 ||
kvacinnānādhāturūpā kvacidrūpyamayasthalī |
ilāvṛtādi varṣādau kvacanasvarṇarūpiṇī || 19/65 ||
175k) kvaciccajānudaghnaiśca [kh: kvaciccha-] tṛṇagulmādi varjjitaiḥ |
śilāśarkarilerdūraṃ [kh: -lairdūraṃ] vālukāmaṇḍalairyutā [kh: -
maṇḍalaurvṛtā] || 19/66 ||
ahorātra samākvāpi nyūnarātriḥ [kh: māsarātriḥ] kvacit kvacit |
daśanāḍīyutā kvāpi kvacinnyūnadinā [kh: -cinyūna-] smṛtā || 19/67 ||
kvacit tithibahutithā [kh: tidhī- -dhā] sūryālokanavarjitā |
ṣaṇmāsamātraṃ kvacanarātri saṃbhavavarjitā || 19/68 ||
kvacicca sarvadādṛsṭa sūryamaṇḍalabhāsvarā |
kvacit kākamukhairmartyaiḥ kvacidgṛdhramukhairnnaraiḥ || 19/69 ||
kvacit kapimukhaiḥ kvaciccaturagānanaiḥ |
iththaṃ bahuvidhākārā sarvādhāranijākṛtiḥ || 19/70 ||
kāṃ kāṃ jāti viśiṣṭānno samutpādayituṃ [kh: -pātayituṃ] kṣamā |
evaṃ samastabhūcakra vāsināṃ sarvadehinām || 19/71 ||
upāsyatvena santyeva devatā bahuśaktayaḥ |
maṃtrāśca santi [k: * *] bahavaḥ phalānivividhāni ca || 19/72 ||
bhaktiśraddhe ca tat kṛtye sarvasādhāraṇaṃ tvidam |
devānāṃ ca samānaiṣārītiḥ [kh: -ti] svarganivāsinām || 19/73 ||
śrutirbhagavatī tatra pramāṇaṃ girinaṃdini [kh: -nī] |
ṛcikāmāyaśaskāmā devā vai satramāsata || 19/74 ||
ityādiśrutirūpeṇa śābdamānena ca priye |
hṛdayaṃ gamayā śaktyātarkaiścanyāya karkaśaiḥ || 19/75 ||
176k) vicāryamāṇe dehitvaṃ devatānāṃ na yujyate |
tarhidaivāni [kh: tairhi-] sarvāṇi viśvarūpāṇi kiṃ vada || 19/76 ||
yaditānyasvarūpāṇi tāni tacchaśaśṛṃgavat |
śaśaśṛṅgasya yat kiñcidrūpaṃ kva pratipadyate || 19/77 ||
itīdamapi durvākyaṃ rūpaṃ devasya kathyate |
yathābhūmyaṃbu tejobhiḥ śabdena marutāpi ca || 19/78 ||
śarīraṃ bhautikaṃ sarvadehinā samupasthitam |
kvacit padārthairurvyādyaiḥ paṃcabhirnnirmalātmabhiḥ || 19/79 ||
saṃsiddhaṃ devatārūpa natvekena [k: tatve * na] vināpitat |
pratyaṃtabhāvanādārḍhyād devatā rūpamīdṛśam || 19/80 ||
samastakāmaphaladaṃ yajvanāṃ [k: * jvanāṃ] saṃprajāyate |
yāgāśca dvividhā proktā yajvanopidvibhedinaḥ || 19/81 ||
vedāgamādi saṃprotktāḥ [kh: -ktā] prakaṭāścāthagopitāḥ |
pauṇḍarīkaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ vājapeyādayaḥ priye || 19/82 ||
karmapradhānāḥ prakaṭāḥ suvāte phalamadvarāḥ [kh: -dhvarāḥ] |
śrīyāgaśyāmalā yāgaḥ śaṃbhuyāgastathā paraḥ || 19/83 ||
mahāvārttālikāyāgaḥ parāyāgastathā paraḥ |
heraṃbayāgo gopālayāgaḥ sūryamakhastathā || 19/84 ||
ete tvāgamikāyāgā mahādhīraniṣevitāḥ |
bhuktipradhānāḥ suśroṇi gopitāḥ parikīrttitāḥ || 19/85 ||
yaṣṭāro'pi dvidaivasyuḥ [kh: -syur] vā japeyādi yājinaḥ |
prakaṭā śrī makhādīnāṃ yaṣṭārogopitāḥ [kh: -tā] smṛtāḥ || 19/86 ||
177k) prākaṭyo naiva tat siddhirguptyācaiṣāṃ [kh: siddhi-] tu siddhayaḥ |
prāmāṇyamubhayatrāpi viśeṣastatra kathyate || 19/87 ||
sukhatṛṣṇāniviṣṭānāṃ [kh: -nā] martyahaṃkāraśālinām |
rājasaprakṛtīnāṃ tu prakaṭeṣṭaḥ prathiyyasi [kh: pradhīyasi] || 19/88 ||
ānaṃdarūpe vimale brahmaṇyālagnacetasām |
antarmukhānāṃ vīrāṇāṃ matyaṃtaunnatyakāṃkṣiṇām || 19/89 ||
śvetī kṛtajagaccakra kīrti sphūrtyabhilāṣiṇām |
gupteṣṭireva niyataṃ phalasaṃpatti kāṃkṣiṇī || 19/90 ||
iṣṭireṣādvidhā sarva devatā sphūrttidāyinī |
tad devatā svarūpaṃ ca paṃcavastvekatātmatā || 19/91 ||
teṣāṃ tu paṃcavastūnāṃ prapaṃcaḥ pravivicyate |
gururvidyā dhyāna kḷptirmanaśca [kh: kluptir-] pavanastathā || 19/92 ||
etadaikyaṃ devatā yā mūrttiradrikumārike |
svāhāṃta maṃtramātraṃ [kh: -māttraṃ] tu devatā prakaṭādvare [kh: -dhvare] || 
19/93 ||
atomīmāṃsakāḥ sarve karmaprādhānyavādinaḥ |
caturthyaṃtaḥ śabda eva devateti sphuṭaṃ jaguḥ || 19/94 ||
guptādhvare tu na tathā devatā sthitiriṣyate |
gurvādivastu saṃbheda eva devatavigraham || 19/95 ||
gurvādyāḥ paṃcabhāvā ye bhāvanābhūṣitā yadi |
te hi siddhaṃ prayacchaṃti te tu nirbhāvanā mudā || 19/96 ||
178k) ato vadāmilolākṣi gurubhāvanalakṣaṇam |
dvādaśāṃta sarojāṃtaḥ karṇikātryaśamaṇḍale [kh: -śramaṇḍale] || 19/97 ||
akathādyarṇa vinyāsa prasaratkānti saṃpadi |
tanmadhye sahalakṣārṇahaṃ sadvyakṣarapīṭhikām || 19/98 ||
athyāsīnau [kh: adhyā-] mṛdusnigdhau nakhanirgata caṃdrikau |
tadaharjātapādābhau navapallavakomalau || 19/99 ||
samastacaitanya mayau sarvānaṃdasudhābhṛtau |
raktaśuklamayākārau raktaśuklanijatviṣau || 19/100 ||
caraṇaucintayennityaṃ gurunāthasya sannidheḥ |
gurubhāvanametat tu  mantrabhāvanamucyate || 19/101 ||
ādvādaśāntamāmūlaṃ taṭitkoṭa [kh: -ṭi] kaḍārabhām |
ratnastaṃbhasamākārā dhāladhalya [kh: thālathalya] sphurat tanuḥ || 19/102 ||
śārīramalatūlāni nirdahanti muhurmmuhuḥ |
ādivarṇaṃ samārabhya yāvanmaṃtrākṣarāvadhi || 19/103 ||
maṃtramūrttiṃ sadādhyāyedeṣāsyānmaṃtrabhāvanā |
pādukopari vidyāsyād adhastādekatātayoḥ || 19/104 ||
ūrdhvāṃgādhoṃgavatkāya [kh: -gāthoṃ-] ekasminneka ucyate |
tasmācchrī [kh: -cchre] deśikāṃghreśca maṃtramūrtteścanobhidā || 19/105 ||
dhyānakliptiṃ pravakṣyāmi tejastaṃbhe mahojvale |
ādvādaśāntamunnamre hṛtsthāne kamalaṃ smaret || 19/106 ||
179k) upariṣṭācca kamalaṃ amṛtaṃ syaṃdavaddalam |
tanmadhye dhyānamiṣṭārtha padaprāptikaraṃ bhajet || 19/107 ||
dhyānabhedāstu bahavo vedāgamanirūpitāḥ |
ekavaktrebhujadvaṃdvaṃ kvacid bāhucatuṣṭayam || 19/108 ||
kvacit ṣaḍbhujatā caiva kvaciccāṣṭabhujākṛtiḥ |
kvacid yadā śirṣasaṃkhyā [kh: -khyaṃ] tadvaiguṇyena bāhavaḥ || 19/109 ||
śivopaśliṣṭadeveṣu triṇetrā iṃduśekharāḥ |
viṣṇūpaśliṣṭa deveṣu dhyānabhedād vilocanāḥ || 19/110 ||
caturbbhujāśca śuśroṇi śaktyupaśliṣṭadaivate |
dhyānabhedāt stanānamramadhyāścandranibhānanāḥ || 19/111 ||
śṛṃgāraveṣa [kh: śriṃ -] saṃpannā nānābharaṇabhūṣitāḥ |
atiprasannāḥ karttavyāḥ madāruṇavilocanāḥ || 19/112 ||
eteṣu dhyānamekaṃ tu pūrvasthānaṃ vinirdiśet |
dhyāna kḷpteriyaṃ [kh: klapti-] proktā bhāvanasāravigrahā || 19/113 ||
manasāmarutasyāpi yadaikyaṃ sarvasiddhidam |
tat pūrvamevagaditaṃ mayāmadhurabhāṣiṇi [kh: -ṇī] || 19/114 ||
uktasya punarullekhaḥ vṛdhātaṃtrasya vistaraḥ |
evaṃ gurvādibhiḥ paṃca padārthairbhāvanānvitaiḥ || 19/115 ||
jāyate devatārūpaṃ bhaktadehe vyavasthitam |
maṃdaṃ madhyaṃ ca tīvraṃ ca tatredhābhaktavarṣaṇī [k: tattre-] || 19/116 ||
180) alpārādyaṃ madhyamaṃba devatā rūpamucyate |
pūrvajanmakṛtān doṣān ihajanmasthitāngatān || 19/117 ||
ādyaṃ tu devatā rūpamavakṛntati pārvati [kh: -tī] |
vācāṃsiddhiṃ vacaḥ siddhiṃ cirāyuṣyaṃ mahadyaśaḥ || 19/118 ||
etādṛśaṃ tadātyaṃba madhyamaḥ devatā vapuḥ |
svayaṃ śivatvānubhavaṃ svecchayābhāvakalpanam || 19/119 ||
akarttuṃ cānyathā [kh: -dhā] karttuṃ karttuṃ śaktiśca sarvataḥ |
saṃkalpasiddhidāṃ sarvalokānugrahakāritām || 19/120 ||
īṣatkopena viśvasya kṣaṇād dahanadakṣatām |
īṣatprasāde nīcānāmapyaindra padadāyitām || 19/121 ||
sarvatatvāvabodhatvaṃ mahāyāgaikaśīlatām |
tīvraṃ tu devatā rūpaṃ vitaratyadri kanyake || 19/122 ||
ādye tu daśavarṣāṇi madhyame viṃśatiḥ samāḥ |
tīvre tu devatā rūpe triṃśadvarṣāṇyu pāsate || 19/123 ||
ityevaṃ devatā tatvaṃ pūrvapakṣanirāsataḥ |
siddhāṃti taṃ maheśāni tadetat svātmasāt kuru || 19/124 ||
idaṃ rūpamurīkṛtya bhaktānpālayabhāvakān |


ityānaṃdataṃtre ekonaviṃśatiḥ paṭalaḥ [kh: -titamaḥ] ||



viṃśati

atha vakṣyāmi deveśi vidyārūpaṃ vilāsinī |
yadrahasya parijñānāt [kh: -nād] vidyāḥ sarvāśca siddhidāḥ || 20/1 ||
vakṣyamāṇamidaṃ vidyā svarūpasya nirūpaṇam |
ye dhārayanti manasā te bhaktābhāgyaśālinaḥ || 20/2 ||
181k) vidyāviśiṣṭaśabdātmā viśeṣaḥ śabdagocaraḥ |
svarūpataḥ śaktipātāt svabhāvena samāgatān [kh: -tāt] || 20/3 ||
śaktipātaḥ śivākāraḥ sarvatrāpi kvacit kvacit |
kiṃcid ratnamayaṃ vastu pratibiṃba na laṃpaṭam || 20/4 ||
yatrorvyāṃ sarasaṃvastu śaktipātotra pārvatī |
yadratnādyajaḍaṃ [k: ya * tnā-] tatrāpyevaṃ tāmarasānane || 20/5 ||
anyatrānirmalatvena śaktipātotra [kh: -na] varttate |
athāṃbu vastuni preśyaḥ śaktipātaparākramaḥ || 20/6 ||
kiṃcijjaḍamatikṣāraṃ kiṃcidvaryamṛtopamam [kh: -dvārya-] |
śaktipātaṃ vinātatra mādhuryasya kva saṃbhavaḥ || 20/7 ||
kiṃcitejastamaḥ prāpya svātmanaṃ tat [k: *] prakāśayet |
khadyotatārakāmukhyā kiṃcit tejastamoharaḥ || 20/8 ||
viśvasya bhāsakaṃ sarvadvīpamāṇikya dīpavat |
mārttāṃḍabhairavākāraṃ pratāpairatiduḥ saham || 20/9 ||
uḍubhyastena caṃḍāṃśo ratyugradyuti saṃbhavaḥ |
hetunāśaktipātena vinānaiva hi dṛśyate || 20/10 ||
māruteśakti pātastu prāṇāpānādibhedite |
ekonīlaḥ prāṇaharaḥ prāṇadaḥ kaścanāparaḥ || 20/11 ||
ekomarujjagaccakra samūlonmūlanakṣamaḥ |
ekomarutkṣaṇādeva jagadghaṭanalaṃpaṭaḥ || 20/12 ||
182k) śaktipātaṃ vinānaiva [kh: -neva] dṛksāhāmāruteśvare |
tadvacchakteḥ [kh: -kte] śaktipātona sarvatra kvacit kvacit || 20/13 ||
santyeva bahavaḥ śabdāsteṣāṃ rūpaṃ pracakṣmahe |
yathā bhumyādikāṃśānāṃ saṃsārogaṇanādhikaḥ [kh: -thikaḥ] || 20/14 ||
tadvacchabdasya saṃsārogaṇyatāṃ nāvagāhate |
samudrabheriyaṃ poṣṭaghuṭikā [kh: poṭṭa-] jhallarīmukhaiḥ || 20/15 ||
vādyaiḥ kṛtāstu ye śabdāḥ [kh: śabdā] na teṣāmihakīrtanam |
varṇātmakāstu ye śabdā nityāḥ sarvagatāścate || 20/16 ||
pṛthak [kh: pṛthag] dravyatayā hetu na guṇāḥ kasyacinmanāḥ |
sarvagā apidṛśyaṃte [kh: lipidṛśyaṃte] nābhivyakterapekṣayā || 20/17 ||
abhivyaktaścatālvoṣṭha puṭavyāpāragocarā [kh: -vāragocarā] |
nityatvaṃ ca maheśāni pratyabhijñā pramāṇataḥ || 20/18 ||
ya yeva devadattena cāritastu varaśrutaḥ |
sa evāṃgaṃ gakārotrayajñadattena ghuṣyate || 20/19 ||
pratyabhijñā pramāṇaṃ hi pratyakṣānubhavo hi sau [kh: hyasau] |
tasmāt sarvepyamīśabdāḥ nityāḥ sarvagatā api  || 20/20 ||
paśyaśabdasya saṃsāraṃ paṃcāśadvarṇamātrataḥ |
utpannasya dṛśorāśiryasya [kh: -śirpyasa] brahmāṇḍamapyaṇu || 20/21 ||
bhūmyaṃbvagni nabhovācāṃ [kh: -tāṃ] saṃsārasyāti bhūtitā |
uttarottaramatyaṃta vitatā vīravaṃdite || 20/22 ||
183k) adyāpi varddhito śabdorāśirājīvalocane |
śabdabrahmaṇi niṣṇātaḥ paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 20/23 ||
catuṣṭayī sa śabdānāṃ pravṛttiḥ parameśvarī |
tājikriyāguṇadravya bhedādbhinnāsahasradhā || 20/24 ||
gauraśvaḥ puruṣohastī [kh: -sti] śakunibrāhmaṇomṛgāḥ |
vṛkṣolatāḥ [kh: -tā] tṛṇaṃ ceti śabdāderjjātivikriyāḥ [k: -de * * vi-] || 20/25 ||
kapilaṃ ca kaḍāraśca nīlaḥ śoṇaśca pāṇḍaraḥ |
dhūmraḥ pītaḥ śyāmalaścāpyete śabdaguṇoththitāḥ || 20/26 ||
bhūmyaṃbvagni marucchabdādhvāṃtaṃ cetastathendriyam |
ghaṭaḥ paṭaśca kuḍyaṃcetyetādravyagatāgiraḥ || 20/27 ||
 īdṛk caturvidhaskaṃdha baṃdhūraṃ śabdasāgaram |
āgalāṃtaṃ mayā pītvā vamyatīti prakārataḥ || 20/28 ||
prabhuḥ sammita ekasyādanyakomitrasammitaḥ |
kāminī sammitaścānyaḥ [kh: -nya] strīprakārohyayaṃ smṛtaḥ || 20/29 ||
prabhusammitaśabdau ghovedarāśirmaheśvari [kh: -rī] |
āgamo'pi ca tat sārabhūtaḥ kamalalocane || 20/30 ||
vedāgamottamarthaṃ tu rājājñāmivamānavaḥ |
asaṃdihānaḥ saṃdehāt pratyavāyaṃ vicintayan || 20/31 ||
hetūna vimṛśanneva tarkkayanna hitāhite |
gṛhṇātyeva nirātaṃkaḥ [kh: -kas] tena sidhyati vartmanāt [kh: -vān] || 20/32 ||
ato vedāgamātmāsau śabdo hi prabhusammitaḥ |
suhṛtsammitaśabdastu suhṛdvākya vaducyate || 20/33 ||
184k) itihāsa purāṇādi tatraivaṃ mānavaṃ prati |
paurāṇikairabhihitaṃ vyāsādyairmaṃtraśālibhiḥ || 20/34 ||
rāmādivadvarttitavyaṃ na punārāvaṇādivat |
dharmaputravadekaiva na tu duryodhanādivat [kh: dhuryo-] || 20/35 ||
ityekayā kathābhaṃgyā rucimutpādyamitravat |
upadeśaṃ diśatiyaḥ sarātirmitrasammitaḥ || 20/36 ||
kāṃtāsammitaśabdastu kāvyālaṃkāranāṭakam |
samastamapi saṃgītaṃ bharatādiprakalpitam || 20/37 ||
sa kāntosammitorāśiḥ kānteva sarasatvataḥ |
alaṃkārairgaṇaścāpi ritibhirvṛttibhistathā || 20/38 ||
śaiyābhedaiśca [k: * * bhe-] pākaiśca dhvanibhiścāti saṃkhyakaiḥ |
abhidhālakṣaṇāgauṇa vṛttyāvaktroktibhistathā || 20/39 ||
lāṭacchekādyanuprāsaiḥ [kh: -sair] gadyaḥ padyaiścacaṃbubhiḥ |
vividhairhṛdayāṃ hlādajanakaiḥ kāvyavartmabhiḥ || 20/40 ||
pramodamupanīyaiva vidagdhevavilāsinī [kh: vidagdhaiva-] |
hitopadeśaṃ [k: hito * * *] tanute [k: * * *] yadyat [k: * *] strītvena [k: * * *] 
sammatā [k: * * *] || 20/41 ||
etaistribhiḥ [k: * * * *] prakāraistu [k: * * raistu] śābdaṃbrahmapravarttate |
na śabda brahmajaladhau śaktipātastu sarvataḥ || 20/42 ||
ānupūrvaviśiṣṭeṣu keṣu cicchabdarāśiṣu [kh: vi-] |
maṃtrabhāvoguḍākeśi ratnatvamiva pārthive || 20/43 ||
mādhuryamivato yeṣu pratāpa ivayāmasu |
ānupūrvāccanaivetya [k: -pūrvyācca-] vedāgamagatematau || 20/44 ||
yadyapyanyatraśabdeṣu śabdānāmaeva nityatā [kh: -tau] |
185k) ānupūrvāsya nityatvaṃ na tathā [kh: +tra] maheśvarī || 20/45 ||
śabdānāmānu pūrvye ca nityatvaṃ maṃtrarāśiṣu |
ānupūrvaṃ viśvadhane maṃtrāniḥ śaktayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 20/46 ||
tasmādīśāni maṃtrāṇi niyatānukrama spṛśam |
śaktipātasvabhāvena varttate vārijekṣaṇe || 20/47 ||
te cātra nilayāmaṃtrā nānyatra kvacidaṃbike |
abhivyakti punasteṣāṃ deśikeṃdropadeśitaḥ || 20/48 ||
viśvāsabhaktyupāstīnāṃ saṃghāte nāpi pārvati [kh: -tī] |
atostvamabhivyaktāśi abhivyaktasvaśaktayaḥ || 20/49 || 20/[kh: nāsti]
sarvāḥ siddhiḥ prayacchaṃti sarvadoṣanikṛṃtanāḥ |
anādya vidyāmālinyatamaḥ kajjalamārjjane || 20/50 ||
navīnanalinī nātha raśmayomaṃtrarāśayaḥ |
saptākūpārato yānāṃ [kh: yāna] gaṇyaṃte pṛṣatānyapi || 20/51 ||
na punargaṇyatāṃ yāṃti koṭiśomaṃtrarāśayaḥ |
sarveṣāmapi maṃtrāṇāṃ sraṣṭāvedeṣu saṃgatāḥ || 20/52 ||
tatopi vaiṣṇavāmaṃtrāḥ paṃcarātremahattarāḥ |
tato'pi maṃtraḥ saurāḥ [kh: -rā] syuḥviryavaṃto [kh: syur-] vilāsinī || 20/53 ||
tato'pi gāṇāpatyāstu maṃtrāḥ siddhi pradāyakāḥ |
tato'pi daurgāmaṃtrāstu dūrgatidhvaṃsa tat parāḥ || 20/54 ||
tao'pi śāṃkarāmaṃtrāḥ tato maṃtrāḥ [kh: -trā] śriyaḥ punaḥ |
tatopi maṃtrā varyāsyuḥsarvasvatyabhidhāyakāḥ [kh: paryāsyuḥ-] || 20/55 ||
tatopi śaktā maṃtrāstu mahodayavivarddhanāḥ |
186k) tataḥ pūrvāmnāyabhavā maṃtrā vīryamahattarāḥ || 20/56 ||
tato'pi dakṣiṇāmnāya maṃtrāḥśaktidhuraṃdharāḥ |
tebhyo'pi paścimāmnāya maṃtraśreṇirmahīyasī [kh: -hiyyasi] || 20/57 ||
tatopi cottarāmnāya maṃtrā uddāmabhāgyadāḥ |
tāsu vidyā svakūpārāsthānīyāsu [kh: -niyyāsu] maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 20/58 ||
śrīcakragocarāmaṃtrāḥ [kh: śrīcakrā-] prabalāḥ siddhivarddhanāḥ |
teṣvapyudagrāvārttālī maṃtrāḥ siddhipradāyakāḥ || 20/59 ||
te ṣvapi śyāmalāmaṃtrāḥ sāṃdrānaṃdasamṛddhidāḥ |
tato'pi lalitādevyo daśabhedasamudbhavāḥ || 20/60 ||
mahāmaṃtrā jagaccakra sārasaṃgraharūpiṇaḥ |
te ṣvapidve [kh: -vidye] mahāvidye saiveyaṃ saṃprakīrttitā [kh: sa-] || 20/61 ||
sihmāsana sthitaṃ yadvat saṃmrājamaparenṛpāḥ |
sevaṃte svasvarakṣārthaṃ svaśakteścāpi labdhaye || 20/62 ||
tathā vidyāmimāṃhṛdyāṃ siṃhāsanamaheśvarīm |
sevaṃte sakalā vidyā vedāgamamahormayaḥ || 20/63 ||
sarvāṇyapi ca śāstrāṇi sarvopyāgamasāgaraḥ |
sarvavedāśca sarvāṇi purāṇānimaheśvari [kh: -rī] || 20/64 ||
imāmeva mahāvidyāṃ praśaṃsaṃti pade pade |
guptatvena viviṃcaṃti natajjānaṃti [kh: nata jñānaṃti] durddhiyaḥ || 20/65 ||
anyathā [kh: -dhā] padamārgoyamanyathālokavarttanam [kh: -dhāloka-] |
na jānaṃti tamomūḍhā lalitāṃ vedamātaram || 20/66 ||
iyaṃ dvjānāṃ sāvitrī gāyatrī brahmavādinām |
sarasvatī kavīṃdrāṇāṃ yogināṃ cāpi bhairavī || 20/67 ||
187k) śāṃtā pāśupatākhyānāṃ jainānāṃ śrīriyaṃ smṛtā |
padmāvatīyaṃ bauddhānāṃ tārā ca tarale kṣaṇe || 20/68 ||
iyaṃ śaktiścaśāktānāṃ cārvākānāṃ tu tātvikā |
mahārājñi ca [kh: -jñiśca] saurāṇāṃ śraddhādruhiṇasevinām || 20/69 ||
durgābhūmi bhujāmeṣā vijayavyavasāyinī |
kimanyataskarāṇāṃ ca luṇṭikādevatā smṛtā || 20/70 ||
ānaṃdāmnāyavīrāṇāṃ [kh: -ṇā] sākṣātripurasuṃdarī |
yodyadāditya saṃkāśā sa cā pekṣu sṛṇīguṇā || 20/71 ||
tryaśrācaṃdrakalottaṃ sāsaiveyaṃ parameśvarī |
nāṃdīyāgasya paṭale yāni proktāni suṃdari [kh: -rī] || 20/72 ||
sudhāratnākaradvīpa mukhyāṇikalabhāṣiṇī |
tānisarvāṇi deveśī śrīcakranagaraṃ [kh: * *cakra-] smṛtam || 20/73 ||
tatra kāmeśvaro deva samrāṭ saubhāgya śevadhiḥ [kh: se-] |
sāmrājñī [kh: -gni] tatra lalitā pradānā śyāmalāṃbikā || 20/74 ||
duṣṭanigrahaśiṣṭānu pālinī purarakṣikā |
devī kroḍamukhī sarvaśatruraktāsavonmadā || 20/75 ||
rājyamānaṃda rūpaṃ syāt śrīcakraṃ [k: nāsti] rājadhānikā [k: nāsti] |
madhurudhyāna [k: nāsti] pālasyāt [k: nāsti] bālarājakumārikā [k: bālā-] || 
20/76 ||
sāmaṃta nṛparūpāsyuḥ [kh: -syur] nityāṃ kāmeśvarī mukhāḥ |
kāmeśvaryādikasta śrīdevyo rājasuhṛṃttamāḥ || 20/77 ||
pradhāna kiṃkarī devā catasraḥ śastradevatāḥ |
vaśinyādyāstu vāgdevyaḥ kavayaścārucāṭavaḥ || 20/78 ||
sarvajñādi mahādevyo rājadhānistha paṇḍitāḥ |
188k) sarvasiddhyādikā maṃtra [kh: maṃtrā] vicārapaṭumaṃtriṇaḥ || 20/79 ||
sarvasaṃkṣobhiṇī mukhyāstīvrā ājñā bhaṭāṃganāḥ |
bhukteṣu dīyamānasya [kh: diyya-] sauṃdaryadhanaveśmanaḥ || 20/80 ||
rakṣaṇāt kośapālyaṃsyu rahasyakusumādayaḥ [k: raha * ku-] |
kāmākarṣiṇikā mukhyā navaśītarucaḥ kalāḥ || 20/81 ||
mahādevī ratāṃtottha [kh: -toddha] khedanodana ceṭikāḥ |
sarvasaṃkṣobhiṇī mudrā mukhyāmudrānava priye || 20/82 ||
iṣṭvāhāna visṛṣṭyādi karmakalpanadāsikāḥ |
aṇimādyā mahādevi [kh: -vī] vinodāyendrajālataḥ || 20/83 ||
krīḍāṃ bahuvidhāṃ devī kurvatyaḥ siddhayoṣitaḥ |
brahmādyāstu mahābhoge saṃtataṃ sevituṃ purāḥ || 20/84 ||
svasvaśakti svarūpaṃ tu brahmādi pratisevakā |
caturdaśajagatyaṃba proccitāḥ [kh: -tā] saudhabhūmayaḥ || 20/85 ||
iyaṃ samastalokānāmadhiṣṭhātri maheśvarī |
evaṃ prakāśitā vidyā sihmāsana maheśvarī || 20/86 ||
yena sāmrājya padavī tasyā maṃtreṣu vidyate |
tasmāt sihmāsanākhyeva triṣulokeṣu viśrutā || 20/87 ||
asyā api mahādevyā hṛdaye kṛtasannidhiḥ |
ānaṃdavallividyeyaṃ samastānaṃdagarbhiṇī || 20/88 ||
tat svarūpamiyaṃ vidyā natayoḥ kvāpibhinnatā [kh: kāpi-] |
ānaṃdadevī lalitā lalitānaṃdabhairavī || 20/89 ||
ekameva paraṃtattvaṃ mithyāsau bhedaḍaṃbaraḥ |
189k) tasmādānaṃda vidheyaṃ [kh: vidyeyaṃ] mahāyāgaikamūlabhūḥ || 20/90 
||
sarvatatvamayākārā sarvānaṃdamayākṛtiḥ |
samastaśāstrasaṃbodhyā vīravaiśvātmadāyinī || 20/91 ||
sādhakānāṃ samastānāṃ smṛtimātreṇa siddhidā |
cintyamānaivamadhurā jalpyamānaivasidhyadā [kh: -ṇaiva-] || 20/92 ||
smaryamāṇaiva pāpaghnī vāryamāṇaiva dharmatā [kh: -dā] |
nānayāsadṛśaṃ kiṃcit [kh: kiṃci] triṣulokeṣu vidyate || 20/93 ||
idameva hi pāṇḍitya miyameva vidagdhatā |
yadānaṃdamahāvidyāmavadhatte sakṛtpumān || 20/94 ||
vṛttinna sarvasaṃdeho bhrāṃtadṛṣṭivirvajitaḥ [kh: -dṛṣṭir-] |
samasta saubhāgya yutosiddhimān [k: -man] sakṛdathate [kh: sakṛdedhate] || 20/95 ||
yāgarājena yaṣṭavyamānaṃdamayamujvalam |
paraṃdhāmāpicidrūpaṃ yato liṃga samāśritam [kh: -ta] || 20/96 ||
tasmālliṃga maheśāni pujyate yogibhiḥ sadā |
amarāśca surāyakṣā rakṣogaṃdharva kinnarāḥ || 20/97 ||
mahārājikasādhyāśca rudrāśca vasavonilāḥ |
niliṃpakāśca pramathāḥ [kh: -dhāḥ] sarvedevā maheśvari [kh: -rī] || 20/98 ||
nānāścabhogino vyālāḥ paśavaḥ pakṣiṇastathā |
siṃhājagājāśca śārdūlāḥ śarabhāśca tathā mṛgāḥ || 20/99 ||
caturāśīti lakṣeṣu yonibhedeṣu codgatāḥ |
prāṇaspaṃdana cinmātrā rūpāḥ sarve vijātayaḥ || 20/100 ||
pūjayantyeva yatsatyaṃ [kh: -tya] mamaliṃgamaheśvari [kh: -rī] |
tasmāt pārvati hastānme pādānmūrdhastathorasaḥ [kh: -mūrghnas-] || 20/101 ||
190k) āsanāt kaṇṭhadeśācca madhyādārohatastathā |
atyaṃtamuttamaṃ liṃgaṃ [k: * *] tadetanmama [k: * * * *] pūjyate [k: * * *] || 
20/102 ||
sarveṣveva [k: * * * *] maṭreṣu [k: * * *] sarvaiścāpi [k: * * * *] mahātmabhiḥ |
adhyāpi paśyadeveśi mamaliṃgaṃ hi pūjyate || 20/103 ||
malliṃga pratimāṃ kṛtvā svayaṃbhūkalpanādibhiḥ |
svayaṃbhubāṇaliṃgaṃ ca tathā sphaṭka liṃgakam || 20/104 ||
ratnaliṃgaṃ ca sikatā liṃgaṃ sauvarṇaliṃgakam |
pārthivaṃ liṃgamīśāni tathā [kh: tadā] rājata liṃgakam || 20/105 ||
evameva prakāreṇamalliṃga pratimāśatam |
samastaiḥ pūjyate devi sarvasaubhāgya kāmukaiḥ || 20/106 ||
etat te sarvamākhyātam etad yaḥ paṭhati priye |
etad yacca śṛṇotyaṃba [kh: śruṇo -] etad yaccāpi naṃdati || 20/107 ||
etadyaḥ pūjayatyaṃtaḥ pustakelikhitākṣaram |
etaccavastu vyākhyāti vidhinaktasya [k: vivi-] yaḥ kramam || 20/108 ||
sarvasaubhāgya saṃpannaḥ siddhikāṃtāpatirbhavet |
malliṃgārcana heturyastvayā pṛṣṭhaḥ kuleśvarī || 20/109 ||
samayā viśadaḥ proktaḥ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi |

nāma viṃśatitamaḥ [k: viṃśatiḥ] paṭalaḥ || ānaṃdataṃtraṃ saṃpūrṇam ||
